theorem CerbND.runNDFuel.eq_def :
∀ {a info err cs st : Type} (fuel : Nat) (m : ndM a info err cs st) (st0 : st),
  CerbND.runNDFuel fuel m st0 =
    match fuel with
    | 0 =>
      panicWithPosWithDecl "CerbND" "CerbND.runNDFuel" 95 4
        "CerbND.runNDFuel: ND-tree depth budget exhausted — this is FUEL EXHAUSTION, not an empty behavior set (see CerbND.lean header; budget: ndDefaultFuel)"
    | fuel.succ =>
      match m with
      | ND m_act =>
        match m_act st0 with
        | (NDactive result, st') => [(Active result, [], st')]
        | (NDkilled reason, st') => [(Killed st' reason, [], st')]
        | (NDnd _info branches, st') =>
          List.foldl
            (fun acc x =>
              match x with
              | (fst, branch) => CerbND.runNDFuel fuel branch st' ++ acc)
            [] branches
        | (NDguard _info _constraint continuation, st') => CerbND.runNDFuel fuel continuation st'
        | (NDbranch _info _constraint left right, st') =>
          CerbND.runNDFuel fuel left st' ++ CerbND.runNDFuel fuel right st'
        | (NDstep _info branches, st') =>
          List.foldl
            (fun acc x =>
              match x with
              | (fst, branch) => CerbND.runNDFuel fuel branch st' ++ acc)
            [] branches
----
def CerberusHeapLang.Audit.allowedAxioms :
List Name

def CerberusHeapLang.Audit.boundaryAxioms :
List Name

def CerberusHeapLang.Audit.boundaryModules :
List Name

inductive CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh :
CerberusHeapLang.Mem → Int → CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell → Prop
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh.alloc :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {id : Int} {c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell},
  CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c →
    ∃ al,
      σ.allocations.get? id = some al ∧
        al.base = c.addr ∧
          al.size = ↑(CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty) ∧
            al.ty = some c.ty ∧ al.isReadonly = CerbMem.ReadonlyStatus.IsWritable
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh.bytes :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {id : Int} {c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell},
  CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c →
    CerbMem.readBytesFrom σ c.addr (CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty) = c.bytes
----
def CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh.casesOn :
{σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} →
  {id : Int} →
    {c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell} →
      {motive : CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c → Sort u} →
        (t : CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c) →
          ((dead : σ.deadAllocations.contains id = false) →
              (alloc :
                  ∃ al,
                    σ.allocations.get? id = some al ∧
                      al.base = c.addr ∧
                        al.size = ↑(CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty) ∧
                          al.ty = some c.ty ∧ al.isReadonly = CerbMem.ReadonlyStatus.IsWritable) →
                (nonAtomic : CerberusHeapLang.atomicTy c.ty = false) →
                  (len : c.bytes.length = CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty) →
                    (bytes : CerbMem.readBytesFrom σ c.addr (CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty) = c.bytes) →
                      (dec_indep :
                          ∀ (lum : List (Int × identifier)) (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
                            CerbMem.reconstructValue lum fpm c.addr c.ty c.bytes =
                              CerberusHeapLang.decodeCell c) →
                        motive ⋯) →
            motive t
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh.dead :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {id : Int} {c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell},
  CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c → σ.deadAllocations.contains id = false
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh.dec_indep :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {id : Int} {c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell},
  CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c →
    ∀ (lum : List (Int × identifier)) (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
      CerbMem.reconstructValue lum fpm c.addr c.ty c.bytes = CerberusHeapLang.decodeCell c
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh.len :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {id : Int} {c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell},
  CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c → c.bytes.length = CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh.mk :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {id : Int} {c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell},
  σ.deadAllocations.contains id = false →
    (∃ al,
        σ.allocations.get? id = some al ∧
          al.base = c.addr ∧
            al.size = ↑(CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty) ∧
              al.ty = some c.ty ∧ al.isReadonly = CerbMem.ReadonlyStatus.IsWritable) →
      CerberusHeapLang.atomicTy c.ty = false →
        c.bytes.length = CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty →
          CerbMem.readBytesFrom σ c.addr (CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty) = c.bytes →
            (∀ (lum : List (Int × identifier)) (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
                CerbMem.reconstructValue lum fpm c.addr c.ty c.bytes =
                  CerberusHeapLang.decodeCell c) →
              CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh.nonAtomic :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {id : Int} {c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell},
  CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c → CerberusHeapLang.atomicTy c.ty = false
----
rec CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh.rec :
{σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} →
  {id : Int} →
    {c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell} →
      {motive : CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c → Sort u} →
        ((dead : σ.deadAllocations.contains id = false) →
            (alloc :
                ∃ al,
                  σ.allocations.get? id = some al ∧
                    al.base = c.addr ∧
                      al.size = ↑(CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty) ∧
                        al.ty = some c.ty ∧ al.isReadonly = CerbMem.ReadonlyStatus.IsWritable) →
              (nonAtomic : CerberusHeapLang.atomicTy c.ty = false) →
                (len : c.bytes.length = CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty) →
                  (bytes : CerbMem.readBytesFrom σ c.addr (CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty) = c.bytes) →
                    (dec_indep :
                        ∀ (lum : List (Int × identifier)) (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
                          CerbMem.reconstructValue lum fpm c.addr c.ty c.bytes =
                            CerberusHeapLang.decodeCell c) →
                      motive ⋯) →
          (t : CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c) → motive t
----
def CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh.recOn :
{σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} →
  {id : Int} →
    {c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell} →
      {motive : CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c → Sort u} →
        (t : CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c) →
          ((dead : σ.deadAllocations.contains id = false) →
              (alloc :
                  ∃ al,
                    σ.allocations.get? id = some al ∧
                      al.base = c.addr ∧
                        al.size = ↑(CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty) ∧
                          al.ty = some c.ty ∧ al.isReadonly = CerbMem.ReadonlyStatus.IsWritable) →
                (nonAtomic : CerberusHeapLang.atomicTy c.ty = false) →
                  (len : c.bytes.length = CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty) →
                    (bytes : CerbMem.readBytesFrom σ c.addr (CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty) = c.bytes) →
                      (dec_indep :
                          ∀ (lum : List (Int × identifier)) (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
                            CerbMem.reconstructValue lum fpm c.addr c.ty c.bytes =
                              CerberusHeapLang.decodeCell c) →
                        motive ⋯) →
            motive t

def CerberusHeapLang.CellMap :

inductive CerberusHeapLang.Coh :
CerberusHeapLang.Mem → CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell → Prop
----
def CerberusHeapLang.Coh.casesOn :
{σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} →
  {m : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell} →
    {motive : CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ m → Sort u} →
      (t : CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ m) →
        ((cells :
              ∀ (id : Int) (c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
                Iris.Std.get? m id = some c → CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c) →
            (disj :
                ∀ (id1 id2 : Int) (c1 c2 : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
                  id1 ≠ id2 →
                    Iris.Std.get? m id1 = some c1 →
                      Iris.Std.get? m id2 = some c2 → CerberusHeapLang.cellsDisjoint c1 c2) →
              motive ⋯) →
          motive t
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Coh.cells :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {m : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell},
  CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ m →
    ∀ (id : Int) (c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
      Iris.Std.get? m id = some c → CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Coh.disj :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {m : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell},
  CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ m →
    ∀ (id1 id2 : Int) (c1 c2 : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
      id1 ≠ id2 →
        Iris.Std.get? m id1 = some c1 →
          Iris.Std.get? m id2 = some c2 → CerberusHeapLang.cellsDisjoint c1 c2
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Coh.mk :
∀ {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {m : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell},
  (∀ (id : Int) (c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
      Iris.Std.get? m id = some c → CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c) →
    (∀ (id1 id2 : Int) (c1 c2 : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
        id1 ≠ id2 →
          Iris.Std.get? m id1 = some c1 →
            Iris.Std.get? m id2 = some c2 → CerberusHeapLang.cellsDisjoint c1 c2) →
      CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ m
----
rec CerberusHeapLang.Coh.rec :
{σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} →
  {m : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell} →
    {motive : CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ m → Sort u} →
      ((cells :
            ∀ (id : Int) (c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
              Iris.Std.get? m id = some c → CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c) →
          (disj :
              ∀ (id1 id2 : Int) (c1 c2 : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
                id1 ≠ id2 →
                  Iris.Std.get? m id1 = some c1 →
                    Iris.Std.get? m id2 = some c2 → CerberusHeapLang.cellsDisjoint c1 c2) →
            motive ⋯) →
        (t : CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ m) → motive t
----
def CerberusHeapLang.Coh.recOn :
{σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} →
  {m : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell} →
    {motive : CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ m → Sort u} →
      (t : CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ m) →
        ((cells :
              ∀ (id : Int) (c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
                Iris.Std.get? m id = some c → CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c) →
            (disj :
                ∀ (id1 id2 : Int) (c1 c2 : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
                  id1 ≠ id2 →
                    Iris.Std.get? m id1 = some c1 →
                      Iris.Std.get? m id2 = some c2 → CerberusHeapLang.cellsDisjoint c1 c2) →
              motive ⋯) →
          motive t
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Coh.store :
∀ (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (m : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell) (i : Int)
  (c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell) (mv : CerbMem.MemValue),
  CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ m →
    Iris.Std.get? m i = some c →
      CerberusHeapLang.StorableAt c.ty mv →
        CerberusHeapLang.Coh (CerbMem.writeBytesTo σ c.addr (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd)
          (Iris.Std.insert m i
            { addr := c.addr, ty := c.ty, bytes := (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd })

theorem CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.merge_merge :
∀ (ds ds' : List dyn_annotation) (v : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal),
  CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.merge ds (CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.merge ds' v) =
    CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.merge (ds ++ ds') v
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.merge_mk :
∀ (ds : List dyn_annotation) (w : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack)
  (Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap),
  CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.merge ds { w := w, ρ := ρ, lbl := Q } =
    { w := CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.merge ds w, ρ := ρ, lbl := Q }
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.mk :
CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal →
  CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap → CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.mk.inj :
∀ {w : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {lbl : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap}
  {w_1 : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal} {ρ_1 : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
  {lbl_1 : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap},
  { w := w, ρ := ρ, lbl := lbl } = { w := w_1, ρ := ρ_1, lbl := lbl_1 } →
    w = w_1 ∧ ρ = ρ_1 ∧ lbl = lbl_1
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.mk.injEq :
∀ (w : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (lbl : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap)
  (w_1 : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal) (ρ_1 : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack)
  (lbl_1 : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap),
  ({ w := w, ρ := ρ, lbl := lbl } = { w := w_1, ρ := ρ_1, lbl := lbl_1 }) =
    (w = w_1 ∧ ρ = ρ_1 ∧ lbl = lbl_1)
----
def CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.mk.noConfusion :
{P : Sort u} →
  {w : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal} →
    {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} →
      {lbl : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} →
        {w' : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal} →
          {ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} →
            {lbl' : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} →
              { w := w, ρ := ρ, lbl := lbl } = { w := w', ρ := ρ', lbl := lbl' } →
                (w = w' → ρ = ρ' → lbl = lbl' → P) → P
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.mk.sizeOf_spec :
∀ (w : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (lbl : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap),
  sizeOf { w := w, ρ := ρ, lbl := lbl } = 1 + sizeOf w + sizeOf ρ + sizeOf lbl
----
def CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.noConfusion :
{P : Sort u} →
  {t t' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal} → t = t' → CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.noConfusionType P t t'

      (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) →
        (lbl : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap) → motive { w := w, ρ := ρ, lbl := lbl }) →

inductive CerberusHeapLang.Decomp :
CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → context → CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → Prop
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.annot :
∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
  {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.annotRooted b = false →
    is_irreducible (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) = false →
      (∀ (n : Nat),
          get_ctx_lemFuel (n + 1) (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) =
            List.map (fun p => (Cannot [] ds p.fst, p.snd)) (get_ctx_lemFuel n b)) →
        CerberusHeapLang.Decomp b ctx r →
          CerberusHeapLang.Decomp (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) (Cannot [] ds ctx) r
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.apply_eq :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx r → apply_ctx ctx r = e
----
inductive CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below :
{motive :
    (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) →
      (a_1 : context) →
        (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_1 a_2 → Prop} →
  {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} →
    {a_1 : context} → {a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} → CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_1 a_2 → Prop
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below.annot :
∀
  {motive :
    (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) →
      (a_1 : context) →
        (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_1 a_2 → Prop}
  {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
  {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (hroot : CerberusHeapLang.annotRooted b = false)
  (hirr : is_irreducible (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) = false)
  (hmap :
    ∀ (n : Nat),
      get_ctx_lemFuel (n + 1) (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) =
        List.map (fun p => (Cannot [] ds p.fst, p.snd)) (get_ctx_lemFuel n b))
  (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp b ctx r),
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below a → motive b ctx r a → CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below ⋯
----
def CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below.casesOn :
∀
  {motive :
    (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) →
      (a_1 : context) →
        (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_1 a_2 → Prop}
  {motive_1 :
    {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} →
      {a_1 : context} →
        {a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} →
          (t : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_1 a_2) → CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below t → Prop}
  {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {a_1 : context} {a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
  {t : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_1 a_2} (t_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below t),
  (∀ {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Redex r), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
    (∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
        {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e1 ctx r)
        (ih : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below a) (a_ih : motive e1 ctx r a), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
      (∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
          {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (hroot : CerberusHeapLang.annotRooted b = false)
          (hirr : is_irreducible (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) = false)
          (hmap :
            ∀ (n : Nat),
              get_ctx_lemFuel (n + 1) (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) =
                List.map (fun p => (Cannot [] ds p.fst, p.snd)) (get_ctx_lemFuel n b))
          (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp b ctx r) (ih : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below a)
          (a_ih : motive b ctx r a), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
        motive_1 t t_1
----
rec CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below.rec :
∀
  {motive :
    (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) →
      (a_1 : context) →
        (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_1 a_2 → Prop}
  {motive_1 :
    {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} →
      {a_1 : context} →
        {a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} →
          (t : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_1 a_2) → CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below t → Prop},
  (∀ {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Redex r), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
    (∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
        {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e1 ctx r)
        (ih : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below a) (a_ih : motive e1 ctx r a),
        motive_1 a ih → motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
      (∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
          {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (hroot : CerberusHeapLang.annotRooted b = false)
          (hirr : is_irreducible (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) = false)
          (hmap :
            ∀ (n : Nat),
              get_ctx_lemFuel (n + 1) (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) =
                List.map (fun p => (Cannot [] ds p.fst, p.snd)) (get_ctx_lemFuel n b))
          (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp b ctx r) (ih : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below a)
          (a_ih : motive b ctx r a), motive_1 a ih → motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
        ∀ {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {a_1 : context} {a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
          {t : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_1 a_2} (t_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below t),
          motive_1 t t_1
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below.root :
∀
  {motive :
    (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) →
      (a_1 : context) →
        (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_1 a_2 → Prop}
  {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Redex r), CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below ⋯
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below.sseq :
∀
  {motive :
    (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) →
      (a_1 : context) →
        (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_1 a_2 → Prop}
  {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
  {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e1 ctx r),
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below a → motive e1 ctx r a → CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below ⋯
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.brecOn :
∀
  {motive :
    (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) →
      (a_1 : context) →
        (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_1 a_2 → Prop}
  {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {a_1 : context} {a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
  (t : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_1 a_2),
  (∀ (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (a_3 : context) (a_4 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr)
      (t : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_3 a_4),
      CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.below t → motive a a_3 a_4 t) →
    motive a a_1 a_2 t
----
def CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.casesOn :
∀
  {motive :
    (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) →
      (a_1 : context) →
        (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_1 a_2 → Prop}
  {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {a_1 : context} {a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
  (t : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_1 a_2),
  (∀ {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Redex r), motive r CTX r ⋯) →
    (∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
        {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e1 ctx r),
        motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) e1 e2))
          (Csseq [] (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) ctx e2) r ⋯) →
      (∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
          {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (hroot : CerberusHeapLang.annotRooted b = false)
          (hirr : is_irreducible (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) = false)
          (hmap :
            ∀ (n : Nat),
              get_ctx_lemFuel (n + 1) (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) =
                List.map (fun p => (Cannot [] ds p.fst, p.snd)) (get_ctx_lemFuel n b))
          (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp b ctx r),
          motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) (Cannot [] ds ctx) r ⋯) →
        motive a a_1 a_2 t
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.get_ctx_at :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx r →
    ∀ (n : Nat), CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ n → get_ctx_lemFuel n e = [(ctx, r)]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.get_ctx_default :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx r →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel → get_ctx e = [(ctx, r)]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.jumpRedex?_none :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx r → CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? e = none
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.not_irreducible :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx r → is_irreducible e = false
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.rebuild :
∀ {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
  {r r' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
  {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx r →
    CerberusHeapLang.Step Q (r, ρ, σ) (r', ρ', σ') →
      CerberusHeapLang.Step Q (e, ρ, σ) (apply_ctx ctx r', ρ', σ')
----
rec CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.rec :
∀
  {motive :
    (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) →
      (a_1 : context) →
        (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_1 a_2 → Prop},
  (∀ {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Redex r), motive r CTX r ⋯) →
    (∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
        {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e1 ctx r),
        motive e1 ctx r a →
          motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) e1 e2))
            (Csseq [] (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) ctx e2) r ⋯) →
      (∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
          {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (hroot : CerberusHeapLang.annotRooted b = false)
          (hirr : is_irreducible (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) = false)
          (hmap :
            ∀ (n : Nat),
              get_ctx_lemFuel (n + 1) (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) =
                List.map (fun p => (Cannot [] ds p.fst, p.snd)) (get_ctx_lemFuel n b))
          (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp b ctx r),
          motive b ctx r a → motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) (Cannot [] ds ctx) r ⋯) →
        ∀ {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {a_1 : context} {a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
          (t : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_1 a_2), motive a a_1 a_2 t
----
def CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.recOn :
∀
  {motive :
    (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) →
      (a_1 : context) →
        (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_1 a_2 → Prop}
  {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {a_1 : context} {a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
  (t : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp a a_1 a_2),
  (∀ {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Redex r), motive r CTX r ⋯) →
    (∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
        {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e1 ctx r),
        motive e1 ctx r a →
          motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) e1 e2))
            (Csseq [] (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) ctx e2) r ⋯) →
      (∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
          {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (hroot : CerberusHeapLang.annotRooted b = false)
          (hirr : is_irreducible (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) = false)
          (hmap :
            ∀ (n : Nat),
              get_ctx_lemFuel (n + 1) (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) =
                List.map (fun p => (Cannot [] ds p.fst, p.snd)) (get_ctx_lemFuel n b))
          (a : CerberusHeapLang.Decomp b ctx r),
          motive b ctx r a → motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) (Cannot [] ds ctx) r ⋯) →
        motive a a_1 a_2 t
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.redex :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx r → CerberusHeapLang.Redex r
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.root :
∀ {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}, CerberusHeapLang.Redex r → CerberusHeapLang.Decomp r CTX r
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.sseq :
∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
  {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e1 ctx r →
    CerberusHeapLang.Decomp (generic_expr.Expr [] (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) e1 e2))
      (Csseq [] (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) ctx e2) r
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.step_factor :
∀ {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
  {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  {out : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx r →
    CerberusHeapLang.Step Q (e, ρ, σ) out →
      ∃ r' ρ' σ', CerberusHeapLang.Step Q (r, ρ, σ) (r', ρ', σ') ∧ out = (apply_ctx ctx r', ρ', σ')
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.toJ :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx r → CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ e ctx r
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.toVal_none :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx r → CerberusHeapLang.toVal e = none
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.Decomp.unseq_ccall_false :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.Decomp e ctx r → is_unseq_with_ccall ctx = false
----
inductive CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ :
CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → context → CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → Prop
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ.annot :
∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
  {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.annotRooted b = false →
    is_irreducible (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) = false →
      (∀ (n : Nat),
          get_ctx_lemFuel (n + 1) (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) =
            List.map (fun p => (Cannot [] ds p.fst, p.snd)) (get_ctx_lemFuel n b)) →
        CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ b ctx r →
          CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) (Cannot [] ds ctx) r
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ.apply_eq :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ e ctx r → apply_ctx ctx r = e
----

  {motive :

def CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ.casesOn :
∀
  {motive :
    (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) →
      (a_1 : context) →
        (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ a a_1 a_2 → Prop}
  {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {a_1 : context} {a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
  (t : CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ a a_1 a_2),
  (∀ {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.RedexJ r), motive r CTX r ⋯) →
    (∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
        {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ e1 ctx r),
        motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) e1 e2))
          (Csseq [] (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) ctx e2) r ⋯) →
      (∀ {pa pb : List annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
          {ctx : context} {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ e1 ctx r),
          motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Esseq (CerberusHeapLang.specPat pa pb x bty) e1 e2))
            (Csseq [] (CerberusHeapLang.specPat pa pb x bty) ctx e2) r ⋯) →
        (∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
            {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (hroot : CerberusHeapLang.annotRooted b = false)
            (hirr : is_irreducible (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) = false)
            (hmap :
              ∀ (n : Nat),
                get_ctx_lemFuel (n + 1) (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) =
                  List.map (fun p => (Cannot [] ds p.fst, p.snd)) (get_ctx_lemFuel n b))
            (a : CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ b ctx r),
            motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) (Cannot [] ds ctx) r ⋯) →
          motive a a_1 a_2 t
----

rec CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ.rec :

  {motive :
    (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) →
      (a_1 : context) →
        (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ a a_1 a_2 → Prop},
  (∀ {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.RedexJ r), motive r CTX r ⋯) →
    (∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
        {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ e1 ctx r),
        motive e1 ctx r a →
          motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) e1 e2))
            (Csseq [] (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) ctx e2) r ⋯) →
      (∀ {pa pb : List annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
          {ctx : context} {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ e1 ctx r),
          motive e1 ctx r a →
            motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Esseq (CerberusHeapLang.specPat pa pb x bty) e1 e2))
              (Csseq [] (CerberusHeapLang.specPat pa pb x bty) ctx e2) r ⋯) →
        (∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
            {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (hroot : CerberusHeapLang.annotRooted b = false)
            (hirr : is_irreducible (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) = false)
            (hmap :
              ∀ (n : Nat),
                get_ctx_lemFuel (n + 1) (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) =
                  List.map (fun p => (Cannot [] ds p.fst, p.snd)) (get_ctx_lemFuel n b))
            (a : CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ b ctx r),
            motive b ctx r a → motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) (Cannot [] ds ctx) r ⋯) →
          ∀ {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {a_1 : context} {a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
            (t : CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ a a_1 a_2), motive a a_1 a_2 t

              (Csseq [] (CerberusHeapLang.specPat pa pb x bty) ctx e2) r ⋯) →
        (∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
            {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (hroot : CerberusHeapLang.annotRooted b = false)

theorem CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ.step_factor :
∀ {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context}
  {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  {out : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ e ctx r →
    CerberusHeapLang.Step Q (e, ρ, σ) out →
      (∃ r' ρ' σ',
          (∀ (ra : core_run_annotation) (l : sym) (pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym)),
              r ≠ CerberusHeapLang.runRedex ra l pes) ∧
            CerberusHeapLang.Step Q (r, ρ, σ) (r', ρ', σ') ∧ out = (apply_ctx ctx r', ρ', σ')) ∨
        ∃ ra l pes, r = CerberusHeapLang.runRedex ra l pes ∧ CerberusHeapLang.Step Q (r, ρ, σ) out
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ.unseq_ccall_false :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ e ctx r → is_unseq_with_ccall ctx = false

theorem CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done.inj :
∀ {v : value} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {v_1 : value} {σ_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ = CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v_1 σ_1 →
    v = v_1 ∧ σ = σ_1

theorem CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done.injEq :
∀ (v : value) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (v_1 : value) (σ_1 : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
  (CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ = CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v_1 σ_1) =
    (v = v_1 ∧ σ = σ_1)

def CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done.noConfusion :
{P : Sort u} →
  {v : value} →
    {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} →
      {v' : value} →
        {σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} →
          CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ = CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v' σ' →
            (v = v' → σ = σ' → P) → P
----

∀ (v : value) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
  sizeOf (CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ) = 1 + sizeOf v + sizeOf σ

inductive CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatch :
CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr →
  CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → CerberusHeapLang.Mem → CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome → Prop
----
def CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatch.casesOn :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome) → CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatch e ρ σ a → Prop}
  {a : CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome} (t : CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatch e ρ σ a),
  (∀ {e' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
      (a : CerberusHeapLang.Step CerberusHeapLang.spikeLbl (e, ρ, σ) (e', ρ', σ')),
      motive (CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next (CerberusHeapLang.envThread e' ρ') σ') ⋯) →
    (∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value}
        (a : e = CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v)),
        motive
          (CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next
            (CerberusHeapLang.envThread (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v))
              ρ)
            σ)
          ⋯) →
      (∀ {v : value} (a : e = CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v)),
          motive (CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.done v) ⋯) →
        (∀ {o : CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome} (a : o.isRefusal)
            (a_1 :
              ∀
                (out :
                  CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
                ¬CerberusHeapLang.Step CerberusHeapLang.spikeLbl (e, ρ, σ) out)
            (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.toVal e = none), motive o ⋯) →
          motive a t
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatch.done :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  {v : value},
  e = CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v) →
    CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatch e ρ σ (CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.done v)
----
rec CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatch.rec :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome) → CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatch e ρ σ a → Prop},
  (∀ {e' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
      (a : CerberusHeapLang.Step CerberusHeapLang.spikeLbl (e, ρ, σ) (e', ρ', σ')),
      motive (CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next (CerberusHeapLang.envThread e' ρ') σ') ⋯) →
    (∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value}
        (a : e = CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v)),
        motive
          (CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next
            (CerberusHeapLang.envThread (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v))
              ρ)
            σ)
          ⋯) →
      (∀ {v : value} (a : e = CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v)),
          motive (CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.done v) ⋯) →
        (∀ {o : CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome} (a : o.isRefusal)
            (a_1 :
              ∀
                (out :
                  CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
                ¬CerberusHeapLang.Step CerberusHeapLang.spikeLbl (e, ρ, σ) out)
            (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.toVal e = none), motive o ⋯) →
          ∀ {a : CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome} (t : CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatch e ρ σ a),
            motive a t
----
def CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatch.recOn :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome) → CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatch e ρ σ a → Prop}
  {a : CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome} (t : CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatch e ρ σ a),
  (∀ {e' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
      (a : CerberusHeapLang.Step CerberusHeapLang.spikeLbl (e, ρ, σ) (e', ρ', σ')),
      motive (CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next (CerberusHeapLang.envThread e' ρ') σ') ⋯) →
    (∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value}
        (a : e = CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v)),
        motive
          (CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next
            (CerberusHeapLang.envThread (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v))
              ρ)
            σ)
          ⋯) →
      (∀ {v : value} (a : e = CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v)),
          motive (CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.done v) ⋯) →
        (∀ {o : CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome} (a : o.isRefusal)
            (a_1 :
              ∀
                (out :
                  CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
                ¬CerberusHeapLang.Step CerberusHeapLang.spikeLbl (e, ρ, σ) out)
            (a_2 : CerberusHeapLang.toVal e = none), motive o ⋯) →
          motive a t
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatch.refused :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  {o : CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome},
  o.isRefusal →
    (∀ (out : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr × CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
        ¬CerberusHeapLang.Step CerberusHeapLang.spikeLbl (e, ρ, σ) out) →
      CerberusHeapLang.toVal e = none → CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatch e ρ σ o
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatch.removeAnnot :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value},
  e = CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v) →
    CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatch e ρ σ
      (CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next

∀ {a pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.EnvStable e1 →
    CerberusHeapLang.EnvStable e2 →
      CerberusHeapLang.EnvStable
        (generic_expr.Expr a (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) e1 e2))
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.EnvStable.step_env :
∀ {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
  {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {e' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
  {σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.EnvStable e →
    CerberusHeapLang.Step Q (e, ρ, σ) (e', ρ', σ') → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStable e' ∧ ρ' = ρ
----
def CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack :
Type
----
inductive CerberusHeapLang.FragJ :
CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → Prop

  CerberusHeapLang.FragJ b → CerberusHeapLang.FragJ (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b))

inductive CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.below :
{motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.FragJ a → Prop} →
  {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} → CerberusHeapLang.FragJ a → Prop
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.below.annot :
∀ {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.FragJ a → Prop}
  {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ b),
  CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.below a → motive b a → CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.below ⋯
----
def CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.below.casesOn :
∀ {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.FragJ a → Prop}
  {motive_1 :
    {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} →
      (t : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ a) → CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.below t → Prop}
  {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {t : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ a}
  (t_1 : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.below t),
  (∀ (v : value), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
    (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool} {ty : ctype}
        {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value} {mo : memory_order}
        (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
      (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype}
          {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {mo : memory_order}
          (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
        (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue}
            {ty : ctype} {pref : prefix0} (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),
            motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
          (∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
              (a : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e1) (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e2)
              (ih : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.below a) (a_ih : motive e1 a)
              (ih_1 : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.below a_1) (a_ih_1 : motive e2 a_1), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
            (∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                (a : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ b) (ih : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.below a)
                (a_ih : motive b a), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
              (∀ {sb : sym × core_base_type}
                  {ps :
                    List
                      (sym ×
                        (core_base_type × Option (ctype × pass_by_value_or_pointer)) ×
                          generic_pexpr Unit sym)}
                  {body : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ body)
                  (ih : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.below a) (a_ih : motive body a), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                (∀ {g : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {e2 e3 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                    (hdg : CerberusHeapLang.peDepth g ≤ lemDefaultFuel)
                    (a : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e2) (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e3)
                    (ih : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.below a) (a_ih : motive e2 a)
                    (ih_1 : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.below a_1) (a_ih_1 : motive e3 a_1),
                    motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                  (∀ {ra : core_run_annotation} {l : sym} {pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym)}
                      (hdep :
                        ∀ (pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym),
                          pe ∈ pes → CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe ≤ lemDefaultFuel),
                      motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                    (∀ {pa pb : List _root_.annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type}
                        {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e1)
                        (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e2) (ih : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.below a)
                        (a_ih : motive e1 a) (ih_1 : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.below a_1)
                        (a_ih_1 : motive e2 a_1), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                      (∀ {pb : List _root_.annot} {x : sym}, motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                        (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype}
                            {pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {mo : memory_order}
                            (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false)
                            (hnv2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = none) (hp2 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe2)
                            (hd2 : CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe2 ≤ lemDefaultFuel), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                          motive_1 t t_1
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.below.create :
∀ {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.FragJ a → Prop}
  {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue} {ty : ctype}
  {pref : prefix0} (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),
  CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.below ⋯

  {g : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {e2 e3 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}

rec CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.below.rec :
∀ {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.FragJ a → Prop}
  {motive_1 :
    {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} →
      (t : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ a) → CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.below t → Prop},
  (∀ (v : value), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
    (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool} {ty : ctype}
        {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value} {mo : memory_order}
        (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
      (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype}
          {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {mo : memory_order}
          (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
        (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue}
            {ty : ctype} {pref : prefix0} (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),
            motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
          (∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
              (a : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e1) (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e2)
              (ih : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.below a) (a_ih : motive e1 a)
              (ih_1 : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.below a_1) (a_ih_1 : motive e2 a_1),
              motive_1 a ih → motive_1 a_1 ih_1 → motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
            (∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                (a : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ b) (ih : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.below a)
                (a_ih : motive b a), motive_1 a ih → motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
              (∀ {sb : sym × core_base_type}
                  {ps :
                    List
                      (sym ×
                        (core_base_type × Option (ctype × pass_by_value_or_pointer)) ×
                          generic_pexpr Unit sym)}
                  {body : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ body)
                  (ih : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.below a) (a_ih : motive body a),
                  motive_1 a ih → motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                (∀ {g : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {e2 e3 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                    (hdg : CerberusHeapLang.peDepth g ≤ lemDefaultFuel)
                    (a : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e2) (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e3)
                    (ih : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.below a) (a_ih : motive e2 a)
                    (ih_1 : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.below a_1) (a_ih_1 : motive e3 a_1),
                    motive_1 a ih → motive_1 a_1 ih_1 → motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                  (∀ {ra : core_run_annotation} {l : sym} {pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym)}
                      (hdep :
                        ∀ (pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym),
                          pe ∈ pes → CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe ≤ lemDefaultFuel),
                      motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                    (∀ {pa pb : List _root_.annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type}
                        {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e1)
                        (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e2) (ih : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.below a)
                        (a_ih : motive e1 a) (ih_1 : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.below a_1)
                        (a_ih_1 : motive e2 a_1),
                        motive_1 a ih → motive_1 a_1 ih_1 → motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                      (∀ {pb : List _root_.annot} {x : sym}, motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                        (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype}
                            {pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {mo : memory_order}
                            (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false)
                            (hnv2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = none) (hp2 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe2)
                            (hd2 : CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe2 ≤ lemDefaultFuel), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
                          ∀ {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {t : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ a}
                            (t_1 : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.below t), motive_1 t t_1
----

∀ {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.FragJ a → Prop}
  {ra : core_run_annotation} {l : sym} {pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym)}
  (hdep : ∀ (pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym), pe ∈ pes → CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe ≤ lemDefaultFuel),
  CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.below ⋯

    List
      (sym × (core_base_type × Option (ctype × pass_by_value_or_pointer)) × generic_pexpr Unit sym)}

  CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.below a → motive body a → CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.below ⋯

theorem CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.brecOn :
∀ {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.FragJ a → Prop}
  {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (t : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ a),
  (∀ (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (t : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ a),
      CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.below t → motive a t) →
    motive a t
----
def CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.casesOn :
∀ {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.FragJ a → Prop}
  {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (t : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ a),
  (∀ (v : value), motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval v)))) ⋯) →
    (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool} {ty : ctype}
        {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value} {mo : memory_order}
        (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),
        motive (CerberusHeapLang.storeRedex loc ann lk ty pv cv mo) ⋯) →
      (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype}
          {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {mo : memory_order}
          (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),
          motive (CerberusHeapLang.loadRedex loc ann ty pv mo) ⋯) →
        (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue}
            {ty : ctype} {pref : prefix0} (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),
            motive (CerberusHeapLang.createRedex loc ann align ty pref) ⋯) →
          (∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
              (a : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e1) (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e2),
              motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) e1 e2)) ⋯) →
            (∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                (a : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ b), motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) ⋯) →
              (∀ {sb : sym × core_base_type}
                  {ps :
                    List
                      (sym ×
                        (core_base_type × Option (ctype × pass_by_value_or_pointer)) ×
                          generic_pexpr Unit sym)}
                  {body : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ body),
                  motive (CerberusHeapLang.saveRedex sb ps body) ⋯) →
                (∀ {g : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {e2 e3 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                    (hdg : CerberusHeapLang.peDepth g ≤ lemDefaultFuel)
                    (a : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e2) (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e3),
                    motive (CerberusHeapLang.ifRedex g e2 e3) ⋯) →
                  (∀ {ra : core_run_annotation} {l : sym} {pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym)}
                      (hdep :
                        ∀ (pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym),
                          pe ∈ pes → CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe ≤ lemDefaultFuel),
                      motive (CerberusHeapLang.runRedex ra l pes) ⋯) →
                    (∀ {pa pb : List _root_.annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type}
                        {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e1)
                        (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e2),
                        motive
                          (generic_expr.Expr []
                            (Esseq (CerberusHeapLang.specPat pa pb x bty) e1 e2))
                          ⋯) →
                      (∀ {pb : List _root_.annot} {x : sym},
                          motive (CerberusHeapLang.pureRedex (Pexpr pb () (PEsym x))) ⋯) →
                        (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype}
                            {pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {mo : memory_order}
                            (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false)
                            (hnv2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = none) (hp2 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe2)
                            (hd2 : CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe2 ≤ lemDefaultFuel),
                            motive (CerberusHeapLang.loadOpRedex loc ann ty pe2 mo) ⋯) →
                          motive a t
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.create :
∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue} {ty : ctype}
  {pref : prefix0},
  CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false →
    CerberusHeapLang.FragJ (CerberusHeapLang.createRedex loc ann align ty pref)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.decompJ :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e →
    CerberusHeapLang.toVal e = none →
      ∃ ctx r, CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ e ctx r ∧ CerberusHeapLang.FragJ r
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.esize_step_bound :
∀ {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
  {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {e' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack}
  {σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e →
    CerberusHeapLang.Step Q (e, ρ, σ) (e', ρ', σ') →
      CerberusHeapLang.esize e' ≤ CerberusHeapLang.esize e + 1 ∨
        ∃ l pes params cont,
          CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? e = some (l, pes) ∧
            CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel Q l = some (params, cont) ∧ e' = cont
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.if_ :
∀ {g : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {e2 e3 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.peDepth g ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
    CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e2 →
      CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e3 → CerberusHeapLang.FragJ (CerberusHeapLang.ifRedex g e2 e3)
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.load :
∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue}
  {mo : memory_order},
  CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false →
    CerberusHeapLang.FragJ (CerberusHeapLang.loadRedex loc ann ty pv mo)
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.load_op :
∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype} {pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym}
  {mo : memory_order},
  CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false →
    valueFromPexpr pe2 = none →
      CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe2 →
        CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe2 ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
          CerberusHeapLang.FragJ (CerberusHeapLang.loadOpRedex loc ann ty pe2 mo)
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.pure_sym :
∀ {pb : List annot} {x : sym},
  CerberusHeapLang.FragJ (CerberusHeapLang.pureRedex (Pexpr pb () (PEsym x)))
----
rec CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.rec :
∀ {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.FragJ a → Prop},
  (∀ (v : value), motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval v)))) ⋯) →
    (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool} {ty : ctype}
        {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value} {mo : memory_order}
        (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),
        motive (CerberusHeapLang.storeRedex loc ann lk ty pv cv mo) ⋯) →
      (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype}
          {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {mo : memory_order}
          (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),
          motive (CerberusHeapLang.loadRedex loc ann ty pv mo) ⋯) →
        (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue}
            {ty : ctype} {pref : prefix0} (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),
            motive (CerberusHeapLang.createRedex loc ann align ty pref) ⋯) →
          (∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
              (a : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e1) (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e2),
              motive e1 a →
                motive e2 a_1 →
                  motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) e1 e2))
                    ⋯) →
            (∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                (a : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ b),
                motive b a → motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) ⋯) →
              (∀ {sb : sym × core_base_type}
                  {ps :
                    List
                      (sym ×
                        (core_base_type × Option (ctype × pass_by_value_or_pointer)) ×
                          generic_pexpr Unit sym)}
                  {body : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ body),
                  motive body a → motive (CerberusHeapLang.saveRedex sb ps body) ⋯) →
                (∀ {g : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {e2 e3 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                    (hdg : CerberusHeapLang.peDepth g ≤ lemDefaultFuel)
                    (a : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e2) (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e3),
                    motive e2 a → motive e3 a_1 → motive (CerberusHeapLang.ifRedex g e2 e3) ⋯) →
                  (∀ {ra : core_run_annotation} {l : sym} {pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym)}
                      (hdep :
                        ∀ (pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym),
                          pe ∈ pes → CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe ≤ lemDefaultFuel),
                      motive (CerberusHeapLang.runRedex ra l pes) ⋯) →
                    (∀ {pa pb : List _root_.annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type}
                        {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e1)
                        (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e2),
                        motive e1 a →
                          motive e2 a_1 →
                            motive
                              (generic_expr.Expr []
                                (Esseq (CerberusHeapLang.specPat pa pb x bty) e1 e2))
                              ⋯) →
                      (∀ {pb : List _root_.annot} {x : sym},
                          motive (CerberusHeapLang.pureRedex (Pexpr pb () (PEsym x))) ⋯) →
                        (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype}
                            {pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {mo : memory_order}
                            (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false)
                            (hnv2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = none) (hp2 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe2)
                            (hd2 : CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe2 ≤ lemDefaultFuel),
                            motive (CerberusHeapLang.loadOpRedex loc ann ty pe2 mo) ⋯) →
                          ∀ {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (t : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ a),
                            motive a t
----
def CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.recOn :
∀ {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.FragJ a → Prop}
  {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (t : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ a),
  (∀ (v : value), motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval v)))) ⋯) →
    (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool} {ty : ctype}
        {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value} {mo : memory_order}
        (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),
        motive (CerberusHeapLang.storeRedex loc ann lk ty pv cv mo) ⋯) →
      (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype}
          {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {mo : memory_order}
          (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),
          motive (CerberusHeapLang.loadRedex loc ann ty pv mo) ⋯) →
        (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue}
            {ty : ctype} {pref : prefix0} (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),
            motive (CerberusHeapLang.createRedex loc ann align ty pref) ⋯) →
          (∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
              (a : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e1) (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e2),
              motive e1 a →
                motive e2 a_1 →
                  motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) e1 e2))
                    ⋯) →
            (∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                (a : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ b),
                motive b a → motive (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds b)) ⋯) →
              (∀ {sb : sym × core_base_type}
                  {ps :
                    List
                      (sym ×
                        (core_base_type × Option (ctype × pass_by_value_or_pointer)) ×
                          generic_pexpr Unit sym)}
                  {body : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ body),
                  motive body a → motive (CerberusHeapLang.saveRedex sb ps body) ⋯) →
                (∀ {g : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {e2 e3 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                    (hdg : CerberusHeapLang.peDepth g ≤ lemDefaultFuel)
                    (a : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e2) (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e3),
                    motive e2 a → motive e3 a_1 → motive (CerberusHeapLang.ifRedex g e2 e3) ⋯) →
                  (∀ {ra : core_run_annotation} {l : sym} {pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym)}
                      (hdep :
                        ∀ (pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym),
                          pe ∈ pes → CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe ≤ lemDefaultFuel),
                      motive (CerberusHeapLang.runRedex ra l pes) ⋯) →
                    (∀ {pa pb : List _root_.annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type}
                        {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e1)
                        (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e2),
                        motive e1 a →
                          motive e2 a_1 →
                            motive
                              (generic_expr.Expr []
                                (Esseq (CerberusHeapLang.specPat pa pb x bty) e1 e2))
                              ⋯) →
                      (∀ {pb : List _root_.annot} {x : sym},
                          motive (CerberusHeapLang.pureRedex (Pexpr pb () (PEsym x))) ⋯) →
                        (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype}
                            {pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {mo : memory_order}
                            (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false)
                            (hnv2 : valueFromPexpr pe2 = none) (hp2 : CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe2)
                            (hd2 : CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe2 ≤ lemDefaultFuel),
                            motive (CerberusHeapLang.loadOpRedex loc ann ty pe2 mo) ⋯) →
                          motive a t
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.run :
∀ {ra : core_run_annotation} {l : sym} {pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym)},
  (∀ (pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym), pe ∈ pes → CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe ≤ lemDefaultFuel) →
    CerberusHeapLang.FragJ (CerberusHeapLang.runRedex ra l pes)
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.save :
∀ {sb : sym × core_base_type}
  {ps :
    List
      (sym × (core_base_type × Option (ctype × pass_by_value_or_pointer)) × generic_pexpr Unit sym)}
  {body : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.FragJ body → CerberusHeapLang.FragJ (CerberusHeapLang.saveRedex sb ps body)
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.sseq :
∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e1 →
    CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e2 →
      CerberusHeapLang.FragJ
        (generic_expr.Expr [] (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) e1 e2))
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.sseq_spec :
∀ {pa pb : List annot} {x : sym} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e1 →
    CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e2 →
      CerberusHeapLang.FragJ
        (generic_expr.Expr [] (Esseq (CerberusHeapLang.specPat pa pb x bty) e1 e2))
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.step :
∀ {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap},
  (∀ (l : sym) (params : List (sym × core_base_type)) (cont : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
      CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel Q l = some (params, cont) → CerberusHeapLang.FragJ cont) →
    ∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
      {e' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
      CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e →
        CerberusHeapLang.Step Q (e, ρ, σ) (e', ρ', σ') → CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e'
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.store :
∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool} {ty : ctype}
  {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value} {mo : memory_order},
  CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false →
    CerberusHeapLang.FragJ (CerberusHeapLang.storeRedex loc ann lk ty pv cv mo)
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.FragJ.val_pure :
∀ (v : value), CerberusHeapLang.FragJ (generic_expr.Expr [] (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval v))))
----

inductive CerberusHeapLang.FragP.below :
{motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.FragP a → Prop} →
  {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} → CerberusHeapLang.FragP a → Prop
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.FragP.below.annot :
∀ {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.FragP a → Prop}
  {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.FragP b),
  CerberusHeapLang.FragP.below a → motive b a → CerberusHeapLang.FragP.below ⋯
----
def CerberusHeapLang.FragP.below.casesOn :
∀ {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.FragP a → Prop}
  {motive_1 :
    {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} →
      (t : CerberusHeapLang.FragP a) → CerberusHeapLang.FragP.below t → Prop}
  {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {t : CerberusHeapLang.FragP a}
  (t_1 : CerberusHeapLang.FragP.below t),
  (∀ (v : value), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
    (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool} {ty : ctype}
        {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value} {mo : memory_order}
        (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
      (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype}
          {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {mo : memory_order}
          (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
        (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue}
            {ty : ctype} {pref : prefix0} (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),
            motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
          (∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
              (a : CerberusHeapLang.FragP e1) (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.FragP e2)
              (ih : CerberusHeapLang.FragP.below a) (a_ih : motive e1 a)
              (ih_1 : CerberusHeapLang.FragP.below a_1) (a_ih_1 : motive e2 a_1), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
            (∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                (a : CerberusHeapLang.FragP b) (ih : CerberusHeapLang.FragP.below a)
                (a_ih : motive b a), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
              motive_1 t t_1
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.FragP.below.create :
∀ {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.FragP a → Prop}
  {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue} {ty : ctype}
  {pref : prefix0} (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),
  CerberusHeapLang.FragP.below ⋯

∀ {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.FragP a → Prop}
  {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue}
  {mo : memory_order} (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),
  CerberusHeapLang.FragP.below ⋯
----
rec CerberusHeapLang.FragP.below.rec :
∀ {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.FragP a → Prop}
  {motive_1 :
    {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} →
      (t : CerberusHeapLang.FragP a) → CerberusHeapLang.FragP.below t → Prop},
  (∀ (v : value), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
    (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool} {ty : ctype}
        {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value} {mo : memory_order}
        (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
      (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype}
          {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {mo : memory_order}
          (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false), motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
        (∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue}
            {ty : ctype} {pref : prefix0} (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false),
            motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
          (∀ {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
              (a : CerberusHeapLang.FragP e1) (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.FragP e2)
              (ih : CerberusHeapLang.FragP.below a) (a_ih : motive e1 a)
              (ih_1 : CerberusHeapLang.FragP.below a_1) (a_ih_1 : motive e2 a_1),
              motive_1 a ih → motive_1 a_1 ih_1 → motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
            (∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
                (a : CerberusHeapLang.FragP b) (ih : CerberusHeapLang.FragP.below a)
                (a_ih : motive b a), motive_1 a ih → motive_1 ⋯ ⋯) →
              ∀ {a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {t : CerberusHeapLang.FragP a}
                (t_1 : CerberusHeapLang.FragP.below t), motive_1 t t_1
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.FragP.below.sseq :
∀ {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.FragP a → Prop} {pa : List annot}
  {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : CerberusHeapLang.FragP e1)
  (a_1 : CerberusHeapLang.FragP e2),
  CerberusHeapLang.FragP.below a →
    motive e1 a → CerberusHeapLang.FragP.below a_1 → motive e2 a_1 → CerberusHeapLang.FragP.below ⋯
----

∀ {motive : (a : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) → CerberusHeapLang.FragP a → Prop}
  {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool} {ty : ctype}
  {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value} {mo : memory_order}
  (hlib : CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false), CerberusHeapLang.FragP.below ⋯

  CerberusHeapLang.FragP.below ⋯
