def CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.blockSpecs :
{hlc : Iris.HasLC} →
  {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
    [CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.ProbeGS hlc GF] →
      CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TFn →
        (Nat → Int → Iris.IProp GF) →
          (Int → CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv → Iris.IProp GF) → Iris.IProp GF
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.blockSpecs_intro :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.ProbeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TFn} {Ls : Nat → Int → Iris.IProp GF}
  {Ψ : Int → CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv → Iris.IProp GF},
  (∀ (l : Nat) (px : Option Nat) (k : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr) (z : Int)
      (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv),
      Q l = some (px, k) →
        Ls l z ⊢
          CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.wps Q Ls Ψ k (CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.bindPat px z ρ)) →
    ⊢ CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.blockSpecs Q Ls Ψ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.blockSpecs_wpsBlock :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.ProbeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TFn} {Ls : Nat → Int → Iris.IProp GF}
  {Ψ : Int → CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv → Iris.IProp GF} (l : Nat),
  CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.blockSpecs Q Ls Ψ ⊢ CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.wpsBlock Q Ls Ψ l
----
def CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.cAddr :
Int
----
def CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.demoFn :
CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TFn
----
def CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.demoLs :
{hlc : Iris.HasLC} →
  {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
    [CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.ProbeGS hlc GF] → Int → Int → Nat → Int → Iris.IProp GF
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.demo_block :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.ProbeGS hlc GF]
  (v0 n x : Int) (ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv),
  CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.demoLs v0 n CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.loopLbl x ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.wps CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.demoFn
      (CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.demoLs v0 n) (CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.demoΨ v0 n)
      CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.loopBody
      (CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.bindPat (some CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.xK) x ρ')
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.demo_blocks :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.ProbeGS hlc GF]
  (v0 n : Int),
  ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.blockSpecs CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.demoFn
      (CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.demoLs v0 n) (CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.demoΨ v0 n)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.demo_loop :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.ProbeGS hlc GF]
  (v0 n : Int),
  0 ≤ n →
    ∀ (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv),
      CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.cAddr ↦{Iris.DFrac.own 1} v0 ⊢
        WP
          { fn := CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.demoFn,
            e :=
              CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr.run CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.loopLbl
                (CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TOp.lit n),
            ρ := ρ } @
          Iris.Stuckness.NotStuck ;
          ⊤ {{ w, ⌜w.z = v0 + n⌝ ∗ CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.cAddr ↦{Iris.DFrac.own 1} v0 + n }}
----
def CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.demoΨ :
{hlc : Iris.HasLC} →
  {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
    [CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.ProbeGS hlc GF] →
      Int → Int → Int → CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv → Iris.IProp GF

theorem CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.fill_cons :
∀ (px : Option Nat) (e2 : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr)
  (K : List CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TFrame) (e : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr),
  CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.fill ((px, e2) :: K) e =
    CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr.seq px (CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.fill K e) e2

theorem CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.fill_eq_of_ne_seq :
∀ {K : List CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TFrame} {e e0 : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr},
  (∀ (px : Option Nat) (a b : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr),
      e0 ≠ CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr.seq px a b) →
    e0 = CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.fill K e → K = [] ∧ e = e0
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.fill_eq_val :
∀ {K : List CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TFrame} {e : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr} {z : Int},
  CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr.val z = CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.fill K e →
    K = [] ∧ e = CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr.val z

def CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.jumpRedex? :
CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr → Option (Nat × CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TOp)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.jumpRedex?.eq_def :
∀ (x : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr),
  CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.jumpRedex? x =
    match x with
    | CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr.run l a => some (l, a)
    | CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr.seq px e1 e2 => CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.jumpRedex? e1
    | x => none
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.jumpRedex?_fill :
∀ (K : List CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TFrame) (e : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr),
  CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.jumpRedex? (CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.fill K e) =
    CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.jumpRedex? e
----

∀ (g : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TOp) (e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr),
  CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.jumpRedex? (CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr.ifz g e1 e2) = none
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.jumpRedex?_load :
∀ (ℓ : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TOp),
  CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.jumpRedex? (CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr.load ℓ) = none
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.jumpRedex?_run :
∀ (l : Nat) (a : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TOp),
  CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.jumpRedex? (CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr.run l a) = some (l, a)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.jumpRedex?_seq :
∀ (px : Option Nat) (e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr),
  CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.jumpRedex? (CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr.seq px e1 e2) =
    CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.jumpRedex? e1
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.jumpRedex?_some_fill :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr} {l : Nat} {a : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TOp},
  CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.jumpRedex? e = some (l, a) →
    ∃ K, e = CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.fill K (CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr.run l a)
----

∀ (ℓ v : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TOp),
  CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.jumpRedex? (CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr.store ℓ v) = none

theorem CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.probeStateInterp_eq :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.ProbeGS hlc GF]
  (σ : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.THeap) (ns : Nat) (κs : List Empty) (nt : Nat),
  Iris.stateInterp σ ns κs nt = Iris.genHeapInterp σ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.probe_primStep_eq :
∀ (r : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TRt) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.THeap) (obs : List Empty)
  (r' : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TRt) (σ' : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.THeap)
  (eₜ : List CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TRt),
  Iris.ProgramLogic.PrimStep.primStep (r, σ) obs (r', σ', eₜ) ↔
    CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.Step r.fn (r.e, r.ρ, σ) (r'.e, r'.ρ, σ') ∧ r'.fn = r.fn ∧ eₜ = []
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.probe_seq_stores :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.ProbeGS hlc GF]
  (Q : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TFn) (Ls : Nat → Int → Iris.IProp GF) (x y vx vy : Int)
  (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv),
  x ↦{Iris.DFrac.own 1} vx ∗ y ↦{Iris.DFrac.own 1} vy ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.wps Q Ls
      (fun x_1 x_2 => iprop(x ↦{Iris.DFrac.own 1} 5 ∗ y ↦{Iris.DFrac.own 1} 6))
      (CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr.seq none
        (CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr.store (CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TOp.lit x)
          (CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TOp.lit 5))
        (CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr.store (CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TOp.lit y)
          (CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TOp.lit 6)))
      ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.probe_store_frame :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.ProbeGS hlc GF]
  (Q : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TFn) (Ls : Nat → Int → Iris.IProp GF) (x y vx vy : Int)
  (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv),
  x ↦{Iris.DFrac.own 1} vx ∗ y ↦{Iris.DFrac.own 1} vy ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.wps Q Ls
      (fun x_1 x_2 => iprop(x ↦{Iris.DFrac.own 1} 7 ∗ y ↦{Iris.DFrac.own 1} vy))
      (CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr.store (CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TOp.lit x)
        (CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TOp.lit 7))
      ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.probe_toVal_eq :
∀ (r : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TRt),
  Iris.ProgramLogic.toVal r = CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.toValRt r
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.step_ifz_inv :
∀ {fn : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TFn} {g : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TOp}
  {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv}
  {σ : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.THeap}
  {out :
    CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr ×
      CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv × CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.THeap},
  CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.Step fn (CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr.ifz g e1 e2, ρ, σ) out →
    ∃ z,
      CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.evalOp ρ g = some z ∧
        (z = 0 ∧ out = (e1, ρ, σ) ∨ z ≠ 0 ∧ out = (e2, ρ, σ))
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.step_jump_inv :
∀ {fn : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TFn} {e : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr}
  {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.THeap}
  {out :
    CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr ×
      CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv × CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.THeap}
  {l : Nat} {a : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TOp},
  CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.jumpRedex? e = some (l, a) →
    CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.Step fn (e, ρ, σ) out →
      ∃ px k z,
        fn l = some (px, k) ∧
          CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.evalOp ρ a = some z ∧
            out = (k, CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.bindPat px z ρ, σ)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.step_load_inv :
∀ {fn : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TFn} {ℓop : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TOp}
  {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.THeap}
  {out :
    CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr ×
      CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv × CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.THeap},
  CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.Step fn (CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr.load ℓop, ρ, σ) out →
    ∃ a v,
      CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.evalOp ρ ℓop = some a ∧
        Iris.Std.get? σ a = some v ∧ out = (CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr.val v, ρ, σ)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.step_of_jumpRedex :
∀ {fn : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TFn} {e : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr}
  {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.THeap} {l : Nat}
  {a : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TOp} {px : Option Nat} {k : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr}
  {z : Int},
  CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.jumpRedex? e = some (l, a) →
    fn l = some (px, k) →
      CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.evalOp ρ a = some z →
        CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.Step fn (e, ρ, σ)
          (k, CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.bindPat px z ρ, σ)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.step_seq_factor :
∀ {fn : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TFn} {px : Option Nat} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr}
  {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.THeap}
  {out :
    CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr ×
      CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv × CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.THeap},
  CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.Step fn (CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr.seq px e1 e2, ρ, σ) out →
    (∃ z,
        e1 = CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr.val z ∧
          out = (e2, CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.bindPat px z ρ, σ)) ∨
      (∃ e1' ρ' σ',
          CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.Step fn (e1, ρ, σ) (e1', ρ', σ') ∧
            CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.jumpRedex? e1 = none ∧
              out = (CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr.seq px e1' e2, ρ', σ')) ∨
        ∃ l a px' k z,
          CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.jumpRedex? e1 = some (l, a) ∧
            fn l = some (px', k) ∧
              CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.evalOp ρ a = some z ∧
                out = (k, CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.bindPat px' z ρ, σ)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.step_store_inv :
∀ {fn : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TFn} {ℓop vop : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TOp}
  {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.THeap}
  {out :
    CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr ×
      CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv × CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.THeap},
  CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.Step fn (CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr.store ℓop vop, ρ, σ) out →
    ∃ a v v0,
      CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.evalOp ρ ℓop = some a ∧
        CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.evalOp ρ vop = some v ∧
          Iris.Std.get? σ a = some v0 ∧
            out = (CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr.val v, ρ, Iris.Std.insert σ a v)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.step_val_elim :
∀ {fn : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TFn} {z : Int} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv}
  {σ : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.THeap}
  {out :
    CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr ×
      CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv × CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.THeap},
  CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.Step fn (CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr.val z, ρ, σ) out → False
----

def CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.wps :
{hlc : Iris.HasLC} →
  {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
    [CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.ProbeGS hlc GF] →
      CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TFn →
        (Nat → Int → Iris.IProp GF) →
          (Int → CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv → Iris.IProp GF) →
            CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr → CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv → Iris.IProp GF
----

{hlc : Iris.HasLC} →
  {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
    [CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.ProbeGS hlc GF] →
      CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TFn →
        (Nat → Int → Iris.IProp GF) →
          ((Int → CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv → Iris.IProp GF) →
              CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr → CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv → Iris.IProp GF) →
            (Int → CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv → Iris.IProp GF) →
              CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr → CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv → Iris.IProp GF
----

∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.ProbeGS hlc GF]
  (Q : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TFn) (Ls : Nat → Int → Iris.IProp GF),
  Iris.OFE.Contractive (CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.wps.pre Q Ls)
----
def CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.wpsBlock :
{hlc : Iris.HasLC} →
  {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
    [CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.ProbeGS hlc GF] →
      CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TFn →
        (Nat → Int → Iris.IProp GF) →
          (Int → CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv → Iris.IProp GF) → Nat → Iris.IProp GF
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.wps_frame :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.ProbeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TFn} {Ls : Nat → Int → Iris.IProp GF}
  {Ψ : Int → CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv → Iris.IProp GF} {R : Iris.IProp GF}
  (e : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv),
  CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.wps Q Ls Ψ e ρ ∗ R ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.wps Q Ls (fun z ρ' => iprop(Ψ z ρ' ∗ R)) e ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.wps_ifz_nonzero :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.ProbeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TFn} {Ls : Nat → Int → Iris.IProp GF}
  {Ψ : Int → CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv → Iris.IProp GF} {g : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TOp}
  {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr} {z : Int} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv},
  CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.evalOp ρ g = some z →
    z ≠ 0 →
      CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.wps Q Ls Ψ e2 ρ ⊢
        CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.wps Q Ls Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr.ifz g e1 e2) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.wps_ifz_zero :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.ProbeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TFn} {Ls : Nat → Int → Iris.IProp GF}
  {Ψ : Int → CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv → Iris.IProp GF} {g : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TOp}
  {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv},
  CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.evalOp ρ g = some 0 →
    CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.wps Q Ls Ψ e1 ρ ⊢
      CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.wps Q Ls Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr.ifz g e1 e2) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.wps_load :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.ProbeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TFn} {Ls : Nat → Int → Iris.IProp GF}
  {Ψ : Int → CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv → Iris.IProp GF} {ℓop : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TOp}
  {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv} {av v : Int} {dq : Iris.DFrac},
  CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.evalOp ρ ℓop = some av →
    av ↦{dq} v ∗ (av ↦{dq} v -∗ Ψ v ρ) ⊢
      CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.wps Q Ls Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr.load ℓop) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.wps_run :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.ProbeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TFn} {Ls : Nat → Int → Iris.IProp GF}
  {Ψ : Int → CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv → Iris.IProp GF} {l : Nat}
  {a : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TOp} {px : Option Nat} {k : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr}
  {z : Int} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv},
  Q l = some (px, k) →
    CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.evalOp ρ a = some z →
      Ls l z ⊢ CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.wps Q Ls Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr.run l a) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.wps_seq :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.ProbeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TFn} {Ls : Nat → Int → Iris.IProp GF}
  {Ψ : Int → CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv → Iris.IProp GF} (px : Option Nat)
  (e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv),
  CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.wps Q Ls
      (fun z ρ' =>
        CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.wps Q Ls Ψ e2 (CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.bindPat px z ρ'))
      e1 ρ ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.wps Q Ls Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr.seq px e1 e2) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.wps_sound :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.ProbeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TFn} {Ls : Nat → Int → Iris.IProp GF}
  {Ψ : Int → CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv → Iris.IProp GF} (e : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr)
  (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv),
  CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.blockSpecs Q Ls Ψ ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.wps Q Ls Ψ e ρ -∗
      WP { fn := Q, e := e, ρ := ρ } @ Iris.Stuckness.NotStuck ; ⊤ {{ w, Ψ w.z w.ρ }}
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.wps_store :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.ProbeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TFn} {Ls : Nat → Int → Iris.IProp GF}
  {Ψ : Int → CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv → Iris.IProp GF}
  {ℓop vop : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TOp} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv} {av v v0 : Int},
  CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.evalOp ρ ℓop = some av →
    CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.evalOp ρ vop = some v →
      av ↦{Iris.DFrac.own 1} v0 ∗ (av ↦{Iris.DFrac.own 1} v -∗ Ψ v ρ) ⊢
        CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.wps Q Ls Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr.store ℓop vop) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.wps_unfold :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.ProbeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TFn} {Ls : Nat → Int → Iris.IProp GF}
  {Ψ : Int → CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv → Iris.IProp GF} {e : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr}
  {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv},
  CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.wps Q Ls Ψ e ρ ⊣⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.wps.pre Q Ls (CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.wps Q Ls) Ψ e ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.wps_val :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.ProbeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TFn} {Ls : Nat → Int → Iris.IProp GF}
  {Ψ : Int → CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv → Iris.IProp GF} (z : Int)
  (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv),
  Ψ z ρ ⊢ CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.wps Q Ls Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr.val z) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.wps_wand :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.ProbeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TFn} {Ls : Nat → Int → Iris.IProp GF}
  {Ψ₁ Ψ₂ : Int → CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv → Iris.IProp GF}
  (e : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TExpr) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.TEnv),
  CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.wps Q Ls Ψ₁ e ρ ⊢
    (∀ z ρ', Ψ₁ z ρ' -∗ Ψ₂ z ρ') -∗ CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.wps Q Ls Ψ₂ e ρ
----
def CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.xK :
Nat

def CerberusHeapLang.StmtProbe.yK :
Nat

inductive CerberusHeapLang.StorableAt :
ctype → CerbMem.MemValue → Prop
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.StorableAt.bytes_fpm :
∀ {ty : ctype} {mv : CerbMem.MemValue},
  CerberusHeapLang.StorableAt ty mv →
    ∀ (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
      (CerbMem.memValueToBytes fpm mv).snd = (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd
----
def CerberusHeapLang.StorableAt.casesOn :
{ty : ctype} →
  {mv : CerbMem.MemValue} →
    {motive : CerberusHeapLang.StorableAt ty mv → Sort u} →
      (t : CerberusHeapLang.StorableAt ty mv) →
        ((compat : CerbMem.ctypeMemCompatible ty (CerbMem.typeofMval mv) = true) →
            (fpm : ∀ (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap), (CerbMem.memValueToBytes fpm mv).fst = fpm) →
              (len :
                  ∀ (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
                    (CerbMem.memValueToBytes fpm mv).snd.length = CerbMem.sizeofCtype ty) →
                (bytes_fpm :
                    ∀ (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
                      (CerbMem.memValueToBytes fpm mv).snd = (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd) →
                  (stored_dec :
                      ∀ (lum : List (Int × identifier)) (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap) (addr : Int),
                        CerbMem.reconstructValue lum fpm addr ty
                            (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd =
                          CerbMem.reconstructValue [] [] addr ty
                            (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd) →
                    motive ⋯) →
          motive t
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.StorableAt.compat :
∀ {ty : ctype} {mv : CerbMem.MemValue},
  CerberusHeapLang.StorableAt ty mv → CerbMem.ctypeMemCompatible ty (CerbMem.typeofMval mv) = true
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.StorableAt.fpm :
∀ {ty : ctype} {mv : CerbMem.MemValue},
  CerberusHeapLang.StorableAt ty mv →
    ∀ (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap), (CerbMem.memValueToBytes fpm mv).fst = fpm
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.StorableAt.len :
∀ {ty : ctype} {mv : CerbMem.MemValue},
  CerberusHeapLang.StorableAt ty mv →
    ∀ (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
      (CerbMem.memValueToBytes fpm mv).snd.length = CerbMem.sizeofCtype ty
----
ctor CerberusHeapLang.StorableAt.mk :
∀ {ty : ctype} {mv : CerbMem.MemValue},
  CerbMem.ctypeMemCompatible ty (CerbMem.typeofMval mv) = true →
    (∀ (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap), (CerbMem.memValueToBytes fpm mv).fst = fpm) →
      (∀ (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
          (CerbMem.memValueToBytes fpm mv).snd.length = CerbMem.sizeofCtype ty) →
        (∀ (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
            (CerbMem.memValueToBytes fpm mv).snd = (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd) →
          (∀ (lum : List (Int × identifier)) (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap) (addr : Int),
              CerbMem.reconstructValue lum fpm addr ty (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd =
                CerbMem.reconstructValue [] [] addr ty (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd) →
            CerberusHeapLang.StorableAt ty mv
----
rec CerberusHeapLang.StorableAt.rec :
{ty : ctype} →
  {mv : CerbMem.MemValue} →
    {motive : CerberusHeapLang.StorableAt ty mv → Sort u} →
      ((compat : CerbMem.ctypeMemCompatible ty (CerbMem.typeofMval mv) = true) →
          (fpm : ∀ (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap), (CerbMem.memValueToBytes fpm mv).fst = fpm) →
            (len :
                ∀ (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
                  (CerbMem.memValueToBytes fpm mv).snd.length = CerbMem.sizeofCtype ty) →
              (bytes_fpm :
                  ∀ (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
                    (CerbMem.memValueToBytes fpm mv).snd = (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd) →
                (stored_dec :
                    ∀ (lum : List (Int × identifier)) (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap) (addr : Int),
                      CerbMem.reconstructValue lum fpm addr ty (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd =
                        CerbMem.reconstructValue [] [] addr ty
                          (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd) →
                  motive ⋯) →
        (t : CerberusHeapLang.StorableAt ty mv) → motive t
----
def CerberusHeapLang.StorableAt.recOn :
{ty : ctype} →
  {mv : CerbMem.MemValue} →
    {motive : CerberusHeapLang.StorableAt ty mv → Sort u} →
      (t : CerberusHeapLang.StorableAt ty mv) →
        ((compat : CerbMem.ctypeMemCompatible ty (CerbMem.typeofMval mv) = true) →
            (fpm : ∀ (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap), (CerbMem.memValueToBytes fpm mv).fst = fpm) →
              (len :
                  ∀ (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
                    (CerbMem.memValueToBytes fpm mv).snd.length = CerbMem.sizeofCtype ty) →
                (bytes_fpm :
                    ∀ (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
                      (CerbMem.memValueToBytes fpm mv).snd = (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd) →
                  (stored_dec :
                      ∀ (lum : List (Int × identifier)) (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap) (addr : Int),
                        CerbMem.reconstructValue lum fpm addr ty
                            (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd =
                          CerbMem.reconstructValue [] [] addr ty
                            (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd) →
                    motive ⋯) →
          motive t
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.StorableAt.stored_dec :
∀ {ty : ctype} {mv : CerbMem.MemValue},
  CerberusHeapLang.StorableAt ty mv →
    ∀ (lum : List (Int × identifier)) (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap) (addr : Int),
      CerbMem.reconstructValue lum fpm addr ty (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd =
        CerbMem.reconstructValue [] [] addr ty (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd

theorem CerberusHeapLang.annot_merge_det :
∀ {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {a1 a2 : List annot} {ds1 ds2 : List dyn_annotation}
  {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  {obs : List Empty} {r' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt} {σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  {eₜ : List CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt},
  Iris.ProgramLogic.PrimStep.primStep
      ({ e := generic_expr.Expr a1 (Eannot ds1 (generic_expr.Expr a2 (Eannot ds2 b))), ρ := ρ,
          lbl := Q },
        σ)
      obs (r', σ', eₜ) →
    obs = [] ∧
      σ' = σ ∧
        r' = { e := generic_expr.Expr (a1 ++ a2) (Eannot (ds1 ++ ds2) b), ρ := ρ, lbl := Q } ∧
          eₜ = []
----

theorem CerberusHeapLang.arrQ_inv :
∀ (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann ra : core_run_annotation) (mo : memory_order)
  (ibty accbty pbty xbty : core_base_type) (n : Int) {l : sym}
  {params : List (sym × core_base_type)} {cont : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel (CerberusHeapLang.arrQ loc ann ra mo ibty accbty pbty xbty n) l =
      some (params, cont) →
    params =
        [(CerberusHeapLang.arrISym, ibty), (CerberusHeapLang.arrAccSym, accbty),
          (CerberusHeapLang.arrPSym, pbty)] ∧
      cont = CerberusHeapLang.arrBody loc ann ra mo xbty n
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.arrQ_lookup :
∀ (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann ra : core_run_annotation) (mo : memory_order)
  (ibty accbty pbty xbty : core_base_type) (n : Int),
  CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel (CerberusHeapLang.arrQ loc ann ra mo ibty accbty pbty xbty n)
      CerberusHeapLang.arrLoopSym =
    some
      ([(CerberusHeapLang.arrISym, ibty), (CerberusHeapLang.arrAccSym, accbty),
          (CerberusHeapLang.arrPSym, pbty)],
        CerberusHeapLang.arrBody loc ann ra mo xbty n)

theorem CerberusHeapLang.arr_args_eval :
∀ {f : Fmap sym value},
  CerberusHeapLang.SymFrame f →
    ∀ (i : Nat) (acc : Int) (rest : List (Fmap sym value)) (x id a : Int),
      CerberusHeapLang.evalPexprs
          (CerberusHeapLang.arrFrameX (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal x) (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal ↑i)
              (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal acc) (Vobject (OVpointer (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id a)))
              f ::
            rest)
          [CerberusHeapLang.arrIncPe, CerberusHeapLang.arrAccXPe, CerberusHeapLang.arrShiftPe] =
        some
          [CerberusHeapLang.ivVal (↑i + 1), CerberusHeapLang.ivVal (acc + x),
            Vobject (OVpointer (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id (a + 4)))]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.arr_blockSpecs :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann ra : core_run_annotation) (mo : memory_order)
  (ibty accbty pbty xbty : core_base_type) (vs : List Int) (id a : Int) (aty : ctype)
  (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  vs.length * 4 ≤ CerbMem.sizeofCtype aty →
    ∀ (ety : integerType),
      (∀ (i : Nat) (hi : i < vs.length) (lum : List (Int × identifier)) (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
          CerbMem.reconstructValue lum fpm (a + ↑(4 * i)) CerberusHeapLang.intTy
              (List.take (CerbMem.sizeofCtype CerberusHeapLang.intTy) (List.drop (4 * i) bs)) =
            CerbMem.MemValue.MVinteger ety (CerbMem.integerIval vs[i])) →
        ⊢
          CerberusHeapLang.blockSpecs
            (CerberusHeapLang.arrQ loc ann ra mo ibty accbty pbty xbty ↑vs.length)
            (CerberusHeapLang.arrLs vs id a aty bs) (CerberusHeapLang.arrPost vs id a aty bs)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.arr_body_wps :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann ra : core_run_annotation) (mo : memory_order)
  (ibty accbty pbty xbty : core_base_type) (vs : List Int) (id a : Int) (aty : ctype)
  (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  vs.length * 4 ≤ CerbMem.sizeofCtype aty →
    ∀ (ety : integerType),
      (∀ (i : Nat) (hi : i < vs.length) (lum : List (Int × identifier)) (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
          CerbMem.reconstructValue lum fpm (a + ↑(4 * i)) CerberusHeapLang.intTy
              (List.take (CerbMem.sizeofCtype CerberusHeapLang.intTy) (List.drop (4 * i) bs)) =
            CerbMem.MemValue.MVinteger ety (CerbMem.integerIval vs[i])) →
        ∀ (i : Nat) (f : Fmap sym value) (rest : List (Fmap sym value)),
          CerberusHeapLang.SymFrame f →
            i ≤ vs.length →
              id ↦{Iris.DFrac.own 1} { addr := a, ty := aty, bytes := bs } ⊢
                CerberusHeapLang.wps
                  (CerberusHeapLang.arrQ loc ann ra mo ibty accbty pbty xbty ↑vs.length)
                  (CerberusHeapLang.arrLs vs id a aty bs) (CerberusHeapLang.arrPost vs id a aty bs)
                  (CerberusHeapLang.arrBody loc ann ra mo xbty ↑vs.length)
                  (CerberusHeapLang.arrFrame (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal ↑i)
                      (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal (List.take i vs).sum)
                      (Vobject (OVpointer (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id (a + ↑(4 * i))))) f ::
                    rest)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.arr_exit_eval :
∀ {f : Fmap sym value},
  CerberusHeapLang.SymFrame f →
    ∀ (i : Nat) (acc : Int) (vp : value) (rest : List (Fmap sym value)),
      CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr
          (CerberusHeapLang.arrFrame (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal ↑i) (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal acc) vp
              f ::
            rest)
          CerberusHeapLang.arrExitPe =
        some (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal acc)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.arr_guard_eval :
∀ {f : Fmap sym value},
  CerberusHeapLang.SymFrame f →
    ∀ (i : Nat) (acc : Int) (vp : value) (rest : List (Fmap sym value)) (n : Int),
      CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr
          (CerberusHeapLang.arrFrame (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal ↑i) (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal acc) vp
              f ::
            rest)
          (CerberusHeapLang.arrGuard n) =
        some (CerberusHeapLang.boolValue (decide (↑i < n)))
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.arr_p_eval :
∀ {f : Fmap sym value},
  CerberusHeapLang.SymFrame f →
    ∀ (i : Nat) (acc : Int) (vp : value) (rest : List (Fmap sym value)),
      CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr
          (CerberusHeapLang.arrFrame (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal ↑i) (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal acc) vp
              f ::
            rest)
          (Pexpr [] () (PEsym CerberusHeapLang.arrPSym)) =
        some vp
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.arr_wp_readout :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann ra : core_run_annotation) (mo : memory_order)
  (ibty accbty pbty xbty : core_base_type) (vs : List Int) (id a : Int) (aty : ctype)
  (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  vs.length * 4 ≤ CerbMem.sizeofCtype aty →
    ∀ (ety : integerType),
      (∀ (i : Nat) (hi : i < vs.length) (lum : List (Int × identifier)) (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
          CerbMem.reconstructValue lum fpm (a + ↑(4 * i)) CerberusHeapLang.intTy
              (List.take (CerbMem.sizeofCtype CerberusHeapLang.intTy) (List.drop (4 * i) bs)) =
            CerbMem.MemValue.MVinteger ety (CerbMem.integerIval vs[i])) →
        ∀ (sbty : core_base_type),
          id ↦{Iris.DFrac.own 1} { addr := a, ty := aty, bytes := bs } ⊢
            WP
              {
                e :=
                  CerberusHeapLang.arrProg loc ann ra mo sbty ibty accbty pbty xbty
                    (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id a) ↑vs.length,
                ρ := [fmapEmpty],
                lbl := CerberusHeapLang.arrQ loc ann ra mo ibty accbty pbty xbty ↑vs.length } @
              Iris.Stuckness.NotStuck ;
              ⊤ {{
                w,
                  ∀ σ' ns κs nt,
                    Iris.stateInterp σ' ns κs nt ={⊤,∅}=∗
                      ⌜w.val = CerberusHeapLang.ivVal vs.sum ∧
                          CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ' id { addr := a, ty := aty, bytes := bs }⌝ }}
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.arr_wps :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann ra : core_run_annotation) (mo : memory_order)
  (ibty accbty pbty xbty : core_base_type) (vs : List Int) (id a : Int) (aty : ctype)
  (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  vs.length * 4 ≤ CerbMem.sizeofCtype aty →
    ∀ (ety : integerType),
      (∀ (i : Nat) (hi : i < vs.length) (lum : List (Int × identifier)) (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
          CerbMem.reconstructValue lum fpm (a + ↑(4 * i)) CerberusHeapLang.intTy
              (List.take (CerbMem.sizeofCtype CerberusHeapLang.intTy) (List.drop (4 * i) bs)) =
            CerbMem.MemValue.MVinteger ety (CerbMem.integerIval vs[i])) →
        ∀ (sbty : core_base_type),
          id ↦{Iris.DFrac.own 1} { addr := a, ty := aty, bytes := bs } ⊢
            CerberusHeapLang.wps
              (CerberusHeapLang.arrQ loc ann ra mo ibty accbty pbty xbty ↑vs.length)
              (CerberusHeapLang.arrLs vs id a aty bs) (CerberusHeapLang.arrPost vs id a aty bs)
              (CerberusHeapLang.arrProg loc ann ra mo sbty ibty accbty pbty xbty
                (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id a) ↑vs.length)
              [fmapEmpty]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.arrayShift_cellPtr :
∀ (id p : Int),
  CerbMem.arrayShiftPtrval (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id p) CerberusHeapLang.intTy
      (CerbMem.integerIval 1) =
    CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id (p + 4)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.array_sum_certified :
∀ (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann ra : core_run_annotation) (mo : memory_order)
  (ibty accbty pbty xbty : core_base_type) {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors}
  [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF] (sbty : core_base_type) (vs : List Int) (id a : Int)
  (aty : ctype) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  vs.length * 4 ≤ CerbMem.sizeofCtype aty →
    ∀ (ety : integerType),
      (∀ (i : Nat) (hi : i < vs.length) (lum : List (Int × identifier)) (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
          CerbMem.reconstructValue lum fpm (a + ↑(4 * i)) CerberusHeapLang.intTy
              (List.take (CerbMem.sizeofCtype CerberusHeapLang.intTy) (List.drop (4 * i) bs)) =
            CerbMem.MemValue.MVinteger ety (CerbMem.integerIval vs[i])) →
        CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false →
          ∀ (σ₀ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
            CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ₀
                (Iris.Std.PartialMap.singleton id { addr := a, ty := aty, bytes := bs }) →
              ∀ (nsteps : Nat) (aids : Nat → Nat),
                4 + nsteps ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
                  3 + nsteps ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
                    have prog :=
                      CerberusHeapLang.arrProg loc ann ra mo sbty ibty accbty pbty xbty
                        (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id a) ↑vs.length;
                    have rs :=
                      CerberusHeapLang.arrRS loc ann ra mo ibty accbty pbty xbty ↑vs.length;
                    (∀ (r : kill_reason mem_error),
                        CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids nsteps
                            (CerberusHeapLang.procThread CerberusHeapLang.arrProcSym prog
                              [fmapEmpty])
                            σ₀ ≠
                          CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.killed r) ∧
                      CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids nsteps
                            (CerberusHeapLang.procThread CerberusHeapLang.arrProcSym prog
                              [fmapEmpty])
                            σ₀ ≠
                          CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.stuck ∧
                        ∀ (v : value) (σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
                          CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids nsteps
                                (CerberusHeapLang.procThread CerberusHeapLang.arrProcSym prog
                                  [fmapEmpty])
                                σ₀ =
                              CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ' →
                            v = CerberusHeapLang.ivVal vs.sum ∧
                              CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ' id { addr := a, ty := aty, bytes := bs }
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.ars_create_active :
∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {pref : prefix0} {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue} {ty : ctype}
  {reqAddr : Option Int} {initOpt : Option CerbMem.MemValue}
  {k : Nat → CerbMem.PointerValue → thread_state} {tid aid : Nat} {dst : driver_state}
  {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.allocateObject 0 pref align ty reqAddr initOpt)
        dst.layout_state =
      some (pv, σ') →
    CerberusHeapLang.runOne
        (action_request_sequential2 loc tid aid (CreateRequest2 pref align ty reqAddr initOpt k))
        dst =
      (NDactive (),
        { core_file := dst.core_file, core_extern := dst.core_extern,
          core_state0 := update_thread_state tid (k aid pv) dst.core_state0,
          core_run_state0 := dst.core_run_state0, layout_state := σ',
          concurrency_state := dst.concurrency_state, fs_state0 := dst.fs_state0,
          trace := ME_allocate_object tid pref align ty initOpt pv :: dst.trace,
          symbolic_assoc := dst.symbolic_assoc, blocked := dst.blocked,
          dr_step_counter := dst.dr_step_counter })
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.ars_load_active :
∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {mo : memory_order} {ty : ctype} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue}
  {k : Nat → CerbMem.Footprint → CerbMem.MemValue → thread_state} {tid aid : Nat}
  {dst : driver_state} {fp : CerbMem.Footprint} {mval : CerbMem.MemValue}
  {σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.loadM loc ty pv) dst.layout_state = some ((fp, mval), σ') →
    CerberusHeapLang.runOne (action_request_sequential2 loc tid aid (LoadRequest2 mo ty pv k)) dst =
      (NDactive (),
        { core_file := dst.core_file, core_extern := dst.core_extern,
          core_state0 := update_thread_state tid (k aid fp mval) dst.core_state0,
          core_run_state0 := dst.core_run_state0, layout_state := σ',
          concurrency_state := dst.concurrency_state, fs_state0 := dst.fs_state0,
          trace := ME_load loc none ty pv mval :: dst.trace, symbolic_assoc := dst.symbolic_assoc,
          blocked := dst.blocked, dr_step_counter := dst.dr_step_counter })
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.ars_store_active :
∀ {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {mo : memory_order} {ty : ctype} {lk : Bool} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue}
  {mv : CerbMem.MemValue} {k : Nat → CerbMem.Footprint → thread_state} {tid aid : Nat}
  {dst : driver_state} {fp : CerbMem.Footprint} {σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.storeM loc ty lk pv mv) dst.layout_state = some (fp, σ') →
    CerberusHeapLang.runOne
        (action_request_sequential2 loc tid aid (StoreRequest2 mo ty lk pv mv k)) dst =
      (NDactive (),
        { core_file := dst.core_file, core_extern := dst.core_extern,
          core_state0 := update_thread_state tid (k aid fp) dst.core_state0,
          core_run_state0 := dst.core_run_state0, layout_state := σ',
          concurrency_state := dst.concurrency_state, fs_state0 := dst.fs_state0,
          trace := ME_store loc none ty lk pv mv :: dst.trace, symbolic_assoc := dst.symbolic_assoc,
          blocked := dst.blocked, dr_step_counter := dst.dr_step_counter })

theorem CerberusHeapLang.aux2_bridge :
∀ {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {v : value},
  CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe →
    CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ρ pe = some v →
      CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
        ∀ (fuel : Nat) (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (loc : CerbLocation.Loc)
          (cloc : Option CerbLocation.Loc) (mem : Option CerbMem.MemState)
          (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation),
          eval_pexpr_aux2_lemFuel (fuel + 1) tds loc cloc fmapEmpty ρ mem file pe =
            exception_undef_return (Sum.inr v)

def CerberusHeapLang.bindArgs :
List (sym × core_base_type) → List value → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.bindArgs_arr :
∀ (ibty accbty pbty : core_base_type) (v1 v2 v3 : value) (f : Fmap sym value)
  (rest : List (Fmap sym value)),
  CerberusHeapLang.bindArgs
      [(CerberusHeapLang.arrISym, ibty), (CerberusHeapLang.arrAccSym, accbty),
        (CerberusHeapLang.arrPSym, pbty)]
      [v1, v2, v3] (f :: rest) =
    CerberusHeapLang.arrFrame v1 v2 v3 f :: rest
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.bindArgs_cons :
∀ (params : List (sym × core_base_type)) (vs : List value) (ev0 : Fmap sym value)
  (evs : List (Fmap sym value)),
  ∃ ev0', CerberusHeapLang.bindArgs params vs (ev0 :: evs) = ev0' :: evs
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.bindArgs_fib :
∀ (ibty abty bbty : core_base_type) (v1 v2 v3 : value) (f : Fmap sym value)
  (rest : List (Fmap sym value)),
  CerberusHeapLang.bindArgs
      [(CerberusHeapLang.fibISym, ibty), (CerberusHeapLang.fibASym, abty),
        (CerberusHeapLang.fibBSym, bbty)]
      [v1, v2, v3] (f :: rest) =
    CerberusHeapLang.fibFrame v1 v2 v3 f :: rest
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.bindArgs_x :
∀ (b : core_base_type) (v : value) (f : Fmap sym value) (rest : List (Fmap sym value)),
  CerberusHeapLang.bindArgs [(CerberusHeapLang.xSym, b)] [v] (f :: rest) =
    CerberusHeapLang.envAdd CerberusHeapLang.xSym v f :: rest

  (ps :

  ∃ ev0', CerberusHeapLang.bindSaveParams ps cvals (ev0 :: evs) = ev0' :: evs

theorem CerberusHeapLang.bindSave_arr :

  (rest : List (Fmap sym value)),
  CerberusHeapLang.bindSaveParams (CerberusHeapLang.arrParams ibty accbty pbty base)

def CerberusHeapLang.blockSpecs :
{hlc : Iris.HasLC} →
  {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
    [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF] →
      CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap →
        CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF →
          (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF) → Iris.IProp GF
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.blockSpecs_intro :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF},
  (∀ (l : sym) (params : List (sym × core_base_type)) (cont : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr)
      (vs : List value) (ev0 : Fmap sym value) (evs : List (Fmap sym value)),
      CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel Q l = some (params, cont) →
        Ls l vs (ev0 :: evs) ⊢
          CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls Ψ cont (CerberusHeapLang.bindArgs params vs (ev0 :: evs))) →
    ⊢ CerberusHeapLang.blockSpecs Q Ls Ψ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.blockSpecs_intro_variant :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (μ : sym → List value → Nat),
  (∀ (l : sym) (params : List (sym × core_base_type)) (cont : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr)
      (vs : List value) (ev0 : Fmap sym value) (evs : List (Fmap sym value)),
      CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel Q l = some (params, cont) →
        (∀ (l' : sym) (params' : List (sym × core_base_type)) (cont' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr)
            (vs' : List value) (ev0' : Fmap sym value) (evs' : List (Fmap sym value)),
            CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel Q l' = some (params', cont') →
              μ l' vs' < μ l vs →
                Ls l' vs' (ev0' :: evs') ⊢
                  CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls Ψ cont'
                    (CerberusHeapLang.bindArgs params' vs' (ev0' :: evs'))) →
          Ls l vs (ev0 :: evs) ⊢
            CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls Ψ cont (CerberusHeapLang.bindArgs params vs (ev0 :: evs))) →
    ⊢ CerberusHeapLang.blockSpecs Q Ls Ψ
----

∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (pv : CerbMem.PointerValue) (ty : ctype) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  pv ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} ty ; bs ⊢

theorem CerberusHeapLang.cells_readout :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS Iris.HasLC.hasLC GF]
  (post : value → CerberusHeapLang.CellMap → Prop) (R : CerberusHeapLang.CellMap) (vv : value),
  ((∃ Q, ⌜post vv Q⌝ ∗ [∗map] i ↦ c ∈ Q, i ↦{Iris.DFrac.own 1} c) ∗
      [∗map] i ↦ c ∈ R, i ↦{Iris.DFrac.own 1} c) ⊢
    ∀ σ' ns κs nt,
      Iris.stateInterp σ' ns κs nt ={⊤,∅}=∗
        ⌜∃ Q,
            post vv Q ∧
              Iris.Std.PartialMap.disjoint Q R ∧
                CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ' (Iris.Std.PartialMap.union Q R)⌝
----

      CerberusHeapLang.yPtr ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ;
        CerberusHeapLang.sixBytes ⊢
    [∗map] i ↦ c ∈ CerberusHeapLang.mC, i ↦{Iris.DFrac.own 1} c

----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.collect_saves_loop :
∀ (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann ra : core_run_annotation) (mo : memory_order)
  (bty xbty sbty : core_base_type) (c : CerbMem.PointerValue) (n : Int),
  collect_saves (CerberusHeapLang.loopProg loc ann ra mo bty xbty sbty c n) =
    CerberusHeapLang.loopQ loc ann ra mo bty xbty c
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.counter_loop_certified :
∀ (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann ra : core_run_annotation) (mo : memory_order)
  (bty xbty : core_base_type) {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF]
  (sbty : core_base_type) (idx addr : Int) (bs0 : List CerbMem.AbsByte) (n : Int),
  0 ≤ n →
    CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false →
      ∀ (σ₀ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
        CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ₀
            (Iris.Std.PartialMap.singleton idx
              { addr := addr, ty := CerberusHeapLang.intTy, bytes := bs0 }) →
          ∀ (nsteps : Nat) (aids : Nat → Nat),
            4 + nsteps ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
              3 + nsteps ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
                have prog :=
                  CerberusHeapLang.loopProg loc ann ra mo bty xbty sbty
                    (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr idx addr) n;
                have rs :=
                  CerberusHeapLang.loopRS loc ann ra mo bty xbty
                    (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr idx addr);
                (∀ (r : kill_reason mem_error),
                    CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids nsteps
                        (CerberusHeapLang.procThread CerberusHeapLang.loopProcSym prog [fmapEmpty])
                        σ₀ ≠
                      CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.killed r) ∧
                  CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids nsteps
                        (CerberusHeapLang.procThread CerberusHeapLang.loopProcSym prog [fmapEmpty])
                        σ₀ ≠
                      CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.stuck ∧
                    ∀ (v : value) (σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
                      CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids nsteps
                            (CerberusHeapLang.procThread CerberusHeapLang.loopProcSym prog
                              [fmapEmpty])
                            σ₀ =
                          CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ' →
                        v = Vunit ∧
                          ∃ bs',
                            (n = 0 ∧ bs' = bs0 ∨ 0 < n ∧ bs' = CerberusHeapLang.sevenBytes) ∧
                              ∃ i a,
                                CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr idx addr = CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr i a ∧
                                  CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ' i
                                    { addr := a, ty := CerberusHeapLang.intTy, bytes := bs' }
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.counter_loop_certified_production :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF] (loc : CerbLocation.Loc)
  (ann ra : core_run_annotation) (mo : memory_order) (bty xbty sbty : core_base_type)
  (idx addr : Int) (bs0 : List CerbMem.AbsByte) (n : Int),
  0 ≤ n →
    CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false →
      ∀ (σ₀ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
        CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ₀
            (Iris.Std.PartialMap.singleton idx
              { addr := addr, ty := CerberusHeapLang.intTy, bytes := bs0 }) →
          ∀ (nsteps : Nat) (aids : Nat → Nat),
            4 + nsteps ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
              3 + nsteps ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
                have prog :=
                  CerberusHeapLang.loopProg loc ann ra mo bty xbty sbty
                    (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr idx addr) n;
                have rs :=
                  initial_core_run_state
                    (collect_labeled_continuations_NEW (CerberusHeapLang.prodFile prog));
                (∀ (r : kill_reason mem_error),
                    CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids nsteps
                        (CerberusHeapLang.procThread CerberusHeapLang.mainSym prog [fmapEmpty]) σ₀ ≠
                      CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.killed r) ∧
                  CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids nsteps
                        (CerberusHeapLang.procThread CerberusHeapLang.mainSym prog [fmapEmpty]) σ₀ ≠
                      CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.stuck ∧
                    ∀ (v : value) (σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
                      CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids nsteps
                            (CerberusHeapLang.procThread CerberusHeapLang.mainSym prog [fmapEmpty])
                            σ₀ =
                          CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ' →
                        v = Vunit ∧
                          ∃ bs',
                            (n = 0 ∧ bs' = bs0 ∨ 0 < n ∧ bs' = CerberusHeapLang.sevenBytes) ∧
                              ∃ i a,
                                CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr idx addr = CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr i a ∧
                                  CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ' i
                                    { addr := a, ty := CerberusHeapLang.intTy, bytes := bs' }
----

def CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep :
Nat → core_run_state → CerberusHeapLang.Mem → core_step2 → CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep_create_active :
∀ {aid : Nat} {rs : core_run_state} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {str : String}
  {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {tid : thread_id} {uw : Bool} {pref : prefix0}
  {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue} {ty : ctype} {reqAddr : Option Int}
  {k : Nat → CerbMem.PointerValue → thread_state} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue},
  CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.allocateObject 0 pref align ty reqAddr none) σ =
      some (pv, σ') →
    CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep aid rs σ
        (Step_action_request2 str loc tid uw
          (stExceptUndef_return (CreateRequest2 pref align ty reqAddr none k))) =
      CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next (k aid pv) σ'
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep_create_refusal :
∀ {aid : Nat} {rs : core_run_state} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {str : String}
  {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {tid : thread_id} {uw : Bool} {pref : prefix0}
  {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue} {ty : ctype} {reqAddr : Option Int}
  {k : Nat → CerbMem.PointerValue → thread_state},
  CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.allocateObject 0 pref align ty reqAddr none) σ = none →
    (CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep aid rs σ
        (Step_action_request2 str loc tid uw
          (stExceptUndef_return (CreateRequest2 pref align ty reqAddr none k)))).isRefusal
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep_load_active :
∀ {aid : Nat} {rs : core_run_state} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {str : String}
  {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {tid : thread_id} {uw : Bool} {mo : memory_order} {ty : ctype}
  {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {k : Nat → CerbMem.Footprint → CerbMem.MemValue → thread_state}
  {fp : CerbMem.Footprint} {mval : CerbMem.MemValue},
  CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.loadM loc ty pv) σ = some ((fp, mval), σ') →
    CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep aid rs σ
        (Step_action_request2 str loc tid uw (stExceptUndef_return (LoadRequest2 mo ty pv k))) =
      CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next (k aid fp mval) σ'
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep_load_refusal :
∀ {aid : Nat} {rs : core_run_state} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {str : String}
  {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {tid : thread_id} {uw : Bool} {mo : memory_order} {ty : ctype}
  {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {k : Nat → CerbMem.Footprint → CerbMem.MemValue → thread_state},

    (CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep aid rs σ
        (Step_action_request2 str loc tid uw

----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep_store_active :
∀ {aid : Nat} {rs : core_run_state} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {str : String}
  {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {tid : thread_id} {uw : Bool} {mo : memory_order} {ty : ctype}
  {lk : Bool} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {mv : CerbMem.MemValue}

          (stExceptUndef_return (StoreRequest2 mo ty lk pv mv k)))).isRefusal
----
def CerberusHeapLang.drive :
(Nat → Nat) → Nat → thread_state → CerberusHeapLang.Mem → CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.drive.eq_def :
∀ (aids : Nat → Nat) (x : Nat) (x_1 : thread_state) (x_2 : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
  CerberusHeapLang.drive aids x x_1 x_2 =
    match x, x_1, x_2 with
    | 0, th, σ => CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.more th σ
    | n.succ, th, σ =>
      match
        List.map (CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep (aids 0) CerberusHeapLang.spikeRunState σ)
          (step_ctx fmapEmpty σ CerberusHeapLang.spikeFile fmapEmpty 0 (none, th)) with
      | [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next th' σ'] =>
        CerberusHeapLang.drive (fun i => aids (i + 1)) n th' σ'
      | [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.done v] => CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ
      | [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.killed r] => CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.killed r
      | x => CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.stuck
----
def CerberusHeapLang.driveJ :
core_run_state →
  (Nat → Nat) → Nat → thread_state → CerberusHeapLang.Mem → CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.driveJ.eq_def :
∀ (rs : core_run_state) (aids : Nat → Nat) (x : Nat) (x_1 : thread_state)
  (x_2 : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
  CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids x x_1 x_2 =
    match x, x_1, x_2 with
    | 0, th, σ => CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.more th σ
    | n.succ, th, σ =>
      match
        List.map (CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep (aids 0) rs σ)
          (step_ctx fmapEmpty σ CerberusHeapLang.spikeFile fmapEmpty 0 (none, th)) with
      | [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next th' σ'] =>
        CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs (fun i => aids (i + 1)) n th' σ'
      | [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.done v] => CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ
      | [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.killed r] => CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.killed r
      | x => CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.stuck
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.driveJ_done :
∀ (p : sym) (rs : core_run_state) (aids : Nat → Nat) (n : Nat) (v : value)
  (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
  CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids (n + 1)
      (CerberusHeapLang.procThread p (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v)) ρ)
      σ =
    CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.driveJ_scrutinee :
∀ (p : sym) (rs : core_run_state) (aid : Nat) (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr)
  (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
  List.map (CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep aid rs σ)
      (step_ctx fmapEmpty σ CerberusHeapLang.spikeFile fmapEmpty 0
        (none, CerberusHeapLang.procThread p e ρ)) =
    CerberusHeapLang.engineOutcomesP p aid rs e ρ σ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.driveJ_step :
∀ {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {p : sym} {rs : core_run_state},
  CerberusHeapLang.LabeledAt rs p Q →
    ∀ (aids : Nat → Nat) (n : Nat) {e e' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ev0 : Fmap sym value}
      {evs : List (Fmap sym value)} {ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
      CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e →
        CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
          CerberusHeapLang.Step Q (e, ev0 :: evs, σ) (e', ρ', σ') →
            CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids (n + 1) (CerberusHeapLang.procThread p e (ev0 :: evs))
                σ =
              CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs (fun i => aids (i + 1)) n
                (CerberusHeapLang.procThread p e' ρ') σ'
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.driveJ_value_pure :
∀ (p : sym) (rs : core_run_state) (φp : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal → CerberusHeapLang.Mem → Prop)
  (aids : Nat → Nat) (v : value) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
  (∃ w, w.val = v ∧ φp w σ) →
    ∀ (n : Nat),
      CerberusHeapLang.DriveOk φp
        (CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids n
          (CerberusHeapLang.procThread p (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v))
            ρ)
          σ)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.drive_after_setup :
∀ (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (fs : CerbFS.FsState) (args : List String) (dstD : driver_state),
  CerberusHeapLang.runOne (driver2_lemFuel lemDefaultFuel fmapEmpty false)
        (CerberusHeapLang.prodEntryState e fs) =
      (NDactive (), dstD) →
    CerberusHeapLang.runOne (drive fmapEmpty false (CerberusHeapLang.prodFile e) args)
        (initial_driver_state (CerberusHeapLang.prodFile e) fs) =
      (NDactive (finalize fmapEmpty "drive (without concur)" dstD), dstD)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.drive_classify :
∀ (e₀ : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ₀ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (σ₀ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem)
  (φp : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal → CerberusHeapLang.Mem → Prop),
  (∀ (r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
      CerberusHeapLang.Reach ({ e := e₀, ρ := ρ₀, lbl := CerberusHeapLang.spikeLbl }, σ₀) (r, σ) →
        Iris.ProgramLogic.PrimStep.NotStuck (r, σ)) →
    (∀ (w : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
        CerberusHeapLang.Reach ({ e := e₀, ρ := ρ₀, lbl := CerberusHeapLang.spikeLbl }, σ₀)
            (CerberusHeapLang.ofValRt w, σ) →
          φp w σ) →
      ∀ (n : Nat) (aids : Nat → Nat) (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ev0 : Fmap sym value)
        (evs : List (Fmap sym value)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
        CerberusHeapLang.Reach ({ e := e₀, ρ := ρ₀, lbl := CerberusHeapLang.spikeLbl }, σ₀)
            ({ e := e, ρ := ev0 :: evs, lbl := CerberusHeapLang.spikeLbl }, σ) →
          CerberusHeapLang.FragP e →
            CerberusHeapLang.esize e + n ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
              CerberusHeapLang.DriveOk φp
                (CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.envThread e (ev0 :: evs)) σ)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.drive_classifyJ :
∀ {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {p : sym} {rs : core_run_state},
  CerberusHeapLang.LabeledAt rs p Q →
    (∀ (l : sym) (params : List (sym × core_base_type)) (cont : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
        CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel Q l = some (params, cont) → CerberusHeapLang.FragJ cont) →
      ∀ (n₀ : Nat),
        (∀ (l : sym) (params : List (sym × core_base_type)) (cont : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
            CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel Q l = some (params, cont) →
              CerberusHeapLang.esize cont + n₀ ≤ lemDefaultFuel) →
          ∀ (e₀ : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ₀ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack)
            (σ₀ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem)
            (φp : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal → CerberusHeapLang.Mem → Prop),
            (∀ (r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
                CerberusHeapLang.Reach ({ e := e₀, ρ := ρ₀, lbl := Q }, σ₀) (r, σ) →
                  Iris.ProgramLogic.PrimStep.NotStuck (r, σ)) →
              (∀ (w : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
                  CerberusHeapLang.Reach ({ e := e₀, ρ := ρ₀, lbl := Q }, σ₀)
                      (CerberusHeapLang.ofValRt w, σ) →
                    φp w σ) →
                ∀ (n : Nat),
                  n ≤ n₀ →
                    ∀ (aids : Nat → Nat) (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ev0 : Fmap sym value)
                      (evs : List (Fmap sym value)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
                      CerberusHeapLang.Reach ({ e := e₀, ρ := ρ₀, lbl := Q }, σ₀)
                          ({ e := e, ρ := ev0 :: evs, lbl := Q }, σ) →
                        CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e →
                          CerberusHeapLang.esize e + n ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
                            CerberusHeapLang.DriveOk φp
                              (CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids n
                                (CerberusHeapLang.procThread p e (ev0 :: evs)) σ)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.drive_done :
∀ {aids : Nat → Nat} {n : Nat} {th : thread_state} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {v : value},
  List.map (CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep (aids 0) CerberusHeapLang.spikeRunState σ)
        (step_ctx fmapEmpty σ CerberusHeapLang.spikeFile fmapEmpty 0 (none, th)) =
      [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.done v] →
    CerberusHeapLang.drive aids (n + 1) th σ = CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.drive_next :
∀ {aids : Nat → Nat} {n : Nat} {th th' : thread_state} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  List.map (CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep (aids 0) CerberusHeapLang.spikeRunState σ)
        (step_ctx fmapEmpty σ CerberusHeapLang.spikeFile fmapEmpty 0 (none, th)) =
      [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next th' σ'] →
    CerberusHeapLang.drive aids (n + 1) th σ =
      CerberusHeapLang.drive (fun i => aids (i + 1)) n th' σ'
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.drive_scrutinee :
∀ (aid : Nat) (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
  List.map (CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep aid CerberusHeapLang.spikeRunState σ)
      (step_ctx fmapEmpty σ CerberusHeapLang.spikeFile fmapEmpty 0
        (none, CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread e)) =
    CerberusHeapLang.engineOutcomes aid e CerberusHeapLang.spikeEnv σ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.drive_scrutinee_env :
∀ (aid : Nat) (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack)
  (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
  List.map (CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep aid CerberusHeapLang.spikeRunState σ)
      (step_ctx fmapEmpty σ CerberusHeapLang.spikeFile fmapEmpty 0
        (none, CerberusHeapLang.envThread e ρ)) =
    CerberusHeapLang.engineOutcomes aid e ρ σ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.drive_step_done :
∀ {aids : Nat → Nat} {n : Nat} {th : thread_state} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {v : value},
  List.map (CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep (aids 0) CerberusHeapLang.spikeRunState σ)
        (step_ctx fmapEmpty σ CerberusHeapLang.spikeFile fmapEmpty 0 (none, th)) =
      [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.done v] →
    CerberusHeapLang.drive aids (n + 1) th σ = CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.drive_step_error :
∀ {aids : Nat → Nat} {n : Nat} {th : thread_state} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {s : String},
  List.map (CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep (aids 0) CerberusHeapLang.spikeRunState σ)
        (step_ctx fmapEmpty σ CerberusHeapLang.spikeFile fmapEmpty 0 (none, th)) =
      [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.error s] →
    CerberusHeapLang.drive aids (n + 1) th σ = CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.stuck
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.drive_step_killed :
∀ {aids : Nat → Nat} {n : Nat} {th : thread_state} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem}
  {r : kill_reason mem_error},
  List.map (CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep (aids 0) CerberusHeapLang.spikeRunState σ)
        (step_ctx fmapEmpty σ CerberusHeapLang.spikeFile fmapEmpty 0 (none, th)) =
      [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.killed r] →
    CerberusHeapLang.drive aids (n + 1) th σ = CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.killed r
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.drive_step_next :
∀ {aids : Nat → Nat} {n : Nat} {th th' : thread_state} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  List.map (CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep (aids 0) CerberusHeapLang.spikeRunState σ)
        (step_ctx fmapEmpty σ CerberusHeapLang.spikeFile fmapEmpty 0 (none, th)) =
      [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next th' σ'] →
    CerberusHeapLang.drive aids (n + 1) th σ =
      CerberusHeapLang.drive (fun i => aids (i + 1)) n th' σ'
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.drive_step_off :
∀ {aids : Nat → Nat} {n : Nat} {th : thread_state} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
  List.map (CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep (aids 0) CerberusHeapLang.spikeRunState σ)
        (step_ctx fmapEmpty σ CerberusHeapLang.spikeFile fmapEmpty 0 (none, th)) =
      [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.offFragment] →
    CerberusHeapLang.drive aids (n + 1) th σ = CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.stuck
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.drive_value_pure :
∀ (φp : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal → CerberusHeapLang.Mem → Prop) (aids : Nat → Nat) (v : value)
  (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
  (∃ w, w.val = v ∧ φp w σ) →
    ∀ (n : Nat),
      CerberusHeapLang.DriveOk φp
        (CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n
          (CerberusHeapLang.envThread (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v)) ρ)
          σ)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.driver2_done :
∀ (fl : Nat) (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (dst dstF : driver_state)
  (th thF : thread_state) (v : value),
  dst.core_state0.thread_states = [(0, none, th)] →
    CerberusHeapLang.runOne (drive_nonmemory_steps_aux2_lemFuel lemDefaultFuel tds fmapEmpty [0])
          dst =
        (NDactive (fmapAddBy defaultCompare 0 [Step_done2 v] fmapEmpty), dstF) →
      dstF.core_state0.thread_states = [(0, none, thF)] →
        CerberusHeapLang.runOne (driver2_lemFuel fl.succ tds false) dst =
          (NDactive (),
            { core_file := dstF.core_file, core_extern := dstF.core_extern,
              core_state0 :=
                have __src := dstF.core_state0;
                {
                  thread_states :=
                    [(0, none,
                        { arena := mk_value_e v, stack0 := Stack_empty, errno := thF.errno,
                          env := thF.env, current_proc_opt := thF.current_proc_opt,
                          exec_loc := thF.exec_loc, current_loc := thF.current_loc })],
                  io := __src.io },
              core_run_state0 := dstF.core_run_state0, layout_state := dstF.layout_state,
              concurrency_state := dstF.concurrency_state, fs_state0 := dstF.fs_state0,
              trace := dstF.trace, symbolic_assoc := dstF.symbolic_assoc, blocked := dstF.blocked,
              dr_step_counter := dstF.dr_step_counter })
----

    core_run_state →

        CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → CerberusHeapLang.Mem → List CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome

  (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
  CerberusHeapLang.engineOutcomesP p aid rs
      (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v)) ρ σ =

          ρ)
        σ]

theorem CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps_beta_annot :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type}
  {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value} {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ e ctx
      (generic_expr.Expr []
        (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty)))
          (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v)) e2)) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      ∀ (ev0 : Fmap sym value) (evs : List (Fmap sym value)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
        CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps e (ev0 :: evs) σ =
          [Step_tau2 "Esseq Eannot" TSK_Misc
              (CerberusHeapLang.envThread (apply_ctx ctx (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds e2)))
                (ev0 :: evs))]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps_beta_pure :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type}
  {v : value} {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ e ctx
      (generic_expr.Expr []
        (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty)))
          (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v)) e2)) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      ∀ (ev0 : Fmap sym value) (evs : List (Fmap sym value)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
        CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps e (ev0 :: evs) σ =
          [Step_tau2 "Esseq" TSK_Misc (CerberusHeapLang.envThread (apply_ctx ctx e2) (ev0 :: evs))]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps_create :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc}
  {ann : core_run_annotation} {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue} {ty : ctype} {pref : prefix0},
  CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ e ctx (CerberusHeapLang.createRedex loc ann align ty pref) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false →
        ∀ (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
          CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps e ρ σ =
            [Step_action_request2 "CreateRequest" loc 0 (is_unseq_with_ccall ctx)
                (stExceptUndef_return
                  (CreateRequest2 pref align ty (get_with_address []) none fun x pv =>
                    CerberusHeapLang.envThread
                      (apply_ctx ctx
                        (generic_expr.Expr []
                          (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vobject (OVpointer pv)))))))
                      ρ))]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps_done :
∀ (v : value) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
  CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v)) ρ σ =
    [Step_done2 v]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps_load :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc}
  {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {mo : memory_order},
  CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ e ctx (CerberusHeapLang.loadRedex loc ann ty pv mo) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false →
        ∀ (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
          CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps e ρ σ =
            [Step_action_request2 "LoadRequest" loc 0 (is_unseq_with_ccall ctx)
                (stExceptUndef_return
                  (LoadRequest2 mo ty pv fun x fp mval =>
                    CerberusHeapLang.envThread
                      (apply_ctx ctx
                        (generic_expr.Expr []
                          (Eannot [DA_pos [] fp]
                            (generic_expr.Expr []
                              (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval (valueFromMemValue mval).snd)))))))
                      ρ))]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps_merge :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {ds1 ds2 : List dyn_annotation}
  {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ e ctx
      (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds1 (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds2 b)))) →
    is_irreducible (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds1 (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds2 b)))) =
        false →
      CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
        ∀ (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
          CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps e ρ σ =
            [Step_tau2 "Eannot" TSK_Misc
                (CerberusHeapLang.envThread
                  (apply_ctx ctx (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot (ds1 ++ ds2) b))) ρ)]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps_remove_annot :
∀ (ds : List dyn_annotation) (v : value) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
  CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v)) ρ σ =
    [Step_tau2 "CTX, Eannot(value)" TSK_Misc
        (CerberusHeapLang.envThread (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v)) ρ)]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps_store :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc}
  {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool} {ty : ctype} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value}
  {mo : memory_order} {mv : CerbMem.MemValue},
  CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ e ctx (CerberusHeapLang.storeRedex loc ann lk ty pv cv mo) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false →
        memValueFromValue fmapEmpty (Ctype [] (unatomic_ ty)) cv = some mv →
          ∀ (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
            CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps e ρ σ =
              [Step_action_request2 "StoreRequest" loc 0 (is_unseq_with_ccall ctx)
                  (stExceptUndef_return
                    (StoreRequest2 mo ty lk pv mv fun x fp =>
                      CerberusHeapLang.envThread
                        (apply_ctx ctx
                          (generic_expr.Expr []
                            (Eannot [DA_pos [] fp]
                              (generic_expr.Expr [] (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval Vunit)))))))
                        ρ))]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps_store_illtyped :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc}
  {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool} {ty : ctype} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value}
  {mo : memory_order},
  CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ e ctx (CerberusHeapLang.storeRedex loc ann lk ty pv cv mo) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      memValueFromValue fmapEmpty (Ctype [] (unatomic_ ty)) cv = none →
        ∀ (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
          CerberusHeapLang.engineSteps e ρ σ =
            [Step_error2
                ((CerbLocation.stringFromLocation loc).append
                  ("the value of a store(".append
                    ((CerbPP.stringFromCore_ctype (Ctype [] (unatomic_ ty))).append
                      (") didn't match the lvalue type: ".append
                        (CerbPP.stringFromCore_value cv)))))]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.engine_adequacyJ :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF] {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap}
  {p : sym} {rs : core_run_state},
  CerberusHeapLang.LabeledAt rs p Q →
    (∀ (l : sym) (params : List (sym × core_base_type)) (cont : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
        CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel Q l = some (params, cont) → CerberusHeapLang.FragJ cont) →
      ∀ (e₀ : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ev00 : Fmap sym value) (evs0 : List (Fmap sym value))
        (σ₀ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (m₀ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
        CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e₀ →
          CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ₀ m₀ →
            ∀ (ψ : value → CerberusHeapLang.Mem → Prop),
              (∀ [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS Iris.HasLC.hasLC GF],
                  ([∗map] i ↦ c ∈ m₀, i ↦{Iris.DFrac.own 1} c) ⊢
                    WP { e := e₀, ρ := ev00 :: evs0, lbl := Q } @ Iris.Stuckness.NotStuck ;
                      ⊤ {{ w, ∀ σ' ns κs nt, Iris.stateInterp σ' ns κs nt ={⊤,∅}=∗ ⌜ψ w.val σ'⌝ }}
                        ) →
                ∀ (n : Nat) (aids : Nat → Nat),
                  CerberusHeapLang.esize e₀ + n ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
                    (∀ (l : sym) (params : List (sym × core_base_type))
                        (cont : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
                        CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel Q l = some (params, cont) →
                          CerberusHeapLang.esize cont + n ≤ lemDefaultFuel) →
                      (∀ (r : kill_reason mem_error),
                          CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids n
                              (CerberusHeapLang.procThread p e₀ (ev00 :: evs0)) σ₀ ≠
                            CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.killed r) ∧
                        CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids n
                              (CerberusHeapLang.procThread p e₀ (ev00 :: evs0)) σ₀ ≠
                            CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.stuck ∧
                          ∀ (v : value) (σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
                            CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids n
                                  (CerberusHeapLang.procThread p e₀ (ev00 :: evs0)) σ₀ =
                                CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ' →
                              ψ v σ'
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.engine_complete :
∀ (aid : Nat) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (ev0 : Fmap sym value) (evs : List (Fmap sym value))
  {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.FragP e →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      ∃ o,
        CerberusHeapLang.engineOutcomes aid e (ev0 :: evs) σ = [o] ∧
          CerberusHeapLang.EngineMatch e (ev0 :: evs) σ o
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.engine_step_matchJ :
∀ {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {p : sym} (aid : Nat) {rs : core_run_state},
  CerberusHeapLang.LabeledAt rs p Q →
    ∀ {e e' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)}
      {ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {σ σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
      CerberusHeapLang.FragJ e →
        CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
          CerberusHeapLang.Step Q (e, ev0 :: evs, σ) (e', ρ', σ') →
            CerberusHeapLang.engineOutcomesP p aid rs e (ev0 :: evs) σ =
              [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next (CerberusHeapLang.procThread p e' ρ') σ']
----
def CerberusHeapLang.envAdd :
sym → value → Fmap sym value → Fmap sym value
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.envAdd_lookup :
∀ {f : Fmap sym value},
  CerberusHeapLang.SymFrame f →
    ∀ (cmp' : sym → sym → LemOrdering) (l k : sym) (v : value),
      fmapLookupBy cmp' l (CerberusHeapLang.envAdd k v f) =
        if CerberusHeapLang.symOrd l k = Ordering.eq then some v else fmapLookupBy cmp' l f
