def CerberusHeapLang.esize :
CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → Nat
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.esize.eq_def :
∀ (x : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
  CerberusHeapLang.esize x =
    match x with
    | generic_expr.Expr a (Esseq a_1 e1 e2) =>
      1 + max (CerberusHeapLang.esize e1) (CerberusHeapLang.esize e2)
    | generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot a_1 b) => 1 + CerberusHeapLang.esize b
    | generic_expr.Expr a (Eif a_1 e2 e3) =>
      1 + max (CerberusHeapLang.esize e2) (CerberusHeapLang.esize e3)
    | generic_expr.Expr a (Esave a_1 a_2 body) => 1 + CerberusHeapLang.esize body
    | x => 1
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.esize_action :
∀ {a : List annot} {p : generic_paction core_run_annotation Unit sym},
  CerberusHeapLang.esize (generic_expr.Expr a (Eaction p)) = 1
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.esize_annot :
∀ {a : List annot} {ds : List dyn_annotation} {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.esize (generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot ds b)) = 1 + CerberusHeapLang.esize b
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.esize_ofVal_annot :
∀ {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value},
  CerberusHeapLang.esize (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v)) = 2
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.esize_ofVal_pure :
∀ {v : value},
  CerberusHeapLang.esize (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v)) = 1
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.esize_pos :
∀ (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr), 1 ≤ CerberusHeapLang.esize e
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.esize_pure :
∀ {a : List annot} {pe : pexpr}, CerberusHeapLang.esize (generic_expr.Expr a (Epure pe)) = 1
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.esize_sseq :
∀ {a : List annot} {pat : pattern} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.esize (generic_expr.Expr a (Esseq pat e1 e2)) =
    1 + max (CerberusHeapLang.esize e1) (CerberusHeapLang.esize e2)
----
def CerberusHeapLang.evalArrayShift :
ctype → value → value → Option value
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.evalArrayShift_ptr_one :
∀ (id a : Int),
  CerberusHeapLang.evalArrayShift CerberusHeapLang.intTy
      (Vobject (OVpointer (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id a))) (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal 1) =
    some (Vobject (OVpointer (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id (a + 4))))
----
def CerberusHeapLang.evalBinop :
binop → value → value → Option value
----
def CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr :
CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → generic_pexpr Unit sym → Option value
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr.eq_def :
∀ (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (x : generic_pexpr Unit sym),
  CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ρ x =
    match x with
    | Pexpr a a_1 (PEval v) => some v
    | Pexpr a a_1 (PEsym x) => lookup_env x ρ
    | Pexpr a a_1 (PEop op pe1 pe2) => do
      let v1 ← CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ρ pe1
      let v2 ← CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ρ pe2
      CerberusHeapLang.evalBinop op v1 v2
    | Pexpr a a_1 (PEarray_shift pe1 ty pe2) => do
      let v1 ← CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ρ pe1
      let v2 ← CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ρ pe2
      CerberusHeapLang.evalArrayShift ty v1 v2
    | x => none
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr.induct :
∀ (motive : generic_pexpr Unit sym → Prop),
  (∀ (a : List annot) (a_1 : Unit) (v : value), motive (Pexpr a a_1 (PEval v))) →
    (∀ (a : List annot) (a_1 : Unit) (x : sym), motive (Pexpr a a_1 (PEsym x))) →
      (∀ (a : List annot) (a_1 : Unit) (op : binop) (pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym),
          motive pe1 → motive pe2 → motive (Pexpr a a_1 (PEop op pe1 pe2))) →
        (∀ (a : List annot) (a_1 : Unit) (pe1 : generic_pexpr Unit sym) (ty : ctype)
            (pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym),
            motive pe1 → motive pe2 → motive (Pexpr a a_1 (PEarray_shift pe1 ty pe2))) →
          (∀ (t : generic_pexpr Unit sym),
              (∀ (a : List annot) (a_1 : Unit) (v : value), t = Pexpr a a_1 (PEval v) → False) →
                (∀ (a : List annot) (a_1 : Unit) (x : sym), t = Pexpr a a_1 (PEsym x) → False) →
                  (∀ (a : List annot) (a_1 : Unit) (op : binop) (pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym),
                      t = Pexpr a a_1 (PEop op pe1 pe2) → False) →
                    (∀ (a : List annot) (a_1 : Unit) (pe1 : generic_pexpr Unit sym) (ty : ctype)
                        (pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym),
                        t = Pexpr a a_1 (PEarray_shift pe1 ty pe2) → False) →
                      motive t) →
            ∀ (a : generic_pexpr Unit sym), motive a
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr_array_shift :
∀ (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (a : List annot) (ty : ctype) (pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym),
  CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ρ (Pexpr a () (PEarray_shift pe1 ty pe2)) = do
    let v1 ← CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ρ pe1
    let v2 ← CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ρ pe2
    CerberusHeapLang.evalArrayShift ty v1 v2
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr_none_of_shape :
∀ {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym},
  (∀ (a : List annot) (u : Unit) (v : value), pe = Pexpr a u (PEval v) → False) →
    (∀ (a : List annot) (u : Unit) (x : sym), pe = Pexpr a u (PEsym x) → False) →
      (∀ (a : List annot) (u : Unit) (op : binop) (pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym),
          pe = Pexpr a u (PEop op pe1 pe2) → False) →
        (∀ (a : List annot) (u : Unit) (pe1 : generic_pexpr Unit sym) (ty : ctype)
            (pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym), pe = Pexpr a u (PEarray_shift pe1 ty pe2) → False) →
          CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ρ pe = none
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr_op :
∀ (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (a : List annot) (op : binop) (pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym),
  CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ρ (Pexpr a () (PEop op pe1 pe2)) = do
    let v1 ← CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ρ pe1
    let v2 ← CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ρ pe2
    CerberusHeapLang.evalBinop op v1 v2
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr_peStrip :
∀ {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym},
  CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe →
    CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ρ (CerberusHeapLang.peStrip pe) = CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ρ pe
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr_shape :
∀ {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {v : value},
  CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ρ pe = some v → CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr_sym :
∀ (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (a : List annot) (x : sym),
  CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ρ (Pexpr a () (PEsym x)) = lookup_env x ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr_val :
∀ (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (a : List annot) (v : value),
  CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ρ (Pexpr a () (PEval v)) = some v
----
def CerberusHeapLang.evalPexprs :
CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → List (generic_pexpr Unit sym) → Option (List value)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.evalPexprs_cons :
∀ (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym)
  (pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym)),
  CerberusHeapLang.evalPexprs ρ (pe :: pes) = do
    let v ← CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ρ pe
    let vs ← CerberusHeapLang.evalPexprs ρ pes
    pure (v :: vs)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.evalPexprs_nil :
∀ (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack), CerberusHeapLang.evalPexprs ρ [] = some []
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.exhibit :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (x y : CerbMem.PointerValue) (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann : core_run_annotation)
  (mo : memory_order) (bs bs' : List CerbMem.AbsByte) (ty' : ctype),
  CerberusHeapLang.triple
    iprop(x ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; bs ∗ y ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} ty' ; bs')
    (CerberusHeapLang.storeExpr loc ann CerberusHeapLang.intTy x CerberusHeapLang.sevenVal mo)
    fun x_1 =>
    iprop(x ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; CerberusHeapLang.sevenBytes ∗
        y ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} ty' ; bs')
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.exhibitA_engine :
∀ (n : Nat) (aids : Nat → Nat),
  n ≤ 999998 →
    (∀ (r : kill_reason mem_error),
        CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread CerberusHeapLang.progA)
            CerberusHeapLang.σ₀ ≠
          CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.killed r) ∧
      CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread CerberusHeapLang.progA)
            CerberusHeapLang.σ₀ ≠
          CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.stuck ∧
        ∀ (v : value) (σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
          CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread CerberusHeapLang.progA)
                CerberusHeapLang.σ₀ =
              CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ' →
            v = CerberusHeapLang.sevenVal
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.exhibitA_prod :
∀ (fs : CerbFS.FsState) (args : List String),
  ∃ dres dst',
    CerbND.runND (drive fmapEmpty false (CerberusHeapLang.prodFile CerberusHeapLang.progAProd) args)
          (initial_driver_state (CerberusHeapLang.prodFile CerberusHeapLang.progAProd) fs) =
        [(Active dres, [], dst')] ∧
      dres.dres_core_value = CerberusHeapLang.sevenVal ∧
        CerbMem.readBytesFrom dst'.layout_state CerberusHeapLang.pxAddr 4 =
          (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] CerberusHeapLang.sevenMval).snd
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.exhibitA_semantic :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF],
  CerberusHeapLang.SemTriple CerberusHeapLang.progA CerberusHeapLang.mA fun v Q =>
    v = CerberusHeapLang.sevenVal ∧ Q = CerberusHeapLang.mA7
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.exhibitA_terminates :
∀ (aids : Nat → Nat),
  CerberusHeapLang.drive aids 6 (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread CerberusHeapLang.progA)
      CerberusHeapLang.σ₀ =
    CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done CerberusHeapLang.sevenVal CerberusHeapLang.σ₁
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.exhibitB_engine :
∀ (n : Nat) (aids : Nat → Nat),
  n ≤ 999999 →
    (∀ (r : kill_reason mem_error),
        CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread CerberusHeapLang.progB)
            CerberusHeapLang.σ₀ ≠
          CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.killed r) ∧
      CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread CerberusHeapLang.progB)
            CerberusHeapLang.σ₀ ≠
          CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.stuck ∧
        ∀ (v : value) (σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
          CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread CerberusHeapLang.progB)
                CerberusHeapLang.σ₀ =
              CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ' →
            v = Vunit ∧
              CerbMem.readBytesFrom σ' CerberusHeapLang.xAddr 4 =
                  (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] CerberusHeapLang.sevenMval).snd ∧
                CerbMem.readBytesFrom σ' CerberusHeapLang.yAddr 4 = CerberusHeapLang.bytesY
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.exhibitB_semantic :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF],
  CerberusHeapLang.SemTriple CerberusHeapLang.progB
    (Iris.Std.PartialMap.union CerberusHeapLang.mA CerberusHeapLang.mF) fun v Q =>
    ∃ Q₀,
      (v = Vunit ∧ Q₀ = CerberusHeapLang.mA7) ∧
        Iris.Std.PartialMap.disjoint Q₀ CerberusHeapLang.mF ∧
          Q = Iris.Std.PartialMap.union Q₀ CerberusHeapLang.mF
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.exhibitC_engine :
∀ (n : Nat) (aids : Nat → Nat),
  n ≤ 999998 →
    (∀ (r : kill_reason mem_error),
        CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread CerberusHeapLang.progC)
            CerberusHeapLang.σ₀ ≠
          CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.killed r) ∧
      CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread CerberusHeapLang.progC)
            CerberusHeapLang.σ₀ ≠
          CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.stuck ∧
        ∀ (v : value) (σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
          CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread CerberusHeapLang.progC)
                CerberusHeapLang.σ₀ =
              CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ' →
            CerbMem.readBytesFrom σ' CerberusHeapLang.xAddr 4 = CerberusHeapLang.fiveBytes ∧
              CerbMem.readBytesFrom σ' CerberusHeapLang.yAddr 4 = CerberusHeapLang.sixBytes
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.exhibitC_semantic :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF],
  CerberusHeapLang.SemTriple CerberusHeapLang.progC CerberusHeapLang.mB fun x Q =>
    Q = CerberusHeapLang.mC
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.exhibitC_triple :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (x y : CerbMem.PointerValue) (loc loc' : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann ann' : core_run_annotation)
  (mo mo' : memory_order) (bty : core_base_type) (bsx bsy : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  CerberusHeapLang.triple
    iprop(x ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; bsx ∗
        y ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; bsy)
    (CerberusHeapLang.sseqExpr bty
      (CerberusHeapLang.storeExpr loc ann CerberusHeapLang.intTy x CerberusHeapLang.fiveVal mo)
      (CerberusHeapLang.storeExpr loc' ann' CerberusHeapLang.intTy y CerberusHeapLang.sixVal mo'))
    fun x_1 =>
    iprop(x ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; CerberusHeapLang.fiveBytes ∗
        y ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; CerberusHeapLang.sixBytes)

theorem CerberusHeapLang.fibQ_inv :
∀ (ra : core_run_annotation) (n : Int) (ibty abty bbty : core_base_type) {l : sym}
  {params : List (sym × core_base_type)} {cont : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel (CerberusHeapLang.fibQ ra n ibty abty bbty) l = some (params, cont) →
    params =
        [(CerberusHeapLang.fibISym, ibty), (CerberusHeapLang.fibASym, abty),
          (CerberusHeapLang.fibBSym, bbty)] ∧
      cont = CerberusHeapLang.fibBody ra n

∀ (ra : core_run_annotation) (n : Int) (ibty abty bbty : core_base_type),
  CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel (CerberusHeapLang.fibQ ra n ibty abty bbty)
      CerberusHeapLang.fibLoopSym =
    some
      ([(CerberusHeapLang.fibISym, ibty), (CerberusHeapLang.fibASym, abty),
          (CerberusHeapLang.fibBSym, bbty)],
        CerberusHeapLang.fibBody ra n)

    CerberusHeapLang.fibProcSym (CerberusHeapLang.fibQ ra n ibty abty bbty)

def CerberusHeapLang.fibSpec :
Nat → Int
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.fibSpec_add_two :
∀ (n : Nat),
  CerberusHeapLang.fibSpec (n + 2) = CerberusHeapLang.fibSpec n + CerberusHeapLang.fibSpec (n + 1)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.fibSpec_one :
CerberusHeapLang.fibSpec 1 = 1
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.fibSpec_zero :
CerberusHeapLang.fibSpec 0 = 0
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.fib_args_eval :
∀ {f : Fmap sym value},
  CerberusHeapLang.SymFrame f →
    ∀ (i a b : Int) (rest : List (Fmap sym value)),
      CerberusHeapLang.evalPexprs
          (CerberusHeapLang.fibFrame (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal i) (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal a)
              (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal b) f ::
            rest)
          [CerberusHeapLang.fibIncPe, CerberusHeapLang.fibBPe, CerberusHeapLang.fibABPe] =
        some
          [CerberusHeapLang.ivVal (i + 1), CerberusHeapLang.ivVal b, CerberusHeapLang.ivVal (a + b)]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.fib_blockSpecs :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (ra : core_run_annotation) (n : Int) (ibty abty bbty : core_base_type),
  ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.blockSpecs (CerberusHeapLang.fibQ ra n ibty abty bbty)
      (CerberusHeapLang.fibLs n) (CerberusHeapLang.fibPost n)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.fib_body_wps :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (ra : core_run_annotation) (n : Int) (ibty abty bbty : core_base_type) (i : Int)
  (f : Fmap sym value) (rest : List (Fmap sym value)),
  CerberusHeapLang.SymFrame f →
    0 ≤ i →
      i ≤ n →
        ⊢
          CerberusHeapLang.wps (CerberusHeapLang.fibQ ra n ibty abty bbty)
            (CerberusHeapLang.fibLs n) (CerberusHeapLang.fibPost n) (CerberusHeapLang.fibBody ra n)
            (CerberusHeapLang.fibFrame (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal i)
                (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal (CerberusHeapLang.fibSpec i.toNat))
                (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal (CerberusHeapLang.fibSpec (i.toNat + 1))) f ::
              rest)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.fib_certified :
∀ (ra : core_run_annotation) (ibty abty bbty : core_base_type) {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors}
  [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF] (sbty : core_base_type) (n : Int),
  0 ≤ n →
    ∀ (σ₀ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (nsteps : Nat) (aids : Nat → Nat),
      3 + nsteps ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
        2 + nsteps ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
          have prog := CerberusHeapLang.fibProg ra n sbty ibty abty bbty;
          have rs := CerberusHeapLang.fibRS ra n ibty abty bbty;
          (∀ (r : kill_reason mem_error),
              CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids nsteps
                  (CerberusHeapLang.procThread CerberusHeapLang.fibProcSym prog [fmapEmpty]) σ₀ ≠
                CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.killed r) ∧
            CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids nsteps
                  (CerberusHeapLang.procThread CerberusHeapLang.fibProcSym prog [fmapEmpty]) σ₀ ≠
                CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.stuck ∧
              ∀ (v : value) (σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
                CerberusHeapLang.driveJ rs aids nsteps
                      (CerberusHeapLang.procThread CerberusHeapLang.fibProcSym prog [fmapEmpty])
                      σ₀ =
                    CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ' →
                  v = CerberusHeapLang.ivVal (CerberusHeapLang.fibSpec n.toNat)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.fib_certified_total :
∀ (ra : core_run_annotation) (ibty abty bbty sbty : core_base_type) (n : Int),
  0 ≤ n →
    ∀ (σ₀ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (aids : Nat → Nat),
      CerberusHeapLang.driveJ (CerberusHeapLang.fibRS ra n ibty abty bbty) aids (2 * n.toNat + 4)
          (CerberusHeapLang.procThread CerberusHeapLang.fibProcSym
            (CerberusHeapLang.fibProg ra n sbty ibty abty bbty) [fmapEmpty])
          σ₀ =
        CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done
          (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal (CerberusHeapLang.fibSpec n.toNat)) σ₀
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.fib_exit_eval :
∀ {f : Fmap sym value},
  CerberusHeapLang.SymFrame f →
    ∀ (i a b : Int) (rest : List (Fmap sym value)),
      CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr
          (CerberusHeapLang.fibFrame (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal i) (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal a)
              (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal b) f ::
            rest)
          CerberusHeapLang.fibExitPe =
        some (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal a)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.fib_guard_eval :
∀ {f : Fmap sym value},
  CerberusHeapLang.SymFrame f →
    ∀ (i a b : Int) (rest : List (Fmap sym value)) (n : Int),
      CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr
          (CerberusHeapLang.fibFrame (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal i) (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal a)
              (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal b) f ::
            rest)
          (CerberusHeapLang.fibGuard n) =
        some (CerberusHeapLang.boolValue (decide (i < n)))
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.fib_labeledAt_production :
∀ (ra : core_run_annotation) (n : Int) (sbty ibty abty bbty : core_base_type),
  CerberusHeapLang.LabeledAt
    (initial_core_run_state
      (collect_labeled_continuations_NEW
        (CerberusHeapLang.prodFile (CerberusHeapLang.fibProg ra n sbty ibty abty bbty))))
    CerberusHeapLang.mainSym (CerberusHeapLang.fibQ ra n ibty abty bbty)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.fib_loop_drive :
∀ (ra : core_run_annotation) (ibty abty bbty : core_base_type) (n i : Int),
  0 ≤ i →
    i ≤ n →
      ∀ (f : Fmap sym value),
        CerberusHeapLang.SymFrame f →
          ∀ (rest : List (Fmap sym value)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (aids : Nat → Nat),
            CerberusHeapLang.driveJ (CerberusHeapLang.fibRS ra n ibty abty bbty) aids
                (2 * (n - i).toNat + 3)
                (CerberusHeapLang.procThread CerberusHeapLang.fibProcSym
                  (CerberusHeapLang.fibBody ra n)
                  (CerberusHeapLang.fibFrame (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal i)
                      (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal (CerberusHeapLang.fibSpec i.toNat))
                      (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal (CerberusHeapLang.fibSpec (i.toNat + 1))) f ::
                    rest))
                σ =
              CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done
                (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal (CerberusHeapLang.fibSpec n.toNat)) σ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.fib_wp_readout :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (ra : core_run_annotation) (n : Int) (ibty abty bbty : core_base_type),
  0 ≤ n →
    ∀ (sbty : core_base_type),
      ⊢
        WP
          { e := CerberusHeapLang.fibProg ra n sbty ibty abty bbty, ρ := [fmapEmpty],
            lbl := CerberusHeapLang.fibQ ra n ibty abty bbty } @
          Iris.Stuckness.NotStuck ;
          ⊤ {{
            w,
              ∀ σ' ns κs nt,
                Iris.stateInterp σ' ns κs nt ={⊤,∅}=∗
                  ⌜w.val = CerberusHeapLang.ivVal (CerberusHeapLang.fibSpec n.toNat)⌝ }}
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.fib_wps :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (ra : core_run_annotation) (n : Int) (ibty abty bbty : core_base_type),
  0 ≤ n →
    ∀ (sbty : core_base_type),
      ⊢
        CerberusHeapLang.wps (CerberusHeapLang.fibQ ra n ibty abty bbty) (CerberusHeapLang.fibLs n)
          (CerberusHeapLang.fibPost n) (CerberusHeapLang.fibProg ra n sbty ibty abty bbty)
          [fmapEmpty]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.finalize_done :
∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (s : String) (dst : driver_state)
  (th : thread_state) (v : value),
  dst.core_state0.thread_states = [(0, none, th)] →
    th.arena = mk_value_e v →
      finalize tds s dst =
        { dres_blocked := dst.blocked, dres_concurrency_state := dst.concurrency_state,
          dres_driver_steps := dst.dr_step_counter, dres_core_value := v,
          dres_stdout := List.foldr String.append "" (toList dst.core_state0.io.stdout),
          dres_stderr := List.foldr String.append "" (toList dst.core_state0.io.stderr) }

theorem CerberusHeapLang.fmapLookupBy_addBy_empty :
∀ {β : Type} (cmpL cmpL' : sym → sym → LemOrdering) (k : sym) (v : β) (l : sym),
  fmapLookupBy cmpL' l (fmapAddBy cmpL k v fmapEmpty) =
    if lemCmpToOrd cmpL l k = Ordering.eq then some v else none
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.foldM_args_bridge :
∀ {th : thread_state} {tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)}
  {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation}
  (f :
    CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack →
      (sym × core_base_type) × generic_pexpr Unit sym →
        core_run_state → exceptM (t0 CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack × core_run_state) core_run_cause),
  (∀ (acc : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (s : sym) (bTy : core_base_type)
      (pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym) (rs' : core_run_state),
      f acc ((s, bTy), pe) rs' =
        stExceptUndef_bind (full_eval_pexpr tds th fmapEmpty σ file pe)
          (fun cval => stExceptUndef_return (update_env (mk_sym_pat s bTy) cval acc)) rs') →
    ∀ (params : List (sym × core_base_type)) (pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym)) (vs : List value)
      (acc : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (rs : core_run_state),
      CerberusHeapLang.evalPexprs th.env pes = some vs →
        (∀ (pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym), pe ∈ pes → CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe ≤ lemDefaultFuel) →
          stExceptUndef_foldM f acc (params.zip pes) rs =
            Result (Defined (CerberusHeapLang.bindArgs params vs acc), rs)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.foldl_update_env_cons :
∀ {α : Type} (f : α → pattern) (g : α → value) (xs : List α) (ev0 : Fmap sym value)
  (evs : List (Fmap sym value)),
  ∃ ev0', List.foldl (fun acc a => update_env (f a) (g a) acc) (ev0 :: evs) xs = ev0' :: evs

def CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? :
CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → Option (sym × List (generic_pexpr Unit sym))
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex?.eq_def :
∀ (x : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
  CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? x =
    match x with
    | generic_expr.Expr a (Erun a_1 l pes) => some (l, pes)
    | generic_expr.Expr a (Esseq a_1 e1 a_2) => CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? e1
    | generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot a_1 b) =>
      if CerberusHeapLang.annotRooted b = true then none else CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? b
    | x => none
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex?_action :
∀ (a : List annot) (p : generic_paction core_run_annotation Unit sym),
  CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? (generic_expr.Expr a (Eaction p)) = none
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex?_annot :
∀ (a : List annot) (ds : List dyn_annotation) (b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
  CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? (generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot ds b)) =
    if CerberusHeapLang.annotRooted b = true then none else CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? b
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex?_annot_of_not_root :
∀ {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : List annot) (ds : List dyn_annotation),
  CerberusHeapLang.annotRooted b = false →
    CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? (generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot ds b)) = CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? b
----

∀ {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (a : List annot) (ds : List dyn_annotation),
  CerberusHeapLang.annotRooted b = true →
    CerberusHeapLang.jumpRedex? (generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot ds b)) = none

def CerberusHeapLang.loadExpr :
CerbLocation.Loc →
  core_run_annotation → ctype → CerbMem.PointerValue → memory_order → CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.loadM_interior_int :
∀ (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (id : Int) (c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell) (off : Nat)
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc),
  CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c →
    off + CerbMem.sizeofCtype CerberusHeapLang.intTy ≤ CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty →
      CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM
          (CerbMem.loadM loc CerberusHeapLang.intTy (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id (c.addr + ↑off)))
          σ =
        some
          ((CerbMem.Footprint.FP CerbMem.FootprintAccess.R (c.addr + ↑off)
                ↑(CerbMem.sizeofCtype CerberusHeapLang.intTy),
              CerbMem.reconstructValue σ.lastUsedUnionMembers σ.funptrmap (c.addr + ↑off)
                CerberusHeapLang.intTy
                (List.take (CerbMem.sizeofCtype CerberusHeapLang.intTy) (List.drop off c.bytes))),
            σ)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.loadM_success :
∀ (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (id : Int) (c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell) (loc : CerbLocation.Loc),
  CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c →
    CerberusHeapLang.cellLoadTrap c = false →
      CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM (CerbMem.loadM loc c.ty (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id c.addr)) σ =
        some
          ((CerbMem.Footprint.FP CerbMem.FootprintAccess.R c.addr ↑(CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty),
              CerberusHeapLang.decodeCell c),
            σ)
----

theorem CerberusHeapLang.lookup_env_head :
∀ {x : sym} {f : Fmap sym value} {v : value},
  fmapLookupBy CerberusHeapLang.symCmpK x f = some v →
    ∀ (rest : List (Fmap sym value)), lookup_env x (f :: rest) = some v
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.lookup_env_xframe :
∀ {f : Fmap sym value} {v : value},
  CerberusHeapLang.IsXFrame f v →
    ∀ (rest : List (Fmap sym value)), lookup_env CerberusHeapLang.xSym (f :: rest) = some v
----
def CerberusHeapLang.loopBody :
CerbLocation.Loc →
  core_run_annotation →
    core_run_annotation →
      memory_order → core_base_type → CerbMem.PointerValue → CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.loopBody_fragJ :
∀ (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann ra : core_run_annotation) (mo : memory_order) (bty : core_base_type)
  (c : CerbMem.PointerValue),
  CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false →
    CerberusHeapLang.FragJ (CerberusHeapLang.loopBody loc ann ra mo bty c)
----
def CerberusHeapLang.loopLs :
{hlc : Iris.HasLC} →
  {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
    [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF] →
      CerbMem.PointerValue → Int → List CerbMem.AbsByte → CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF
----
def CerberusHeapLang.loopPost :
{hlc : Iris.HasLC} →
  {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
    [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF] →
      CerbMem.PointerValue →
        Int →
          List CerbMem.AbsByte →
            CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF
----
def CerberusHeapLang.loopProcSym :
sym
----
def CerberusHeapLang.loopProg :
CerbLocation.Loc →
  core_run_annotation →
    core_run_annotation →
      memory_order →
        core_base_type →
          core_base_type → core_base_type → CerbMem.PointerValue → Int → CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr
----
def CerberusHeapLang.loopQ :
CerbLocation.Loc →
  core_run_annotation →
    core_run_annotation →
      memory_order →
        core_base_type → core_base_type → CerbMem.PointerValue → CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.loopQ_inv :
∀ (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann ra : core_run_annotation) (mo : memory_order)
  (bty xbty : core_base_type) (c : CerbMem.PointerValue) {l : sym}
  {params : List (sym × core_base_type)} {cont : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel (CerberusHeapLang.loopQ loc ann ra mo bty xbty c) l =
      some (params, cont) →
    params = [(CerberusHeapLang.xSym, xbty)] ∧ cont = CerberusHeapLang.loopBody loc ann ra mo bty c
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.loopQ_lookup :
∀ (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann ra : core_run_annotation) (mo : memory_order)
  (bty xbty : core_base_type) (c : CerbMem.PointerValue),
  CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel (CerberusHeapLang.loopQ loc ann ra mo bty xbty c)
      CerberusHeapLang.loopSym =
    some ([(CerberusHeapLang.xSym, xbty)], CerberusHeapLang.loopBody loc ann ra mo bty c)
----
def CerberusHeapLang.loopRS :
CerbLocation.Loc →
  core_run_annotation →
    core_run_annotation →
      memory_order → core_base_type → core_base_type → CerbMem.PointerValue → core_run_state
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.loopRS_labeledAt :
∀ (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann ra : core_run_annotation) (mo : memory_order)
  (bty xbty : core_base_type) (c : CerbMem.PointerValue),
  CerberusHeapLang.LabeledAt (CerberusHeapLang.loopRS loc ann ra mo bty xbty c)
    CerberusHeapLang.loopProcSym (CerberusHeapLang.loopQ loc ann ra mo bty xbty c)
----
def CerberusHeapLang.loopSym :
sym
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.loop_blockSpecs :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann ra : core_run_annotation) (mo : memory_order)
  (bty xbty : core_base_type) (c : CerbMem.PointerValue) (n : Int) (bs0 : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.blockSpecs (CerberusHeapLang.loopQ loc ann ra mo bty xbty c)
      (CerberusHeapLang.loopLs c n bs0) (CerberusHeapLang.loopPost c n bs0)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.loop_body_wps :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann ra : core_run_annotation) (mo : memory_order)
  (bty xbty : core_base_type) (c : CerbMem.PointerValue) (n : Int) (bs0 : List CerbMem.AbsByte)
  (i : Int) (f : Fmap sym value) (rest : List (Fmap sym value)),
  CerberusHeapLang.XReady f →
    0 ≤ i →
      i ≤ n →
        ⌜i = n⌝ ∗ c ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; bs0 ∨
            ⌜i < n⌝ ∗ c ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; CerberusHeapLang.sevenBytes ⊢
          CerberusHeapLang.wps (CerberusHeapLang.loopQ loc ann ra mo bty xbty c)
            (CerberusHeapLang.loopLs c n bs0) (CerberusHeapLang.loopPost c n bs0)
            (CerberusHeapLang.loopBody loc ann ra mo bty c)
            (CerberusHeapLang.envAdd CerberusHeapLang.xSym (CerberusHeapLang.ivVal i) f :: rest)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.loop_labeledAt_production :
∀ (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann ra : core_run_annotation) (mo : memory_order)
  (bty xbty sbty : core_base_type) (c : CerbMem.PointerValue) (n : Int),
  CerberusHeapLang.LabeledAt
    (initial_core_run_state
      (collect_labeled_continuations_NEW
        (CerberusHeapLang.prodFile (CerberusHeapLang.loopProg loc ann ra mo bty xbty sbty c n))))
    CerberusHeapLang.mainSym (CerberusHeapLang.loopQ loc ann ra mo bty xbty c)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.loop_readout_val :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (c : CerbMem.PointerValue) (n : Int) (bs0 : List CerbMem.AbsByte) (w : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal),
  CerberusHeapLang.loopPost c n bs0 w.w w.ρ ⊢
    ∀ σ' ns κs nt,
      Iris.stateInterp σ' ns κs nt ={⊤,∅}=∗
        ⌜w.val = Vunit ∧
            ∃ bs',
              (n = 0 ∧ bs' = bs0 ∨ 0 < n ∧ bs' = CerberusHeapLang.sevenBytes) ∧
                ∃ i a,
                  c = CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr i a ∧
                    CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ' i
                      { addr := a, ty := CerberusHeapLang.intTy, bytes := bs' }⌝
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.loop_step_action :
∀ (fl : Nat) (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition))
  (acc : Fmap thread_id (List core_step2)) {dst dst' : driver_state} {th : thread_state}
  {s : String} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc} {req : action_request2 thread_state},
  dst.core_state0.thread_states = [(0, none, th)] →
    step_ctx tds dst.layout_state dst.core_file dst.core_extern 0 (none, th) =
        [Step_action_request2 s loc 0 false (stExceptUndef_return req)] →
      CerberusHeapLang.runOne (action_request_sequential2 loc 0 dst.core_run_state0.aid_supply req)
            { core_file := dst.core_file, core_extern := dst.core_extern,
              core_state0 := dst.core_state0,
              core_run_state0 :=
                have __src := dst.core_run_state0;
                { tid_supply := __src.tid_supply, aid_supply := dst.core_run_state0.aid_supply + 1,
                  excluded_supply := __src.excluded_supply, sym_supply := __src.sym_supply,
                  labeled := __src.labeled },
              layout_state := dst.layout_state, concurrency_state := dst.concurrency_state,
              fs_state0 := dst.fs_state0, trace := dst.trace, symbolic_assoc := dst.symbolic_assoc,
              blocked := dst.blocked, dr_step_counter := dst.dr_step_counter } =
          (NDactive (), dst') →
        CerberusHeapLang.runOne (drive_nonmemory_steps_aux2_lemFuel fl.succ tds acc [0]) dst =
          CerberusHeapLang.runOne (drive_nonmemory_steps_aux2_lemFuel fl tds acc [0]) dst'
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.loop_step_done :
∀ (fl : Nat) (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition))
  (acc : Fmap thread_id (List core_step2)) {dst : driver_state} {th : thread_state} {v : value},
  dst.core_state0.thread_states = [(0, none, th)] →
    step_ctx tds dst.layout_state dst.core_file dst.core_extern 0 (none, th) = [Step_done2 v] →
      CerberusHeapLang.runOne (drive_nonmemory_steps_aux2_lemFuel fl.succ.succ tds acc [0]) dst =
        (NDactive (fmapAddBy defaultCompare 0 [Step_done2 v] acc), dst)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.loop_step_tau :
∀ (fl : Nat) (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition))
  (acc : Fmap thread_id (List core_step2)) {dst : driver_state} {th th' : thread_state}
  {s : String},
  dst.core_state0.thread_states = [(0, none, th)] →
    step_ctx tds dst.layout_state dst.core_file dst.core_extern 0 (none, th) =
        [Step_tau2 s TSK_Misc th'] →
      CerberusHeapLang.runOne (drive_nonmemory_steps_aux2_lemFuel fl.succ tds acc [0]) dst =
        CerberusHeapLang.runOne (drive_nonmemory_steps_aux2_lemFuel fl tds acc [0])
          (have __src :=
            { core_file := dst.core_file, core_extern := dst.core_extern,
              core_state0 := dst.core_state0, core_run_state0 := dst.core_run_state0,
              layout_state := dst.layout_state, concurrency_state := dst.concurrency_state,
              fs_state0 := dst.fs_state0, trace := dst.trace, symbolic_assoc := dst.symbolic_assoc,
              blocked := dst.blocked, dr_step_counter := dst.dr_step_counter + 1 };
          { core_file := __src.core_file, core_extern := __src.core_extern,
            core_state0 := update_thread_state 0 th' dst.core_state0,
            core_run_state0 := __src.core_run_state0, layout_state := __src.layout_state,
            concurrency_state := __src.concurrency_state, fs_state0 := __src.fs_state0,
            trace := __src.trace, symbolic_assoc := __src.symbolic_assoc, blocked := __src.blocked,
            dr_step_counter := __src.dr_step_counter })
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.loop_wp_readout :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann ra : core_run_annotation) (mo : memory_order)
  (bty xbty : core_base_type) (c : CerbMem.PointerValue) (n : Int) (bs0 : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  0 ≤ n →
    ∀ (sbty : core_base_type),
      c ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; bs0 ⊢
        WP
          { e := CerberusHeapLang.loopProg loc ann ra mo bty xbty sbty c n, ρ := [fmapEmpty],
            lbl := CerberusHeapLang.loopQ loc ann ra mo bty xbty c } @
          Iris.Stuckness.NotStuck ;
          ⊤ {{
            w,
              ∀ σ' ns κs nt,
                Iris.stateInterp σ' ns κs nt ={⊤,∅}=∗
                  ⌜w.val = Vunit ∧
                      ∃ bs',
                        (n = 0 ∧ bs' = bs0 ∨ 0 < n ∧ bs' = CerberusHeapLang.sevenBytes) ∧
                          ∃ i a,
                            c = CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr i a ∧
                              CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ' i
                                { addr := a, ty := CerberusHeapLang.intTy, bytes := bs' }⌝ }}
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.loop_wps :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann ra : core_run_annotation) (mo : memory_order)
  (bty xbty : core_base_type) (c : CerbMem.PointerValue) (n : Int) (bs0 : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  0 ≤ n →
    ∀ (sbty : core_base_type),
      c ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; bs0 ⊢
        CerberusHeapLang.wps (CerberusHeapLang.loopQ loc ann ra mo bty xbty c)
          (CerberusHeapLang.loopLs c n bs0) (CerberusHeapLang.loopPost c n bs0)
          (CerberusHeapLang.loopProg loc ann ra mo bty xbty sbty c n) [fmapEmpty]
----

def CerberusHeapLang.peDepth :
generic_pexpr Unit sym → Nat
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.peDepth.eq_def :
∀ (x : generic_pexpr Unit sym),
  CerberusHeapLang.peDepth x =
    match x with
    | Pexpr a a_1 (PEop a_2 pe1 pe2) =>
      1 + max (CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe1) (CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe2)
    | Pexpr a a_1 (PEarray_shift pe1 a_2 pe2) =>
      1 + max (CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe1) (CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe2)
    | x => 1
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.peDepth_array_shift :
∀ (a : List annot) (ty : ctype) (pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym),
  CerberusHeapLang.peDepth (Pexpr a () (PEarray_shift pe1 ty pe2)) =
    1 + max (CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe1) (CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe2)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.peDepth_op :
∀ (a : List annot) (op : binop) (pe1 pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym),
  CerberusHeapLang.peDepth (Pexpr a () (PEop op pe1 pe2)) =
    1 + max (CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe1) (CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe2)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.peDepth_peStrip :
∀ {pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym},
  CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe →
    CerberusHeapLang.peDepth (CerberusHeapLang.peStrip pe) = CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.peDepth_pos :
∀ (pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym), 1 ≤ CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.peDepth_sym_le :
∀ (pb : List annot) (x : sym), CerberusHeapLang.peDepth (Pexpr pb () (PEsym x)) ≤ lemDefaultFuel
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.peDepth_val_le :
∀ (a : List annot) (v : value), CerberusHeapLang.peDepth (Pexpr a () (PEval v)) ≤ lemDefaultFuel
----
def CerberusHeapLang.peStrip :
generic_pexpr Unit sym → generic_pexpr Unit sym
----
def CerberusHeapLang.pointsToCell :
{hlc : Iris.HasLC} →
  {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
    [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF] →
      CerbMem.PointerValue → Iris.DFrac → ctype → List CerbMem.AbsByte → Iris.IProp GF
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.pointsToCell_iff :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (pv : CerbMem.PointerValue) (dq : Iris.DFrac) (ty : ctype) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  pv ↦c{dq} ty ; bs ⊣⊢
    ∃ i a, ⌜pv = CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr i a⌝ ∗ i ↦{dq} { addr := a, ty := ty, bytes := bs }
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.prepare_exit_single :
∀ (cs : core_state) (th : thread_state) (v : value),
  cs.thread_states = [(0, none, th)] →
    prepare_exit cs v =
      {
        thread_states :=
          [(0, none,
              { arena := mk_value_e v, stack0 := Stack_empty, errno := th.errno, env := th.env,
                current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt, exec_loc := th.exec_loc,
                current_loc := th.current_loc })],
        io := cs.io }
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.primStep_eq :
∀ (r : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (obs : List Empty)
  (r' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt) (σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (efs : List CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt),
  Iris.ProgramLogic.PrimStep.primStep (r, σ) obs (r', σ', efs) ↔
    CerberusHeapLang.Step r.lbl (r.e, r.ρ, σ) (r'.e, r'.ρ, σ') ∧ r'.lbl = r.lbl ∧ efs = []

theorem CerberusHeapLang.process_done :
∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition))
  (cont : Bool → ndM Unit step_kind driver_error (mem_constraint CerbMem.IntegerValue) driver_state)
  (v : value) (dst : driver_state) (th : thread_state),
  dst.core_state0.thread_states = [(0, none, th)] →
    CerberusHeapLang.runOne (process_core_step2 tds false cont (Step_done2 v)) dst =
      (NDactive (),
        { core_file := dst.core_file, core_extern := dst.core_extern,
          core_state0 :=
            have __src := dst.core_state0;
            {
              thread_states :=
                [(0, none,
                    { arena := mk_value_e v, stack0 := Stack_empty, errno := th.errno,
                      env := th.env, current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt,
                      exec_loc := th.exec_loc, current_loc := th.current_loc })],
              io := __src.io },
          core_run_state0 := dst.core_run_state0, layout_state := dst.layout_state,
          concurrency_state := dst.concurrency_state, fs_state0 := dst.fs_state0,
          trace := dst.trace, symbolic_assoc := dst.symbolic_assoc, blocked := dst.blocked,
          dr_step_counter := dst.dr_step_counter })
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.prodA_pre :
∀ (aids : Nat → Nat) (n : Nat),
  CerberusHeapLang.drive aids (2 + n) (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread CerberusHeapLang.progAProd)
      CerberusHeapLang.prodMem₀ =
    CerberusHeapLang.drive (fun i => aids (i + 2)) n
      (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread CerberusHeapLang.progAProdC) CerberusHeapLang.σcP
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.prodA_terminates :
∀ (aids : Nat → Nat),
  CerberusHeapLang.drive aids 6 (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread CerberusHeapLang.progAProdC)
      CerberusHeapLang.σcP =
    CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done CerberusHeapLang.sevenVal CerberusHeapLang.σ1P
----

----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.prod_loop_done :
∀ (th₀ : thread_state),
  th₀.stack0 = Stack_empty →
    ∀ {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)},
      th₀.env = ev0 :: evs →
        ∀ (v : value) (σfin : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (n fl : Nat) (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr)
          (dst : driver_state) (acc : Fmap thread_id (List core_step2)),
          CerberusHeapLang.FragP e →
            dst.core_state0.thread_states =
                [(0, none,
                    { arena := e, stack0 := th₀.stack0, errno := th₀.errno, env := th₀.env,
                      current_proc_opt := th₀.current_proc_opt, exec_loc := th₀.exec_loc,
                      current_loc := th₀.current_loc })] →
              CerberusHeapLang.esize e + n ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
                n + 2 ≤ fl →
                  (∀ (aids : Nat → Nat),
                      CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread e)
                          dst.layout_state =
                        CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σfin) →
                    ∃ rs' tr ctr,
                      CerberusHeapLang.runOne
                          (drive_nonmemory_steps_aux2_lemFuel fl fmapEmpty acc [0]) dst =
                        (NDactive (fmapAddBy defaultCompare 0 [Step_done2 v] acc),
                          { core_file := dst.core_file, core_extern := dst.core_extern,
                            core_state0 :=
                              have __src := dst.core_state0;
                              {
                                thread_states :=
                                  [(0, none,
                                      {
                                        arena :=
                                          CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v),
                                        stack0 := th₀.stack0, errno := th₀.errno, env := th₀.env,
                                        current_proc_opt := th₀.current_proc_opt,
                                        exec_loc := th₀.exec_loc,
                                        current_loc := th₀.current_loc })],
                                io := __src.io },
                            core_run_state0 := rs', layout_state := σfin,
                            concurrency_state := dst.concurrency_state, fs_state0 := dst.fs_state0,
                            trace := tr, symbolic_assoc := dst.symbolic_assoc,
                            blocked := dst.blocked, dr_step_counter := ctr })
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.prod_run_eq :
∀ (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
  CerberusHeapLang.FragP e →
    ∀ (v : value) (σfin : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (k : Nat),
      (∀ (aids : Nat → Nat),
          CerberusHeapLang.drive aids k (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread e) CerberusHeapLang.prodMem₀ =
            CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σfin) →
        CerberusHeapLang.esize e + k + 2 ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
          ∀ (fs : CerbFS.FsState) (args : List String),
            ∃ dres dst',
              CerbND.runND (drive fmapEmpty false (CerberusHeapLang.prodFile e) args)
                    (initial_driver_state (CerberusHeapLang.prodFile e) fs) =
                  [(Active dres, [], dst')] ∧
                dres.dres_core_value = v ∧
                  dres.dres_blocked = false ∧
                    dres.dres_stdout = "" ∧ dres.dres_stderr = "" ∧ dst'.layout_state = σfin
----

theorem CerberusHeapLang.readBytesFrom_sub :
∀ (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (a : Int) (m : Nat) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  CerbMem.readBytesFrom σ a m = bs →
    ∀ (off k : Nat),
      off + k ≤ m → CerbMem.readBytesFrom σ (a + ↑off) k = List.take k (List.drop off bs)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.readBytesFrom_writeBytesTo_disjoint :
∀ (st : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (a : Int) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte) (a' : Int) (n : Nat),
  a' + ↑n ≤ a ∨ a + ↑bs.length ≤ a' →
    CerbMem.readBytesFrom (CerbMem.writeBytesTo st a bs) a' n = CerbMem.readBytesFrom st a' n
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.readBytesFrom_writeBytesTo_self :
∀ (st : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (a : Int) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte),
  CerbMem.readBytesFrom (CerbMem.writeBytesTo st a bs) a bs.length = bs

          have __src := dst.core_run_state0;

theorem CerberusHeapLang.semAProd :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF],
  CerberusHeapLang.SemTriple CerberusHeapLang.progAProdC CerberusHeapLang.mAP fun v Q =>
    v = CerberusHeapLang.sevenVal ∧ Q = CerberusHeapLang.mAP7
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.sem_triple_prod :
∀ (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
  CerberusHeapLang.FragP e →
    ∀ (ec : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (P : CerberusHeapLang.CellMap)
      (post : value → CerberusHeapLang.CellMap → Prop),
      CerberusHeapLang.SemTriple ec P post →
        ∀ (R : CerberusHeapLang.CellMap),
          Iris.Std.PartialMap.disjoint P R →
            ∀ (σc : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (j : Nat),
              (∀ (aids : Nat → Nat) (n : Nat),
                  CerberusHeapLang.drive aids (j + n) (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread e)
                      CerberusHeapLang.prodMem₀ =
                    CerberusHeapLang.drive (fun i => aids (i + j)) n
                      (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread ec) σc) →
                CerberusHeapLang.Sat σc (Iris.Std.PartialMap.union P R) →
                  ∀ (v : value) (σfin : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (k : Nat),
                    (∀ (aids : Nat → Nat),
                        CerberusHeapLang.drive aids k (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread ec) σc =
                          CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σfin) →
                      CerberusHeapLang.esize e + (j + k) + 2 ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
                        CerberusHeapLang.esize ec + k ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
                          ∀ (fs : CerbFS.FsState) (args : List String),
                            ∃ dres dst',
                              CerbND.runND
                                    (drive fmapEmpty false (CerberusHeapLang.prodFile e) args)
                                    (initial_driver_state (CerberusHeapLang.prodFile e) fs) =
                                  [(Active dres, [], dst')] ∧
                                dres.dres_core_value = v ∧
                                  dst'.layout_state = σfin ∧
                                    ∃ Q,
                                      post v Q ∧
                                        Iris.Std.PartialMap.disjoint Q R ∧
                                          CerberusHeapLang.Sat σfin (Iris.Std.PartialMap.union Q R)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.semantic_frame :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF]
  {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.FragP e →
    ∀ {P : CerberusHeapLang.CellMap} (F : CerberusHeapLang.CellMap)
      {post : value → CerberusHeapLang.CellMap → Prop},
      Iris.Std.PartialMap.disjoint P F →
        CerberusHeapLang.ProvenTriple GF e P post →
          CerberusHeapLang.SemTriple e (Iris.Std.PartialMap.union P F) fun v Q =>
            ∃ Q₀, post v Q₀ ∧ Iris.Std.PartialMap.disjoint Q₀ F ∧ Q = Iris.Std.PartialMap.union Q₀ F
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.semantic_triple_sound :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF]
  {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.FragP e →
    ∀ {P : CerberusHeapLang.CellMap} {post : value → CerberusHeapLang.CellMap → Prop},
      CerberusHeapLang.ProvenTriple GF e P post → CerberusHeapLang.SemTriple e P post
----

theorem CerberusHeapLang.spike_engine_adequacy :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF] (e₀ : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr)
  (σ₀ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (m₀ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
  CerberusHeapLang.FragP e₀ →
    CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ₀ m₀ →
      ∀ (ψ : value → CerberusHeapLang.Mem → Prop),
        (∀ [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS Iris.HasLC.hasLC GF],
            ([∗map] i ↦ c ∈ m₀, i ↦{Iris.DFrac.own 1} c) ⊢
              WP { e := e₀, ρ := CerberusHeapLang.spikeEnv, lbl := CerberusHeapLang.spikeLbl } @
                Iris.Stuckness.NotStuck ;
                ⊤ {{ w, ∀ σ' ns κs nt, Iris.stateInterp σ' ns κs nt ={⊤,∅}=∗ ⌜ψ w.val σ'⌝ }} ) →
          ∀ (n : Nat) (aids : Nat → Nat),
            CerberusHeapLang.esize e₀ + n ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
              (∀ (r : kill_reason mem_error),
                  CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread e₀) σ₀ ≠
                    CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.killed r) ∧
                CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread e₀) σ₀ ≠
                    CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.stuck ∧
                  ∀ (v : value) (σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem),
                    CerberusHeapLang.drive aids n (CerberusHeapLang.spikeThread e₀) σ₀ =
                        CerberusHeapLang.DriveResult.done v σ' →
                      ψ v σ'
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.spike_step_adequacy :
∀ {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGpreS GF] (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt)
  (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (m₀ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeHeapF CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell),
  CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ m₀ →
    ∀ (φp : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal → CerberusHeapLang.Mem → Prop),
      (∀ [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS Iris.HasLC.hasLC GF],
          ([∗map] i ↦ c ∈ m₀, i ↦{Iris.DFrac.own 1} c) ⊢
            WP e @ Iris.Stuckness.NotStuck ;
              ⊤ {{ v, ∀ σ' ns κs nt, Iris.stateInterp σ' ns κs nt ={⊤,∅}=∗ ⌜φp v σ'⌝ }} ) →
        ∀ {t2 : List CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt} {σ2 : CerberusHeapLang.Mem},
          FromMathlib.Relation.ReflTransGen Iris.ProgramLogic.Language.ErasedStep ([e], σ)
              (t2, σ2) →
            (∀ (e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt),
                e2 ∈ t2 → Iris.ProgramLogic.PrimStep.NotStuck (e2, σ2)) ∧
              ∀ (w : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal) (t2' : List CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt),
                t2 = CerberusHeapLang.ofValRt w :: t2' → φp w σ2
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.spike_wp_wand :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {s : Iris.Stuckness} {E : CoPset} {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt}
  {Φ Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal → Iris.IProp GF},
  WP e @ s ; E {{ Φ }} ⊢ (∀ v, Φ v -∗ Ψ v) -∗ WP e @ s ; E {{ Ψ }}
----
def CerberusHeapLang.sseqExpr :
core_base_type → CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.sseq_annot_det :
∀ {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {a pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type}
  {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value} {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ev0 : Fmap sym value}
  {evs : List (Fmap sym value)} {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {obs : List Empty}
  {r' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt} {σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {eₜ : List CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt},
  Iris.ProgramLogic.PrimStep.primStep
      ({
          e :=
            generic_expr.Expr a
              (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty)))
                (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v)) e2),
          ρ := ev0 :: evs, lbl := Q },
        σ)
      obs (r', σ', eₜ) →
    obs = [] ∧
      σ' = σ ∧
        r' = { e := generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds e2), ρ := ev0 :: evs, lbl := Q } ∧ eₜ = []
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.sseq_pure_det :
∀ {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {a pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type} {v : value}
  {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)}
  {σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {obs : List Empty} {r' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt}
  {σ' : CerberusHeapLang.Mem} {eₜ : List CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt},
  Iris.ProgramLogic.PrimStep.primStep
      ({
          e :=
            generic_expr.Expr a
              (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty)))
                (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v)) e2),
          ρ := ev0 :: evs, lbl := Q },
        σ)
      obs (r', σ', eₜ) →
    obs = [] ∧ σ' = σ ∧ r' = { e := e2, ρ := ev0 :: evs, lbl := Q } ∧ eₜ = []
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.stExceptUndef_bind_apply :
∀ {a b c d e : Type} (m : e → exceptM (t0 d × a) c) (f : d → a → exceptM (t0 b × a) c) (st : e),
  stExceptUndef_bind m f st =
    match m st with
    | Result (Defined z, st') => f z st'
    | Result (Undef loc1 ubs, st') => stExpect_return (undef loc1 ubs) st'
    | Result (Error loc1 str, st') => stExpect_return (error0 loc1 str) st'
    | exceptM.Exception err => fail0 err
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.stExceptUndef_foldM_cons :
∀ {a b c e : Type} (f : a → e → c → exceptM (t0 a × c) b) (acc : a) (x : e) (xs : List e),
  stExceptUndef_foldM f acc (x :: xs) =
    stExceptUndef_bind (f acc x) fun z => stExceptUndef_foldM f z xs
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.stExceptUndef_return_apply :
∀ {a b c : Type} (z : a) (st : b), stExceptUndef_return z st = Result (Defined z, st)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.stateInterp_eq :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (ns : Nat) (κs : List Empty) (nt : Nat),
  Iris.stateInterp σ ns κs nt = iprop(∃ m, ⌜CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ m⌝ ∗ Iris.genHeapInterp m)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.stateInterp_iff :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (ns : Nat) (κs : List Empty) (nt : Nat),
  Iris.stateInterp σ ns κs nt ⊣⊢ ∃ m, ⌜CerberusHeapLang.Coh σ m⌝ ∗ Iris.genHeapInterp m
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.stepDischarge_if_false :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {g : generic_pexpr Unit sym}
  {e2 e3 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ e ctx (CerberusHeapLang.ifRedex g e2 e3) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      CerberusHeapLang.peDepth g ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
        ∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem)
          (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation) (tid : Nat) (parent : Option Nat)
          (th : thread_state),
          th.arena = e →
            CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr th.env g = some Vfalse →
              ∀ (aid : Nat) (rs : core_run_state),
                List.map (CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep aid rs σ)
                    (step_ctx tds σ file fmapEmpty tid (parent, th)) =
                  [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next
                      { arena := apply_ctx ctx e3, stack0 := th.stack0, errno := th.errno,
                        env := th.env, current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt,
                        exec_loc := th.exec_loc, current_loc := th.current_loc }
                      σ]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.stepDischarge_if_true :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {g : generic_pexpr Unit sym}
  {e2 e3 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ e ctx (CerberusHeapLang.ifRedex g e2 e3) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      CerberusHeapLang.peDepth g ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
        ∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem)
          (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation) (tid : Nat) (parent : Option Nat)
          (th : thread_state),
          th.arena = e →
            CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr th.env g = some Vtrue →
              ∀ (aid : Nat) (rs : core_run_state),
                List.map (CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep aid rs σ)
                    (step_ctx tds σ file fmapEmpty tid (parent, th)) =
                  [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next
                      { arena := apply_ctx ctx e2, stack0 := th.stack0, errno := th.errno,
                        env := th.env, current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt,
                        exec_loc := th.exec_loc, current_loc := th.current_loc }
                      σ]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.stepDischarge_load_eval :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc}
  {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype} {pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {mo : memory_order}
  {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue},
  CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ e ctx (CerberusHeapLang.loadOpRedex loc ann ty pe2 mo) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      valueFromPexpr pe2 = none →
        CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe2 →
          CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe2 ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
            ∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem)
              (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation) (tid : Nat) (parent : Option Nat)
              (th : thread_state),
              th.arena = e →
                CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr th.env pe2 = some (Vobject (OVpointer pv)) →
                  ∀ (aid : Nat) (rs : core_run_state),
                    List.map (CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep aid rs σ)
                        (step_ctx tds σ file fmapEmpty tid (parent, th)) =
                      [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next
                          { arena := apply_ctx ctx (CerberusHeapLang.loadRedex loc ann ty pv mo),
                            stack0 := th.stack0, errno := th.errno, env := th.env,
                            current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt, exec_loc := th.exec_loc,
                            current_loc := th.current_loc }
                          σ]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.stepDischarge_pure_sym :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {pb : List annot} {x : sym} {v : value},
  CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ e ctx (CerberusHeapLang.pureRedex (Pexpr pb () (PEsym x))) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      ∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem)
        (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation) (tid : Nat) (parent : Option Nat)
        (th : thread_state),
        th.arena = e →
          CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr th.env (Pexpr pb () (PEsym x)) = some v →
            ∀ (aid : Nat) (rs : core_run_state),
              List.map (CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep aid rs σ)
                  (step_ctx tds σ file fmapEmpty tid (parent, th)) =
                [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next
                    { arena := apply_ctx ctx (generic_expr.Expr [] (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval v)))),
                      stack0 := th.stack0, errno := th.errno, env := th.env,
                      current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt, exec_loc := th.exec_loc,
                      current_loc := th.current_loc }
                    σ]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.stepDischarge_run :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {ra : core_run_annotation} {l : sym}
  {pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym)},
  CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ e ctx (CerberusHeapLang.runRedex ra l pes) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      ∀ {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {params : List (sym × core_base_type)}
        {cont : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {vs : List value},
        CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel Q l = some (params, cont) →
          (∀ (pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym),
              pe ∈ pes → CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe ≤ lemDefaultFuel) →
            ∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem)
              (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation) (tid : Nat) (parent : Option Nat)
              (p : sym) (th : thread_state),
              th.arena = e →
                th.current_proc_opt = some p →
                  CerberusHeapLang.evalPexprs th.env pes = some vs →
                    ∀ (aid : Nat) (rs : core_run_state),
                      CerberusHeapLang.LabeledAt rs p Q →
                        List.map (CerberusHeapLang.dischargeStep aid rs σ)
                            (step_ctx tds σ file fmapEmpty tid (parent, th)) =
                          [CerberusHeapLang.EngineOutcome.next
                              { arena := cont, stack0 := th.stack0, errno := th.errno,
                                env := CerberusHeapLang.bindArgs params vs th.env,
                                current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt, exec_loc := th.exec_loc,
                                current_loc := th.current_loc }
                              σ]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.step_ctx_beta_annot :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {pa : List annot} {bty : core_base_type}
  {ds : List dyn_annotation} {v : value} {e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ e ctx
      (generic_expr.Expr []
        (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty)))
          (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v)) e2)) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      ∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem)
        (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation) (ext : Fmap sym sym) (tid : Nat)
        (parent : Option Nat) (th : thread_state),
        th.arena = e →
          ∀ {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)},
            th.env = ev0 :: evs →
              step_ctx tds σ file ext tid (parent, th) =
                [Step_tau2 "Esseq Eannot" TSK_Misc
                    { arena := apply_ctx ctx (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds e2)),
                      stack0 := th.stack0, errno := th.errno, env := th.env,
                      current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt, exec_loc := th.exec_loc,
                      current_loc := th.current_loc }]
----
