theorem CerberusHeapLang.step_ctx_case_value :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {a : List annot} {cval : value}
  {pats : List (pattern × CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr)} {e' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ e ctx (CerberusHeapLang.caseRedex (Pexpr a () (PEval cval)) pats) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      select_case subst_sym_expr cval pats = some e' →
        ∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem)
          (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation) (ext : Fmap sym sym) (tid : Nat)
          (parent : Option Nat) (th : thread_state),
          th.arena = e →
            step_ctx tds σ file ext tid (parent, th) =
              [Step_tau2 "Ecase" TSK_Misc
                  { arena := apply_ctx ctx e', stack0 := th.stack0, errno := th.errno,
                    env := th.env, current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt, exec_loc := th.exec_loc,
                    current_loc := th.current_loc }]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.step_ctx_create :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc}
  {ann : core_run_annotation} {align : CerbMem.IntegerValue} {ty : ctype} {pref : prefix0},
  CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ e ctx (CerberusHeapLang.createRedex loc ann align ty pref) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false →
        ∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem)
          (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation) (ext : Fmap sym sym) (tid : Nat)
          (parent : Option Nat) (th : thread_state),
          th.arena = e →
            step_ctx tds σ file ext tid (parent, th) =
              [Step_action_request2 "CreateRequest" loc tid (is_unseq_with_ccall ctx)
                  (stExceptUndef_return
                    (CreateRequest2 pref align ty (get_with_address []) none fun x pv =>
                      {
                        arena :=
                          apply_ctx ctx
                            (generic_expr.Expr []
                              (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval (Vobject (OVpointer pv)))))),
                        stack0 := th.stack0, errno := th.errno, env := th.env,
                        current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt, exec_loc := th.exec_loc,
                        current_loc := th.current_loc }))]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.step_ctx_done :
∀ (v : value) (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem)
  (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation) (ext : Fmap sym sym) (tid : Nat)
  (th : thread_state),
  th.arena = CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v) →
    th.stack0 = Stack_empty → step_ctx tds σ file ext tid (none, th) = [Step_done2 v]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.step_ctx_load :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc}
  {ann : core_run_annotation} {ty : ctype} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {mo : memory_order},
  CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ e ctx (CerberusHeapLang.loadRedex loc ann ty pv mo) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false →
        ∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem)
          (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation) (ext : Fmap sym sym) (tid : Nat)
          (parent : Option Nat) (th : thread_state),
          th.arena = e →
            step_ctx tds σ file ext tid (parent, th) =
              [Step_action_request2 "LoadRequest" loc tid (is_unseq_with_ccall ctx)
                  (stExceptUndef_return
                    (LoadRequest2 mo ty pv fun x fp mval =>
                      {
                        arena :=
                          apply_ctx ctx
                            (generic_expr.Expr []
                              (Eannot [DA_pos [] fp]
                                (generic_expr.Expr []
                                  (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval (valueFromMemValue mval).snd)))))),
                        stack0 := th.stack0, errno := th.errno, env := th.env,
                        current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt, exec_loc := th.exec_loc,
                        current_loc := th.current_loc }))]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.step_ctx_merge :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {ds1 ds2 : List dyn_annotation}
  {b : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ e ctx
      (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds1 (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds2 b)))) →
    is_irreducible (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds1 (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds2 b)))) =
        false →
      CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
        ∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem)
          (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation) (ext : Fmap sym sym) (tid : Nat)
          (parent : Option Nat) (th : thread_state),
          th.arena = e →
            step_ctx tds σ file ext tid (parent, th) =
              [Step_tau2 "Eannot" TSK_Misc
                  { arena := apply_ctx ctx (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot (ds1 ++ ds2) b)),
                    stack0 := th.stack0, errno := th.errno, env := th.env,
                    current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt, exec_loc := th.exec_loc,
                    current_loc := th.current_loc }]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.step_ctx_remove_annot :
∀ (ds : List dyn_annotation) (v : value) (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition))
  (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation) (ext : Fmap sym sym)
  (tid : Nat) (parent : Option Nat) (th : thread_state),
  th.arena = CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v) →
    step_ctx tds σ file ext tid (parent, th) =
      [Step_tau2 "CTX, Eannot(value)" TSK_Misc
          { arena := CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v), stack0 := th.stack0,
            errno := th.errno, env := th.env, current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt,
            exec_loc := th.exec_loc, current_loc := th.current_loc }]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.step_ctx_save :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {sb : sym × core_base_type}
  {ps :
    List
      (sym × (core_base_type × Option (ctype × pass_by_value_or_pointer)) × generic_pexpr Unit sym)}
  {body : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {cvals : List value},
  CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ e ctx (CerberusHeapLang.saveRedex sb ps body) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      valueFromPexprs (CerberusHeapLang.saveParamPexprs ps) = some cvals →
        ∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem)
          (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation) (ext : Fmap sym sym) (tid : Nat)
          (parent : Option Nat) (th : thread_state),
          th.arena = e →
            ∀ {ev0 : Fmap sym value} {evs : List (Fmap sym value)},
              th.env = ev0 :: evs →
                step_ctx tds σ file ext tid (parent, th) =
                  [Step_tau2 "Esave" TSK_Misc
                      { arena := apply_ctx ctx body, stack0 := th.stack0, errno := th.errno,
                        env := CerberusHeapLang.bindSaveParams ps cvals (ev0 :: evs),
                        current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt, exec_loc := th.exec_loc,
                        current_loc := th.current_loc }]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.step_ctx_store :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc}
  {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool} {ty : ctype} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value}
  {mo : memory_order} {mv : CerbMem.MemValue},
  CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ e ctx (CerberusHeapLang.storeRedex loc ann lk ty pv cv mo) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      CerbLocation.isLibraryLocation loc = false →
        ∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)),
          memValueFromValue tds (Ctype [] (unatomic_ ty)) cv = some mv →
            ∀ (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation)
              (ext : Fmap sym sym) (tid : Nat) (parent : Option Nat) (th : thread_state),
              th.arena = e →
                step_ctx tds σ file ext tid (parent, th) =
                  [Step_action_request2 "StoreRequest" loc tid (is_unseq_with_ccall ctx)
                      (stExceptUndef_return
                        (StoreRequest2 mo ty lk pv mv fun x fp =>
                          {
                            arena :=
                              apply_ctx ctx
                                (generic_expr.Expr []
                                  (Eannot [DA_pos [] fp]
                                    (generic_expr.Expr [] (Epure (Pexpr [] () (PEval Vunit)))))),
                            stack0 := th.stack0, errno := th.errno, env := th.env,
                            current_proc_opt := th.current_proc_opt, exec_loc := th.exec_loc,
                            current_loc := th.current_loc }))]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.step_ctx_store_illtyped :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ctx : context} {loc : CerbLocation.Loc}
  {ann : core_run_annotation} {lk : Bool} {ty : ctype} {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue} {cv : value}
  {mo : memory_order},
  CerberusHeapLang.DecompJ e ctx (CerberusHeapLang.storeRedex loc ann lk ty pv cv mo) →
    CerberusHeapLang.esize e ≤ lemDefaultFuel →
      ∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)),
        memValueFromValue tds (Ctype [] (unatomic_ ty)) cv = none →
          ∀ (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation)
            (ext : Fmap sym sym) (tid : Nat) (parent : Option Nat) (th : thread_state),
            th.arena = e →
              step_ctx tds σ file ext tid (parent, th) =
                [Step_error2
                    ((CerbLocation.stringFromLocation loc).append
                      ("the value of a store(".append
                        ((CerbPP.stringFromCore_ctype (Ctype [] (unatomic_ ty))).append
                          (") didn't match the lvalue type: ".append
                            (CerbPP.stringFromCore_value cv)))))]
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.step_eval_bridge :
∀ {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym} {v : value},
  CerberusHeapLang.PePure pe →
    CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ρ pe = some v →
      ∀ (fuel : Nat),
        CerberusHeapLang.peDepth pe ≤ fuel →
          ∀ (tds : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition)) (n : Nat) (loc : CerbLocation.Loc)
            (cloc : Option CerbLocation.Loc) (mem : Option CerbMem.MemState)
            (file : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation),
            step_eval_pexpr_lemFuel fuel tds n loc cloc fmapEmpty ρ mem file false pe =
              exception_undef_return (Pexpr [] () (PEval v))
----
def CerberusHeapLang.storeExpr :
CerbLocation.Loc →
  core_run_annotation →
    ctype → CerbMem.PointerValue → value → memory_order → CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.storeM_success :
∀ (σ : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (id : Int) (c : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeCell) (mv : CerbMem.MemValue)
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc),
  CerberusHeapLang.CellCoh σ id c →
    CerberusHeapLang.StorableAt c.ty mv →
      CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM
          (CerbMem.storeM loc c.ty false (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id c.addr) mv) σ =
        some
          (CerbMem.Footprint.FP CerbMem.FootprintAccess.W c.addr ↑(CerbMem.sizeofCtype c.ty),
            CerbMem.writeBytesTo σ c.addr (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd)
----
def CerberusHeapLang.storeRedex :
CerbLocation.Loc →
  core_run_annotation →
    Bool → ctype → CerbMem.PointerValue → value → memory_order → CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.store_applies :
CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM
    (CerbMem.storeM CerberusHeapLang.loc0 CerberusHeapLang.intTy false CerberusHeapLang.xPtr
      CerberusHeapLang.sevenMval)
    CerberusHeapLang.σ₀ =
  some (CerberusHeapLang.fpS, CerberusHeapLang.σ₁)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.store_applies_P :
CerberusHeapLang.applyMemM
    (CerbMem.storeM CerberusHeapLang.loc0 CerberusHeapLang.intTy false CerberusHeapLang.pxPtr
      CerberusHeapLang.sevenMval)
    CerberusHeapLang.σcP =
  some (CerberusHeapLang.fpSP, CerberusHeapLang.σ1P)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.string_compare_gt :
∀ {d1 d2 : String}, ¬d1 < d2 → d1 ≠ d2 → Ord.compare d1 d2 = Ordering.gt
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.string_compare_lt :
∀ {d1 d2 : String}, d1 < d2 → Ord.compare d1 d2 = Ordering.lt
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.string_compare_self :
∀ (d : String), Ord.compare d d = Ordering.eq
----
def CerberusHeapLang.symCmpK :
sym → sym → LemOrdering
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.symCmpK_eq :
∀ (d1 : String) (n1 : Nat) (sd1 : symbol_description) (d2 : String) (n2 : Nat)
  (sd2 : symbol_description),
  CerberusHeapLang.symCmpK (Symbol d1 n1 sd1) (Symbol d2 n2 sd2) =
    if
        (intLtb (CerberusFresh.digest_compare d1 d2) 0 ||
            CerberusFresh.digest_compare d1 d2 == 0 && natLtb n1 n2) =
          true then
      LemOrdering.LT
    else
      if symbolEquality (Symbol d1 n1 sd1) (Symbol d2 n2 sd2) = true then LemOrdering.EQ
      else LemOrdering.GT
----
def CerberusHeapLang.symCmpL :
sym → sym → LemOrdering
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.symFrame_empty :
CerberusHeapLang.SymFrame fmapEmpty
----
def CerberusHeapLang.symKey :
sym → String × Nat
----
def CerberusHeapLang.symOrd :
sym → sym → Ordering
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.symOrd_eq_compareOn :
CerberusHeapLang.symOrd =
  compareLex (compareOn fun s => (CerberusHeapLang.symKey s).fst)
    (compareOn fun s => (CerberusHeapLang.symKey s).snd)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.symbolEquality_eq :
∀ (d1 : String) (n1 : Nat) (sd1 : symbol_description) (d2 : String) (n2 : Nat)
  (sd2 : symbol_description),
  symbolEquality (Symbol d1 n1 sd1) (Symbol d2 n2 sd2) =
    (CerberusFresh.digest_compare d1 d2 == 0 && n1 == n2)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.take_sum_succ :
∀ (vs : List Int) (i : Nat) (h : i < vs.length),
  (List.take (i + 1) vs).sum = (List.take i vs).sum + vs[i]
----
def CerberusHeapLang.toVal :
CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → Option CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal
----
def CerberusHeapLang.toValRt :
CerberusHeapLang.CoreRt → Option CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.toValRt_mk :
∀ (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) (Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap),
  CerberusHeapLang.toValRt { e := e, ρ := ρ, lbl := Q } =
    Option.map (fun w => { w := w, ρ := ρ, lbl := Q }) (CerberusHeapLang.toVal e)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.toValRt_ofValRt :
∀ (v : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal), CerberusHeapLang.toValRt (CerberusHeapLang.ofValRt v) = some v
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.toValRt_of_toVal :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap}
  {w : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal},
  CerberusHeapLang.toVal e = some w →
    CerberusHeapLang.toValRt { e := e, ρ := ρ, lbl := Q } = some { w := w, ρ := ρ, lbl := Q }
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.toValRt_of_toVal_none :
∀ {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack} {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap},
  CerberusHeapLang.toVal e = none → CerberusHeapLang.toValRt { e := e, ρ := ρ, lbl := Q } = none
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.toVal_action_node :
∀ (a : List annot) (p : generic_paction core_run_annotation Unit sym),
  CerberusHeapLang.toVal (generic_expr.Expr a (Eaction p)) = none
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.toVal_annot_cases :
∀ (a : List annot) (c : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ds : List dyn_annotation),
  (a = [] ∧
      ∃ v,
        c = CerberusHeapLang.ofVal (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v) ∧
          CerberusHeapLang.toVal (generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot ds c)) =
            some (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot ds v)) ∨
    CerberusHeapLang.toVal (generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot ds c)) = none
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.toVal_annot_none :
∀ {a : List annot} {ds : List dyn_annotation} {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.toVal e = none →
    CerberusHeapLang.toVal (generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot ds e)) = none
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.toVal_ofVal :
∀ (v : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal), CerberusHeapLang.toVal (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal v) = some v
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.toVal_pure_none :
∀ {a : List annot} {pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym},
  valueFromPexpr pe = none → CerberusHeapLang.toVal (generic_expr.Expr a (Epure pe)) = none
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.toVal_sseq_node :
∀ (a : List annot) (pat : pattern) (e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr),
  CerberusHeapLang.toVal (generic_expr.Expr a (Esseq pat e1 e2)) = none
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.treeMap_get?_insert_empty :
∀ {α β : Type} (cmp : α → α → Ordering) (k : α) (v : β) (l : α),
  (Std.TreeMap.empty.insert k v).get? l = if cmp l k = Ordering.eq then some v else none
----
def CerberusHeapLang.triple :
{hlc : Iris.HasLC} →
  {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
    [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF] →
      Iris.IProp GF → CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → (CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal → Iris.IProp GF) → Prop
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.triple_conseq :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {P' P : Iris.IProp GF} {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
  {Ψ Ψ' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal → Iris.IProp GF},
  (P' ⊢ P) →
    (∀ (v : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal), Ψ v ⊢ Ψ' v) →
      CerberusHeapLang.triple P e Ψ → CerberusHeapLang.triple P' e Ψ'
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.triple_frame :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {P R : Iris.IProp GF} {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal → Iris.IProp GF},
  CerberusHeapLang.triple P e Ψ → CerberusHeapLang.triple iprop(P ∗ R) e fun v => iprop(Ψ v ∗ R)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.triple_seq :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {P Q R : Iris.IProp GF} {bty : core_base_type} {e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr},
  CerberusHeapLang.EnvStable e1 →
    (CerberusHeapLang.triple P e1 fun x => Q) →
      (CerberusHeapLang.triple Q e2 fun x => R) →
        CerberusHeapLang.triple P (CerberusHeapLang.sseqExpr bty e1 e2) fun x => R
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.update_env_aux_spec :
∀ (pa pb : List annot) (x : sym) (bty : core_base_type) (ov : object_value) (m : Fmap sym value),
  update_env_aux (CerberusHeapLang.specPat pa pb x bty) (Vloaded (LVspecified ov)) m =
    CerberusHeapLang.envAdd x (Vobject ov) m
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.update_env_aux_sym :
∀ (x : sym) (b : core_base_type) (v : value) (m : Fmap sym value),
  update_env_aux (mk_sym_pat x b) v m = CerberusHeapLang.envAdd x v m
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.update_env_cons :
∀ (pat : pattern) (v : value) (ev0 : Fmap sym value) (evs : List (Fmap sym value)),
  update_env pat v (ev0 :: evs) = update_env_aux pat v ev0 :: evs
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.update_env_spec :
∀ (pa pb : List annot) (x : sym) (bty : core_base_type) (ov : object_value) (ev0 : Fmap sym value)
  (evs : List (Fmap sym value)),
  update_env (CerberusHeapLang.specPat pa pb x bty) (Vloaded (LVspecified ov)) (ev0 :: evs) =
    CerberusHeapLang.envAdd x (Vobject ov) ev0 :: evs
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.update_thread_state_single :
∀ (th th' : thread_state) (cs : core_state),
  cs.thread_states = [(0, none, th)] →
    update_thread_state 0 th' cs = { thread_states := [(0, none, th')], io := cs.io }
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.valueFromMemValue_int :
∀ (ety : integerType) (iv : CerbMem.IntegerValue),
  (valueFromMemValue (CerbMem.MemValue.MVinteger ety iv)).snd = Vloaded (LVspecified (OVinteger iv))
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.valueFromPexpr_val :
∀ (a : List annot) (v : value), valueFromPexpr (Pexpr a () (PEval v)) = some v
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wp_annot :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {s : Iris.Stuckness} {E : CoPset} (ds : List dyn_annotation) (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr)
  (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) {Φ : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal → Iris.IProp GF},
  WP { e := e, ρ := ρ, lbl := CerberusHeapLang.spikeLbl } @ s ;
      E {{ v, Φ (CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.merge ds v) }} ⊢
    WP { e := generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds e), ρ := ρ, lbl := CerberusHeapLang.spikeLbl } @ s ;
      E {{ Φ }}
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wp_annot_reindex :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {s : Iris.Stuckness} {E : CoPset} (a : List annot) (dsA dsB : List dyn_annotation)
  (c : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack)
  {Φ₁ Φ₂ : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal → Iris.IProp GF},
  (∀ (v : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal),
      Φ₁ (CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.merge dsA v) = Φ₂ (CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal.merge dsB v)) →
    WP { e := generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot dsA c), ρ := ρ, lbl := CerberusHeapLang.spikeLbl } @ s ;
        E {{ Φ₁ }} ⊢
      WP { e := generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot dsB c), ρ := ρ, lbl := CerberusHeapLang.spikeLbl } @ s ;
        E {{ Φ₂ }}
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wp_env_invariant_stable :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {s : Iris.Stuckness} {E : CoPset} (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  CerberusHeapLang.EnvStable e →
    ∀ {Φ : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal → Iris.IProp GF},
      WP { e := e, ρ := ρ, lbl := CerberusHeapLang.spikeLbl } @ s ; E {{ Φ }} ⊢
        WP { e := e, ρ := ρ, lbl := CerberusHeapLang.spikeLbl } @ s ; E {{ v, ⌜v.ρ = ρ⌝ ∗ Φ v }}
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wp_load :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {s : Iris.Stuckness} {E : CoPset} {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} (loc : CerbLocation.Loc)
  (ann : core_run_annotation) (ty : ctype) (pv : CerbMem.PointerValue) (mo : memory_order)
  (dq : Iris.DFrac) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  CerberusHeapLang.cellLoadTrap { addr := CerberusHeapLang.addrOf pv, ty := ty, bytes := bs } =
      false →
    pv ↦c{dq} ty ; bs ⊢
      WP { e := CerberusHeapLang.loadExpr loc ann ty pv mo, ρ := ρ, lbl := Q } @ s ;
        E {{
          w,
            ∃ fp,
              ⌜w =
                    {
                      w :=
                        CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot [DA_pos [] fp]
                          (CerberusHeapLang.loadedVal pv ty bs),
                      ρ := ρ, lbl := Q }⌝ ∗
                pv ↦c{dq} ty ; bs }}
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wp_ofVal :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {s : Iris.Stuckness} {E : CoPset} (v : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal)
  {Φ : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal → Iris.IProp GF},
  Φ v ⊢ WP (CerberusHeapLang.ofValRt v) @ s ; E {{ Φ }}
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wp_sseq :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {s : Iris.Stuckness} {E : CoPset} (a pa : List annot) (bty : core_base_type)
  (e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ev0 : Fmap sym value) (evs : List (Fmap sym value))
  {Φ : CerberusHeapLang.CoreRVal → Iris.IProp GF},
  WP { e := e1, ρ := ev0 :: evs, lbl := CerberusHeapLang.spikeLbl } @ s ;
      E {{
        v,
          ▷
            WP { e := e2, ρ := v.ρ, lbl := CerberusHeapLang.spikeLbl } @ s ;
              E {{ w, Φ (CerberusHeapLang.mergeInto v w) }} }} ⊢
    WP
      { e := generic_expr.Expr a (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) e1 e2), ρ := ev0 :: evs,
        lbl := CerberusHeapLang.spikeLbl } @
      s ; E {{ Φ }}
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wp_store :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {s : Iris.Stuckness} {E : CoPset} {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} (loc : CerbLocation.Loc)
  (ann : core_run_annotation) (ty : ctype) (pv : CerbMem.PointerValue) (cv : value)
  (mo : memory_order) (mv : CerbMem.MemValue) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte)
  (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  memValueFromValue fmapEmpty (Ctype [] (unatomic_ ty)) cv = some mv →
    CerberusHeapLang.StorableAt ty mv →
      pv ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} ty ; bs ⊢
        WP { e := CerberusHeapLang.storeExpr loc ann ty pv cv mo, ρ := ρ, lbl := Q } @ s ;
          E {{
            w,
              ∃ fp,
                ⌜w =
                      { w := CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot [DA_pos [] fp] Vunit, ρ := ρ,
                        lbl := Q }⌝ ∗
                  pv ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} ty ; (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd }}
----
def CerberusHeapLang.wps :
{hlc : Iris.HasLC} →
  {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
    [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF] →
      CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap →
        CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF →
          (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF) →
            CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF
----
def CerberusHeapLang.wps.pre :
{hlc : Iris.HasLC} →
  {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} →
    [CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF] →
      CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap →
        CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF →
          ((CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF) →
              CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF) →
            (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF) →
              CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps.pre.contractive :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  (Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap) (Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF),
  Iris.OFE.Contractive (CerberusHeapLang.wps.pre Q Ls)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_annot :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF} (ds : List dyn_annotation)
  (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack)
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF},
  CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls (fun w ρ' => Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.merge ds w) ρ') e ρ ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls Ψ (generic_expr.Expr [] (Eannot ds e)) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_annot_reindex :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF} (a : List annot)
  (dsA dsB : List dyn_annotation) (c : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack)
  {Ψ₁ Ψ₂ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF},
  (∀ (w : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal) (ρ' : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
      Ψ₁ (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.merge dsA w) ρ' =
        Ψ₂ (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.merge dsB w) ρ') →
    CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls Ψ₁ (generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot dsA c)) ρ ⊢
      CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls Ψ₂ (generic_expr.Expr a (Eannot dsB c)) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_case_value :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF} (a : List annot)
  (pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym) (pats : List (pattern × CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr)) {cval : value}
  {e' : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  valueFromPexpr pe = some cval →
    select_case subst_sym_expr cval pats = some e' →
      CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls Ψ e' ρ ⊢
        CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls Ψ (generic_expr.Expr a (Ecase pe pats)) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_exhibit_seq_stores :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF} (x y : CerbMem.PointerValue)
  (loc loc' : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann ann' : core_run_annotation) (mo mo' : memory_order)
  (bty : core_base_type) (bsx bsy : List CerbMem.AbsByte) (ev0 : Fmap sym value)
  (evs : List (Fmap sym value)),
  x ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; bsx ∗
      y ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; bsy ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls
      (fun x_1 x_2 =>
        iprop(x ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; CerberusHeapLang.fiveBytes ∗
            y ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; CerberusHeapLang.sixBytes))
      (CerberusHeapLang.sseqExpr bty
        (CerberusHeapLang.storeExpr loc ann CerberusHeapLang.intTy x CerberusHeapLang.fiveVal mo)
        (CerberusHeapLang.storeExpr loc' ann' CerberusHeapLang.intTy y CerberusHeapLang.sixVal mo'))
      (ev0 :: evs)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_exhibit_store_frame :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF} (x y : CerbMem.PointerValue)
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann : core_run_annotation) (mo : memory_order)
  (bs bs' : List CerbMem.AbsByte) (ty' : ctype) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  x ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; bs ∗ y ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} ty' ; bs' ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls
      (fun x_1 x_2 =>
        iprop(x ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} CerberusHeapLang.intTy ; CerberusHeapLang.sevenBytes ∗
            y ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} ty' ; bs'))
      (CerberusHeapLang.storeExpr loc ann CerberusHeapLang.intTy x CerberusHeapLang.sevenVal mo) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_frame :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF} {R : Iris.IProp GF}
  (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls Ψ e ρ ∗ R ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls (fun w ρ' => iprop(Ψ w ρ' ∗ R)) e ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_if_false :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF} (a : List annot)
  (g : generic_pexpr Unit sym) (e2 e3 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ρ g = some Vfalse →
    CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls Ψ e3 ρ ⊢
      CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls Ψ (generic_expr.Expr a (Eif g e2 e3)) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_if_true :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF} (a : List annot)
  (g : generic_pexpr Unit sym) (e2 e3 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ρ g = some Vtrue →
    CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls Ψ e2 ρ ⊢
      CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls Ψ (generic_expr.Expr a (Eif g e2 e3)) ρ

theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_load :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann : core_run_annotation) (ty : ctype) (pv : CerbMem.PointerValue)
  (mo : memory_order) (dq : Iris.DFrac) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  CerberusHeapLang.cellLoadTrap { addr := CerberusHeapLang.addrOf pv, ty := ty, bytes := bs } =
      false →
    (pv ↦c{dq} ty ; bs ∗
        ∀ fp,
          pv ↦c{dq} ty ; bs -∗
            Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot [DA_pos [] fp] (CerberusHeapLang.loadedVal pv ty bs))
              ρ) ⊢
      CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.loadExpr loc ann ty pv mo) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_load_eval :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann : core_run_annotation) (ty : ctype) (pe2 : generic_pexpr Unit sym)
  (mo : memory_order) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) {pv : CerbMem.PointerValue},
  valueFromPexpr pe2 = none →
    CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ρ pe2 = some (Vobject (OVpointer pv)) →
      CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.loadExpr loc ann ty pv mo) ρ ⊢
        CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.loadOpRedex loc ann ty pe2 mo) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_load_interior :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann : core_run_annotation) (aty : ctype) (id a : Int) (off : Nat)
  (mo : memory_order) (dq : Iris.DFrac) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack)
  {mv : CerbMem.MemValue},
  off + CerbMem.sizeofCtype CerberusHeapLang.intTy ≤ CerbMem.sizeofCtype aty →
    (∀ (lum : List (Int × identifier)) (fpm : CerbMem.Funptrmap),
        CerbMem.reconstructValue lum fpm (a + ↑off) CerberusHeapLang.intTy
            (List.take (CerbMem.sizeofCtype CerberusHeapLang.intTy) (List.drop off bs)) =
          mv) →
      (id ↦{dq} { addr := a, ty := aty, bytes := bs } ∗
          ∀ fp,
            id ↦{dq} { addr := a, ty := aty, bytes := bs } -∗
              Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot [DA_pos [] fp] (valueFromMemValue mv).snd) ρ) ⊢
        CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls Ψ
          (CerberusHeapLang.loadExpr loc ann CerberusHeapLang.intTy
            (CerberusHeapLang.cellPtr id (a + ↑off)) mo)
          ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_ofVal :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (w : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  Ψ w ρ ⊢ CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.ofVal w) ρ

theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_pure :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack) {v : value},
  valueFromPexpr pe = none →
    CerberusHeapLang.evalPexpr ρ pe = some v →
      Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.pure v) ρ ⊢
        CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls Ψ (generic_expr.Expr [] (Epure pe)) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_run :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF} (a : List annot)
  (ra : core_run_annotation) (l : sym) (pes : List (generic_pexpr Unit sym))
  {params : List (sym × core_base_type)} {cont : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {vs : List value}
  (ev0 : Fmap sym value) (evs : List (Fmap sym value)),
  CerberusHeapLang.lookupLabel Q l = some (params, cont) →
    CerberusHeapLang.evalPexprs (ev0 :: evs) pes = some vs →
      Ls l vs (ev0 :: evs) ⊢
        CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls Ψ (generic_expr.Expr a (Erun ra l pes)) (ev0 :: evs)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_save :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF} (a : List annot)
  (sb : sym × core_base_type)
  (ps :
    List
      (sym × (core_base_type × Option (ctype × pass_by_value_or_pointer)) × generic_pexpr Unit sym))
  (body : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) {cvals : List value} (ev0 : Fmap sym value)
  (evs : List (Fmap sym value)),
  valueFromPexprs (CerberusHeapLang.saveParamPexprs ps) = some cvals →
    CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls Ψ body (CerberusHeapLang.bindSaveParams ps cvals (ev0 :: evs)) ⊢
      CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls Ψ (generic_expr.Expr a (Esave sb ps body)) (ev0 :: evs)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_seq :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF} (a pa : List annot)
  (bty : core_base_type) (e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ev0 : Fmap sym value)
  (evs : List (Fmap sym value)),
  CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls
      (fun w ρ' => CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls (fun u ρ'' => Ψ (w.mergeInto u) ρ'') e2 ρ') e1
      (ev0 :: evs) ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls Ψ
      (generic_expr.Expr a (Esseq (Pattern pa (CaseBase (none, bty))) e1 e2)) (ev0 :: evs)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_seq_spec :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF} (a pa pb : List annot)
  (x : sym) (bty : core_base_type) (e1 e2 : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ev0 : Fmap sym value)
  (evs : List (Fmap sym value)),
  CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls
      (fun w ρ' =>
        iprop(∃ ov,
            ⌜w.val = Vloaded (LVspecified ov)⌝ ∗
              CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls (fun u ρ'' => Ψ (w.mergeInto u) ρ'') e2
                (update_env (CerberusHeapLang.specPat pa pb x bty) (Vloaded (LVspecified ov)) ρ')))
      e1 (ev0 :: evs) ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls Ψ
      (generic_expr.Expr a (Esseq (CerberusHeapLang.specPat pa pb x bty) e1 e2)) (ev0 :: evs)
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_sound :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  CerberusHeapLang.blockSpecs Q Ls Ψ ⊢
    CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls Ψ e ρ -∗
      WP { e := e, ρ := ρ, lbl := Q } @ Iris.Stuckness.NotStuck ; ⊤ {{ w, Ψ w.w w.ρ }}
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_store :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (loc : CerbLocation.Loc) (ann : core_run_annotation) (ty : ctype) (pv : CerbMem.PointerValue)
  (cv : value) (mo : memory_order) (mv : CerbMem.MemValue) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte)
  (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  memValueFromValue fmapEmpty (Ctype [] (unatomic_ ty)) cv = some mv →
    CerberusHeapLang.StorableAt ty mv →
      (pv ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} ty ; bs ∗
          ∀ fp,
            pv ↦c{Iris.DFrac.own 1} ty ; (CerbMem.memValueToBytes [] mv).snd -∗
              Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal.annot [DA_pos [] fp] Vunit) ρ) ⊢
        CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls Ψ (CerberusHeapLang.storeExpr loc ann ty pv cv mo) ρ
----
theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_unfold :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  {e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr} {ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack},
  CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls Ψ e ρ ⊣⊢ CerberusHeapLang.wps.pre Q Ls (CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls) Ψ e ρ

theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_value_inv :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (w : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),

theorem CerberusHeapLang.wps_wand :
∀ {hlc : Iris.HasLC} {GF : Iris.BundledGFunctors} [inst : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeGS hlc GF]
  {Q : CerberusHeapLang.LabelMap} {Ls : CerberusHeapLang.LabelSpec GF}
  {Ψ₁ Ψ₂ : CerberusHeapLang.SpikeVal → CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack → Iris.IProp GF}
  (e : CerberusHeapLang.CoreExpr) (ρ : CerberusHeapLang.EnvStack),
  CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls Ψ₁ e ρ ⊢
    (∀ w ρ', Ψ₁ w ρ' -∗ Ψ₂ w ρ') -∗ CerberusHeapLang.wps Q Ls Ψ₂ e ρ

theorem CerberusHeapLang.writeBytesTo_bytemap_get? :
∀ (st : CerberusHeapLang.Mem) (a : Int) (bs : List CerbMem.AbsByte) (k : Int),
  (CerbMem.writeBytesTo st a bs).bytemap.get? k =
    if a ≤ k ∧ k < a + ↑bs.length then bs[(k - a).toNat]? else st.bytemap.get? k

theorem apply_ctx.eq_def :
∀ (ctx : context) (expr1 : generic_expr core_run_annotation Unit sym),
  apply_ctx ctx expr1 =
    match ctx with
    | CTX => expr1
    | Cunseq annot1 es1 ctx' es2 =>
      generic_expr.Expr annot1 (Eunseq (es1 ++ apply_ctx ctx' expr1 :: es2))
    | Cwseq annot1 pat ctx' e2 => generic_expr.Expr annot1 (Ewseq pat (apply_ctx ctx' expr1) e2)
    | Csseq annot1 pat ctx' e2 => generic_expr.Expr annot1 (Esseq pat (apply_ctx ctx' expr1) e2)
    | Cannot annot1 xs ctx' => generic_expr.Expr annot1 (Eannot xs (apply_ctx ctx' expr1))
    | Cbound annot1 ctx' => generic_expr.Expr annot1 (Ebound (apply_ctx ctx' expr1))
----
theorem assoc_adjust.eq_def :
∀ {a b : Type} [inst : Lem_Basic_classes.Eq0 a] (f : b → b) (k : a) (x : List (a × b)),
  assoc_adjust f k x =
    match x with
    | [] => []
    | (k', v) :: xs => if (k == k') = true then (k, f v) :: xs else (k', v) :: assoc_adjust f k xs
----
theorem drive_nonmemory_steps_aux2_lemFuel.eq_def :
∀ (lemFuel : Nat) (_lemReader_tagDefs : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition))
  (acc : Fmap thread_id (List core_step2)),
  drive_nonmemory_steps_aux2_lemFuel lemFuel _lemReader_tagDefs acc =
    match (motive :=
      Nat →
        List Nat →
          ndM (Fmap thread_id (List core_step2)) step_kind driver_error
            (mem_constraint CerbMem.IntegerValue) driver_state)
      lemFuel with
    | 0 => fuelExhausted fun x => ND fun st => (NDkilled (Undef0 CerbLocation.Loc.unknown []), st)
    | lemFuel.succ => fun x =>
      match x with
      | [] => nd_return acc
      | tid1 :: xs' =>
        nd_bind
          (nd_read fun dr_st =>
            have th_info :=
              match Lem_List.lookupBy (fun x y => x == y) tid1 dr_st.core_state0.thread_states with
              | some z => z
              | none =>
                failwithI
                  ("Driver.drive_nonmemory_steps_aux2 => invalid tid: ".append
                    (Lem_String_extra.stringFromNat tid1));
            step_ctx _lemReader_tagDefs dr_st.layout_state dr_st.core_file dr_st.core_extern tid1
              th_info)
          fun steps =>
          match find_can_advance steps with
          | none =>
            drive_nonmemory_steps_aux2_lemFuel lemFuel _lemReader_tagDefs
              (fmapAddBy defaultCompare tid1 steps acc) xs'
          | some step1 =>
            nd_bind (advance_step _lemReader_tagDefs tid1 step1) fun wakeups =>
              have xs'' :=
                match wakeups with
                | NOWAKEUP => tid1 :: xs'
                | WAKEUP false tids => tid1 :: list_inserts tids xs'
                | WAKEUP true tids => list_inserts tids xs';
              drive_nonmemory_steps_aux2_lemFuel lemFuel _lemReader_tagDefs acc xs''
----
theorem driver2_lemFuel.eq_def :
∀ (lemFuel : Nat) (_lemReader_tagDefs : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition))
  (with_concurrency : Bool),
  driver2_lemFuel lemFuel _lemReader_tagDefs with_concurrency =
    match lemFuel with
    | 0 => fuelExhausted (ND fun st => (NDkilled (Undef0 CerbLocation.Loc.unknown []), st))
    | lemFuel.succ =>
      nd_bind (new_drive_core_threads _lemReader_tagDefs ()) fun tid_steps =>
        nd_bind nd_get fun post_core_dr_st =>
          have _non_blocked_th_sts :=
            List.filter
              (fun p =>
                match p with
                | (tid1, th_info) =>
                  (step_ctx _lemReader_tagDefs post_core_dr_st.layout_state
                        post_core_dr_st.core_file post_core_dr_st.core_extern tid1 th_info).any
                    fun step1 => step1 != Step_blocked2)
              post_core_dr_st.core_state0.thread_states;
          if
              Lem_Maybe.maybeEqualBy (fun x y => x == y) (CerbGlobal.current_execution_mode ())
                  (some CerbGlobal.ExecutionMode.random) =
                true then
            bindExhaustive (pick (SK_misc ["driver 2"]) tid_steps) fun p =>
              match p with
              | (tid1, step_opt) =>
                match step_opt with
                | some step1 =>
                  process_core_step2 _lemReader_tagDefs with_concurrency
                    (driver2_lemFuel lemFuel _lemReader_tagDefs) step1
                | none => nd_return ()
          else
            have non_blocked :=
              List.filter
                (fun p =>
                  match p with
                  | (tid1, step_opt) =>
                    !Lem_Maybe.maybeEqualBy (fun x y => x == y) step_opt (some Step_blocked2))
                tid_steps;
            nd_bind (if (non_blocked.length == 0) = true then nd_return () else nd_return ())
              fun u =>
              nd_bind (pick (SK_misc ["driver non_blocked"]) non_blocked) fun x =>
                match x with
                | (fst, none) => nd_return ()
                | (fst, some step1) =>
                  process_core_step2 _lemReader_tagDefs with_concurrency
                    (driver2_lemFuel lemFuel _lemReader_tagDefs) step1
----
theorem eval_pexpr_aux2_lemFuel.eq_def :
∀ (lemFuel : Nat) (_lemReader_tagDefs : Fmap sym (CerbLocation.Loc × tag_definition))
  (loc1 : CerbLocation.Loc) (current_call_loc_opt : Option CerbLocation.Loc)
  (core_extern1 : Fmap sym sym) (env1 : List (Fmap sym value))
  (mem_st_opt : Option CerbMem.MemState) (file1 : generic_file Unit core_run_annotation)
  (pe : generic_pexpr Unit sym),
  eval_pexpr_aux2_lemFuel lemFuel _lemReader_tagDefs loc1 current_call_loc_opt core_extern1 env1
      mem_st_opt file1 pe =
    match lemFuel with
    | 0 => fuelExhausted (Result (Undef CerbLocation.Loc.unknown []))
    | lemFuel.succ =>
      match
        have pulled := pull_constrained 0 pe;
        pulled with
      | pulled_pe@h:(Pexpr a PUnit.unit (PEconstrained a_1)) =>
        exception_undef_return (Sum.inl pulled_pe)
      | pulled_pe =>
        exception_undef_bind
          (step_eval_pexpr _lemReader_tagDefs 0 loc1 current_call_loc_opt core_extern1 env1
            mem_st_opt file1 false pulled_pe)
          fun pe' =>
          match valueFromPexpr pe' with
          | some cval => exception_undef_return (Sum.inr cval)
          | none =>
            eval_pexpr_aux2_lemFuel lemFuel _lemReader_tagDefs loc1 current_call_loc_opt
              core_extern1 env1 mem_st_opt file1 pe'
----
theorem get_loc.eq_def :
∀ (annots1 : List annot),
  get_loc annots1 =
    match annots1 with
    | [] => none
    | Aloc loc1 :: tail => some loc1
    | Astd a :: annots' => get_loc annots'
    | Auid a :: annots' => get_loc annots'
    | Amarker a :: annots' => get_loc annots'
    | Amarker_object_types a :: annots' => get_loc annots'
    | Abmc a :: annots' => get_loc annots'
    | Aattrs a :: annots' => get_loc annots'
    | Atypedef a :: annots' => get_loc annots'
    | Alabel a :: annots' => get_loc annots'
    | Acerb a :: annots' => get_loc annots'
    | Avalue a :: annots' => get_loc annots'
    | Ainlined_label a :: annots' => get_loc annots'
    | Astmt :: annots' => get_loc annots'
    | Aexpr :: annots' => get_loc annots'
----
theorem is_unseq_with_ccall_aux.eq_def :
∀ (acc : Bool) (x : context),
  is_unseq_with_ccall_aux acc x =
    match x with
    | CTX => acc
    | Cunseq a es1 ctx' es2 =>
      have acc' := acc || (es1 ++ es2).any has_ccall;
      is_unseq_with_ccall_aux acc' ctx'
    | Cwseq a a_1 ctx' e2 => is_unseq_with_ccall_aux acc ctx'
    | Csseq a a_1 ctx' e2 => is_unseq_with_ccall_aux acc ctx'
    | Cannot a a_1 ctx' => is_unseq_with_ccall_aux acc ctx'
    | Cbound a ctx' => is_unseq_with_ccall_aux false ctx'
----
theorem liftAction_lemFuel.eq_def :
∀ {a cs err1 err2 info1 info2 st1 st2 : Type} (lemFuel : Nat) (get2 : st2 → st1)
  (put1 : st2 → st1 → st2) (liftInfo : info1 → info2) (liftErr : err1 → err2),
  liftAction_lemFuel lemFuel get2 put1 liftInfo liftErr =
    match (motive := Nat → nd_action a info1 err1 cs st1 → nd_action a info2 err2 cs st2)
      lemFuel with
    | 0 => fuelExhausted fun x => NDkilled (Undef0 CerbLocation.Loc.unknown [])
    | lemFuel.succ =>
      match (motive :=
        (st2 → st1) →
          (st2 → st1 → st2) →
            (info1 → info2) →
              (err1 → err2) → nd_action a info1 err1 cs st1 → nd_action a info2 err2 cs st2)
        get2, put1, liftInfo, liftErr with
      | get2, put1, liftInfo, liftErr => fun x =>
        match x with
        | NDactive a1 => NDactive a1
        | NDkilled r =>
          NDkilled
            (match r with
            | Undef0 loc1 ubs => Undef0 loc1 ubs
            | Error0 loc1 str => Error0 loc1 str
            | Other err => Other (liftErr err))
        | NDnd info str_ms =>
          NDnd (liftInfo info)
            (List.map
              (fun p =>
                match p with
                | (info, m_act) =>
                  (liftInfo info, liftND_lemFuel lemFuel get2 put1 liftInfo liftErr m_act))
              str_ms)
        | NDguard info cs1 act =>
          NDguard (liftInfo info) cs1 (liftND_lemFuel lemFuel get2 put1 liftInfo liftErr act)
        | NDbranch info cs1 m_act1 m_act2 =>
          NDbranch (liftInfo info) cs1 (liftND_lemFuel lemFuel get2 put1 liftInfo liftErr m_act1)
            (liftND_lemFuel lemFuel get2 put1 liftInfo liftErr m_act2)
        | NDstep info info_ms =>
          NDstep (liftInfo info)
            (List.map
              (fun p =>
                match p with
                | (info, m) => (liftInfo info, liftND_lemFuel lemFuel get2 put1 liftInfo liftErr m))
              info_ms)
----
theorem liftND_lemFuel.eq_def :
∀ {a cs err1 err2 info1 info2 st1 st2 : Type} (lemFuel : Nat) (get2 : st2 → st1)
  (put1 : st2 → st1 → st2) (liftInfo : info1 → info2) (liftErr : err1 → err2)
  (n : ndM a info1 err1 cs st1),
  liftND_lemFuel lemFuel get2 put1 liftInfo liftErr n =
    match lemFuel with
    | 0 => fuelExhausted (ND fun st => (NDkilled (Undef0 CerbLocation.Loc.unknown []), st))
    | lemFuel.succ =>
      match get2, put1, liftInfo, liftErr, n with
      | get2, put1, liftInfo, liftErr, ND m =>
        ND fun st21 =>
          match m (get2 st21) with
          | (act, st1') =>
            (liftAction_lemFuel lemFuel get2 put1 liftInfo liftErr act, put1 st21 st1')
----
theorem lookup_env.eq_def :
∀ {a b : Type} [inst : Lem_Map.MapKeyType b] (sym1 : b) (x : List (Fmap b a)),
  lookup_env sym1 x =
    match x with
    | [] => none
    | env1 :: xs =>
      match fmapLookupBy Lem_Map.mapKeyCompare sym1 env1 with
      | none => lookup_env sym1 xs
      | some ret => some ret
----
theorem nd_bind_lemFuel.eq_def :
∀ {a b c d e f : Type} (lemFuel : Nat) (n : ndM f b d a c) (f1 : f → ndM e b d a c),
  nd_bind_lemFuel lemFuel n f1 =
    match lemFuel with
    | 0 => fuelExhausted (ND fun st => (NDkilled (Undef0 CerbLocation.Loc.unknown []), st))
    | lemFuel.succ =>
      match n, f1 with
      | ND m_act, f1 =>
        ND fun st =>
          match m_act st with
          | (NDactive z, st') =>
            match f1 z with
            | ND m_act' => m_act' st'
          | (NDkilled r, st') => (NDkilled r, st')
          | (NDnd debug_str str_ms, st') =>
            (NDnd debug_str
                (List.map
                  (fun p =>
                    match p with
                    | (str, m) => (str, nd_bind_lemFuel lemFuel m f1))
                  str_ms),
              st')
          | (NDguard debug_str cs m_act', st') =>
            (NDguard debug_str cs (nd_bind_lemFuel lemFuel m_act' f1), st')
          | (NDbranch debug_str cs m_act1 m_act2, st') =>
            (NDbranch debug_str cs (nd_bind_lemFuel lemFuel m_act1 f1)
                (nd_bind_lemFuel lemFuel m_act2 f1),
              st')
          | (NDstep info str_ms, st') =>
            (NDstep info
                (List.map
                  (fun p =>
                    match p with
                    | (str, m) => (str, nd_bind_lemFuel lemFuel m f1))
                  str_ms),
              st')
----
theorem update_env_aux_lemFuel.eq_def :
∀ {a : Type} [inst : Lem_Map.MapKeyType a] (lemFuel : Nat) (g : generic_pattern a) (cval : value)
  (env1 : Fmap a value),
  update_env_aux_lemFuel lemFuel g cval env1 =
    match lemFuel with
    | 0 => fuelExhausted env1
    | lemFuel.succ =>
      match g, cval, env1 with
      | Pattern a_1 pat, cval, env1 =>
        match pat, cval with
        | CaseBase (none, snd), x => env1
        | CaseBase (some sym1, snd), x => fmapAddBy Lem_Map.mapKeyCompare sym1 cval env1
        | CaseCtor (Cnil a) [], Vlist a_2 [] => env1
        | CaseCtor Ccons [pat1, pat2], Vlist bTy_elem (cval1 :: cvals) =>
          update_env_aux_lemFuel lemFuel pat1 cval1
            (update_env_aux_lemFuel lemFuel pat2 (Vlist bTy_elem cvals) env1)
        | CaseCtor Ctuple pats', Vtuple cvals =>
          List.foldr
            (fun p acc =>
              match p, acc with
              | (pat', cval'), acc => update_env_aux_lemFuel lemFuel pat' cval' acc)
            env1 (pats'.zip cvals)
        | CaseCtor Cspecified [pat'], Vloaded (LVspecified oval) =>
          update_env_aux_lemFuel lemFuel pat' (Vobject oval) env1
        | CaseCtor Cunspecified [pat'], Vloaded (LVunspecified ty1) =>
          update_env_aux_lemFuel lemFuel pat' (Vctype ty1) env1
        | CaseCtor ctor1 pats, x =>
          have str_ctor :=
            match ctor1 with
            | Cnil a => "nil"
            | Ccons => "cons"
            | Ctuple => "tuple"
            | Carray => "array"
            | Civmax => "ivmax"
            | Civmin => "ivmin"
            | Civsizeof => "ivsizeof"
            | Civalignof => "ivalignof"
            | CivCOMPL => "ivCOMPL"
            | CivAND => "ivAND"
            | CivOR => "ivOR"
            | CivXOR => "ivXOR"
            | Cspecified => "specified"
            | Cunspecified => "unspecified"
            | Cfvfromint => "fvfromint"
            | Civfromfloat => "ivfromfloat"
            | CivNULLcap is_signed =>
              "ivNULLcap(".append (if is_signed = true then "signed" else "unsigned".append ")");
          failwithI
            ("WIP: Core_aux.update_env_aux ==> ctor= ".append
              (str_ctor.append
                (", |pats|= ".append
                  ((Lem_String_extra.stringFromNat pats.length).append
                    (" -- ".append (CerbPP.stringFromCore_value cval))))))
----
